record alone is copied into both forms; the rarity falls back to the tier; status, merge count, chess type (`PRESET`)
and the hidden flags follow our economy and a different value is named; sellPrice only when both are equal; module
talents dropped; a pack unit covered by its pair is not written twice, and a pair of a declared 6★ unit gives two
records; official kits: one operator must own every skill, a chess kit needs the same default (the same tier first),
a 自选 record takes only the 自选 kit, 补位 stand-in kits are not bound, and the official skill ids stay so the kit finds
its alternate skills. The golden results do not move.

### 32.13 The catalogue and the frozen plugin API

The owner's decision for 0.3.0 (the plan's C8, work package I): a community list is a document format and a tool, not a
service. This project does not host plugins and does not run a market. `mod-system.md` §8.2 ("local files only, no
network") is superseded here: an address install is https, and the zip is pinned by SHA-256.

**Catalogue.** `schemas/catalog.schema.json` and `shared/plugins/catalog.js` read `{ "catalog": 1, "name", "plugins":
[{ "id", "name", "version", "class", "description", "url", "sha256", "bytes", "homepage", "license", "authors", "app" }] }`.
`url` is https with a dotted host and no user or password. `sha256` is the zip. Unknown fields are problems, so the
schema and the check agree on one fixture set (`test/plugins/catalog.test.js`), including the same id on two different
entries and an `app` that is not a version range. The schema's `uniqueItemProperties: ["id"]` is not a stock JSON
Schema keyword: `test/helpers/jsonSchema.js` enforces it, and a stock editor that ignores the keyword will not see a
duplicate id. The normative check is that validator plus `readCatalog`. A missing `app` is compatible. An empty or
whitespace-only `app` is invalid, and `entryStatus` reports it incompatible (an unreadable range is not treated as a
match). `docs/plugin-catalog.example.json` points at `example.invalid` and is documentation only.

**Install.** `node tools/plugin.mjs install` and `POST /studio/api/catalog/install` share `server/plugins/install.js`.
A local zip installs as it is. A Windows drive (`C:/…`, `C:\…`) and a UNC path (`\\server\…` or `//server/…`) are files,
not addresses. An https URL requires the hash. The bytes are checked with SHA-256. The download stops at
`PLUGIN_LIMITS.pluginBytes` (200 MB), including a `Content-Length` over the cap and a body that grows past it. One
deadline covers DNS, every redirect and the body (60 s for a zip, 20 s for a catalogue document); it is not reset on
each hop. Redirects use `redirect: 'manual'`. Each hop, including the first, must be https on port 443 with no user
info, and the destination must be public: the literal address after `new URL` (short, octal, hex and decimal IPv4 are
folded first) and, on the connection, every address the name resolves to. Loopback, private, link-local, CGNAT,
multicast, unspecified, documentation and IPv4-mapped IPv6 addresses are refused, as are `.local` and `.localhost`.
The production transport is `https.request` with a lookup that classifies those addresses before it returns any of
them, so the socket is not connected to one it refused. An injected `fetch` with no `lookup` (the tests) still checks
the literal address and the port, and does not resolve. The operator allowlist is `SP_PLUGIN_DOWNLOAD_ALLOW` and
`plugins/config.json` `allowDownloadHosts` (exact host, or `host:port`; IPv6 only as `[addr]` or `[addr]:port`; no
wildcards; default empty). A matching entry permits that host's port and a non-public resolution of that name. A
catalogue document cannot add itself. A missing, unreadable or wrong-typed `allowDownloadHosts` adds nothing, and a
public download still works. `policy.js` ignores the key. The zip is read by `readPluginZip`
(`server/plugins/zip.js`), which `unzipFiles` does not replace: encrypted entries, data descriptors, ZIP64 (a central or
local extra id `0x0001`, a version-needed of 45 or more, a ZIP64 locator, or a `0xffffffff` size), symbolic links, any
other non-file, path traversal, duplicate names, two paths that differ only by the case of a segment, a path that is
both a file and a folder, more than `PLUGIN_LIMITS.files` entries, and a declared uncompressed or compressed size over
the cap are refused before inflate and before `plugins/_incoming/`. The tree is written under
`plugins/_incoming/<random>/<id>/` without following a symbolic link, `checkPluginFolder` must report no problem (a
warning does not stop the install), and the folder is then renamed to `plugins/<id>/`. The same id is not overwritten
unless `force`: the old folder, or the symbolic link if the id is a link, is renamed to `plugins/_replaced/<id>-<UTC
stamp>/` and is not deleted. A failed check deletes only the temporary folder. No file of the package is imported,
spawned or otherwise run. The studio page 「从目录安装」 posts a catalogue URL or the text of a file the browser read.
It does not download the zip. The server does, on this machine and the LAN, with the studio key. A path on the server's
disk is not accepted from the browser. `server/index.js` passes `opts.fetch` into the studio so a test injects the
download and never touches the public network.

**Frozen table.** `docs/PLUGIN-API.md` and `shared/plugins/surface.js` name every member of the closed sets: manifest
fields, permissions, classes, patch operations, compact-record fields, overlay leaves, the DSL vocabulary (passed in;
this module does not import `server/`), postMessage names, slots, studio routes (passed in; stable, and local-only in
the prose rather than as a fourth stability), error codes, catalogue fields, `sp:sdk@1` (experimental: its members
and the module's exports, P3a) and `PLUGIN_API.min` / `current`. `since` is `0.3.0`. `test/plugins/surface.test.js` requires the
document, `buildSurface` and those sets to agree, and `test/plugins/surface.stable.json` fails if a stable name is
removed, renamed or added until that file is updated on purpose. `sdk.SDK_MEMBERS` is an internal row anchored at that
export. `uncoveredSdkExports` fails when `shared/plugins/sdk.js` gains an export the table does not anchor. A new
function in another module that is not a new member of one of those sets is not a new row. The studio route test reads
`api()` and requires the method and path it dispatches to equal `STUDIO_ROUTES` in both directions, so a route literal
removed or a method changed fails even when `STUDIO_ROUTES` is left as it was. At integration the P3a module replaced
the placeholder: the table anchors its exports (`SDK_EXPORTS`: constants, createSDK, kit, math) and its members, and the
code plugins' classes and their `overrides` field are experimental in 0.3.0 (the new stable rows were the seven P3a
error codes, added to the baseline on purpose).

**Errors.** `PLUGIN_CATALOG_*` and `PLUGIN_INSTALL_*` are in `shared/plugins/errors.js`. Shape, URL, hash, id and field
problems are level `problem`. Size, fetch and every install failure are level `studio`. They are not refusal codes.
The symptom tables are regenerated with `node tools/plugin.mjs errors --write`. The anchor is PLUGINS.md §17.

[ASSUMED] — for the owner to confirm or change: catalogue field limits (`CATALOG_LIMITS`: name 80, description 400,
url 500, license 80, one author 80, 16 authors, app 80, 500 entries); a fetched catalogue document at most 1 MB; a zip
download waits 60 seconds and a catalogue document 20 seconds, and that one deadline covers DNS, every redirect and
the body; at most 5 redirects; only port 443 unless the operator allowlist names the host; the allowlist names are
`SP_PLUGIN_DOWNLOAD_ALLOW` (comma-separated) and `plugins/config.json` `allowDownloadHosts` (a string array in the
room-policy file; `policy.js` does not read it); an entry is an exact case-insensitive host, `host:port`, or `[ipv6]`
/ `[ipv6]:port`, with no wildcards; a matching entry permits a non-public resolution and a non-443 port; a catalogue
document cannot authorize a host; a missing or broken allowlist adds no hosts and does not block a public download; a
single-label host such as `localhost` is refused by the catalogue syntax and `example.invalid` is accepted by it; a
leading `//` is a UNC file path, not a protocol-relative URL, and is never fetched; `2001::/32` (Teredo) and the whole
`2002::/16` (6to4) are non-public, as is every IPv4-mapped IPv6 address including one whose embedded IPv4 is public;
the download does not pin a certificate beyond Node's default TLS check; case folding of a zip path is Unicode
`toLowerCase` of each segment; a missing `app` is compatible for every class, including cosmetic, and a present empty
or unreadable `app` is not; "updatable" compares MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH only (a pre-release counts as its release; an
unreadable version compares equal); `bytes`, when the install is told it, must equal the download; the replaced-folder
stamp is UTC `YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-mmm`; a Unix mode of 0 in a zip is a plain file; dotfiles are not installed; per-file
uncompressed and compressed sizes share the 200 MB cap and are checked before inflate; `STAT_NAMES` is part of the
frozen DSL vocabulary; `sp:sdk@1` is named before P3a implements it, and SDK rows stay experimental; a class this
build will not load (`script`, `trusted`) may still be stored when the check passes, and is not executed; an
incompatible catalogue entry may still be installed, and the page only warns. A name that resolves to a public address
and presents a certificate Node accepts is downloaded. The tests do not open a public connection: a private literal is
refused before `fetch`, and a lookup that returns a private address is refused before that address is returned.

Tests: `test/plugins/catalog.test.js`, `test/plugins/install.test.js`, `test/plugins/surface.test.js`,
`test/studio/catalog.test.js`, `test/ui/studio-catalog.e2e.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`), `test/plugins/errors.test.js`,
`node tools/i18n.mjs check --all --strict`, `node tools/golden.mjs` (287 unchanged). Vanilla — no plugin — is unchanged:
the sim, the match, the data tables and the golden files are not touched.

### 32.14.1 Allied fire support for isolated guards (#452)

After placing the formation, the planner relocates an isolated self-range guard to a free legal road tile only when
the exposure score does not decrease. Each incoming ground route must have an allied damage dealer covering both
the guard tile and the preceding blocking contact; different routes may use different allies. An already supported
guard, or one without a qualifying alternative, stays in place. Missing layout-unit indices skip the repair.
The placement preference is [ASSUMED], tracked in [issue #315](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/issues/315).
Normative: DESIGN §6.6; planner reference: META §1.5.

Actual battle regressions cover single- and multi-seat formations and both entrances to a route junction. The
previously unsupported junction entrance leaks all three shield enemies without the repair and clears all three
with it. Match goldens are regenerated because placement changes combat and later economy and bond development;
[PR #452](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/pull/452) records the per-player comparison against upstream.

### 32.14.2 Official scene lighting after the client's shader (#549, PR #571)

GitHub #549 (PR #571 by @andrew05060414). Seen: after 0.2.6 set `SCENE_LIGHT.direct` / `.indirect` to 1 (§31.7) the
official battlefield scenes are still darker than the client and flat — no sand ripples, black shadows. 0.2.5
(gains 0.8 / 0.4) measured about half the client's brightness; 0.2.6 was not measured for this note.

Cause: the client renders in gamma colour space (PlayerSettings `m_ActiveColorSpace` 0, LDR camera; read from the PC
client's `globalgamemanagers`, Unity 2021.3.39f1), so Unity adds the baked light (RGBM pages, `m_LightmapFormat` 2,
read as 5 · A · RGB) and the key light (Mixed / Shadowmask) in display values, while `sceneMaterial` added them in
linear light. The official GLES program of `Torappu/Scene/StandardRealtimeShadow`
(`shaders/standardrealtimeshadow.ab` of the Android version `26-04-13-12-31-42_e9b560`, the variant with the sand
material's keywords) confirms the gamma-space sum and the RGBM read, and has three more terms that were not drawn.

Now:
- `sceneMaterial` follows that program: diffuse = albedo × 0.779 · (1 − metallic), lit by the lightmap and by the key
  light · N·L · the baked shadow; the key light's specular highlight; the reflection of the scene's default reflection
  cubemap by roughness and Fresnel (from 0.221); N bent by the normal map; all in display values, clamped to 1.
  `SCENE_LIGHT`: `range` 3.0, `ambient` 0.5, `direct` 1, `indirect` 1.
- The baked shadow is used whole: `_ShadowStrength` scales only the units' realtime shadow map, which is not drawn.
- `scenes.py decode_rgbm` writes the pages as 5 · A · RGB / 3.0 with scene.json `lightEncoding: 'gamma'`; it adds to
  each scene the decoded reflection atlas `env.png` (scene.json `env`) and to `scene/tex` the materials' normal and
  metallic-gloss maps. The atlas is the cubemap the client binds as `unity_SpecCube0`: the baked texture of the
  ReflectionProbe whose box holds the board, at the probe's intensity (act2autochess_m01: box 40, 0.8; m03: box 50,
  0.6; every renderer there blends probes), else the default reflection of RenderSettings at the scene's reflection
  intensity (the other nine scenes, which share one cubemap; `env.source` says which). The cubemap data is
  face-major (each face with its mip chain) and RGBM (texture usage 5): each face's mip 0, box-downsampled, differs
  from its own mip 1 by 4–9 of 255 and from the other faces' by 24–42; two faces of the shared cubemap (+Y and −Z)
  are byte-identical in the asset. In these scenes Unity −Z is up, and the probe atlases of m01 / m03 have the sky
  on the −Z face and on the −Z side of the four faces around it, as the shader's lookup expects. The 11 scenes have to be extracted again (`node tools/setup.mjs --scenes`) and the full zip
  rebuilt for the exact result.
- A scene extracted earlier still draws: without `lightEncoding` through the `SCENE_LM_V1` read-back, without `env`
  as the plain sum albedo × (lightmap + key light · N·L · shadow).
- The focus falloff outside the lit field rect stays (`uFocusDim` 0.72, owner decision 2026-10-12: the client has
  none).

Measured on act2autochess_m02, prep camera, 0–255, against a client screenshot of another framing (like places, not
the same pixels); "before" is 0.2.5:

| place | 0.2.5 | now | client |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| light stone tiles | 107 | 186 | ≈ 186 |
| planks | 108 | 203 | ≈ 206 |
| sand left of the field (under the focus falloff) | 97 | 164 | ≈ 183 |

**[ASSUMED]** (inferred, not read from the client; marked in `materials.js SCENE_LIGHT` and `scenes.py`):
- `SCENE_LM_V1`: the pages of an earlier extraction hold (L_linear / 8)^(1/2.2) per channel; the shader rebuilds the
  display value approximately (a few percent bright).
- The tangent frame of the normal map comes from screen-space derivatives, t along +u and b along +v (scene.bin has
  no tangents); the bump's sign was not checked against the client surface by surface.
- One reflection cubemap per scene, chosen at the board's anchor: a renderer outside the probe's box (far
  background) would show the default reflection in the client, and the blend at the box's edge is not reproduced.
- The reflection's azimuth against the board follows from uploading the faces in stored order and looking them up
  with the Unity-space direction; checked only by where the sky sits (below), not against a client screenshot.
- The vertex-colour blend is left out on the reading that it is a tile buried in sand
  (`mix(albedo, _BlendColor, smoothstep(colour.r) · _BlendStrength)`), which the client uncovers at run time by a rule
  not found yet; drawn as written it covers every patterned tile of the field.
- `ambient` 0.5: the light of a mesh without a lightmap page.

Still not done: emission maps, the units' realtime shadow, map effects (`mapeff_*`), the post-processing profile, the
water (PR #555; subsequently ported in §32.14.6). No gameplay change; golden results are not moved.

Tests: `test/render/board3d-scene.test.js` (the decode, the material's uniforms and defines for both page encodings).

### 32.14.3 Asset downloads checked by content (#457)

PR #457 by @IceCodeNew. `tools/fetch-assets.mjs` no longer takes a 40x for a download: a confirmed missing source
(HTTP 404 / 410) keeps the old file and its ledger entry, a file without a blob record stays on disk but out of the
rebuilt manifest, and the font and Spine post-processing use verified files only; a failed check keeps the derived WOFF2
files and `fonts.css`. The optional `--source-snapshot=PATH` checks every upstream download against a pinned Git tree
(the blob hash of the raw bytes); it stays outside the release flow (ASSETS.md "Source snapshot verification"). The
PR's test fixes pin the lobby rate-limit clock instead of racing it and build the Windows-bundle case-folding case in a
temporary folder. No gameplay change; golden unchanged.

Tests: `test/assets-source-verification.test.js`, `test/lobby.test.js`, `test/windows-bundle.test.js`.

### 32.14.4 Passenger carriers (#564, idea from PR #592)

GitHub #564 (Hanyx2021): round-11 bounty carriers did not carry or slow. The behaviour is ported from the approach in
PR #592 (Siornya) onto the 0.3.0 enemy kits. That PR is not merged: it was based on 0.2.6, used tabs, and wrote its
history into the already released 0.2.6 chapter.

Seen: `enemy_10159_mntrjn` (伊利昂的木驮兽) was only 无法被阻挡, and `enemy_1302_ymtro_2` (“越长尘”) only required
block ≥4. Both now share one passenger bus (`content/enemies/passengers.js`).

Now, from PRTS 天赋&能力 (伊利昂的木驮兽 and “越长尘”, checked 2026-10-14) and the generated blackboards
(`bus.max_cnt` 5, `bus.move_speed` −0.2, `bus.half_cnt` 2, `Bus.max_cnt` 4):

- Boarding radius is 0.5 (PRTS 载客半径, not a blackboard key). A candidate is a living deployed enemy that is not a
  leader (`isBoss` or `rank === 'BOSS'`), not a machine (`tags` includes `machine`), and not `motion === 'FLY'`.
  No faction, weight or elite restriction. Hidden candidates cannot board. The carrier does not pick up while it is
  hidden, unbalanced (`unbalanceUntil`), stunned, frozen, sleeping or levitated (`canCast` plus those flags; freeze,
  sleep and 浮空 also set `flags.stun`). Silence does not stop pickup. Capacity is the blackboard value.
- A passenger keeps its identity, bounty and HP already lost. It is hidden: omitted from the snapshot, skipped by
  targeting, and not moved or attacked (`updateEnemy` returns, and `onTick` skips its kit). While carried it is
  pinned to the carrier's coordinates, so it does not sit on the tile where it boarded.
- 木驮兽: one replaced `moveMul` of `max(0, 1 + n · bus.move_speed)` (the cuts add; they are not `0.8^n`), then
  `unit.mem.moveSpeedFloor` 0.1 after every other modifier. Base moveSpeed 1 with one to five passengers is
  0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.2, 0.1. “越长尘” does not slow (moveSpeed 0.3, no `bus.move_speed`).
- `bus.half_cnt` 2 is the PRTS model swap (the skin changes at ≥2 passengers and again at the cap). It is not an
  [ASSUMED] guess. The client draws this enemy's default skin (ASSETS.md) and no snapshot field selects a Spine
  skin, so the key is not applied. `enemy_10159_mntrjn_2` (伊利昂的铁驮兽) is out of scope.
- Unload is on removal. Kill and leak both emit `death`. The camel places each passenger in a 0.4-side square
  centred on itself (`rng.range(−0.2, 0.2)` on x, then y — half-open, the battle's existing stream, boarding order)
  and gives it an independent copy of the camel's remaining route. “越长尘” places each passenger on the centre of
  the carrier's tile (`round` of its coordinates) and does not draw. Both skip an in-progress wait leg. Passengers
  then walk. Timeout does not emit `death`: each living unit, hidden passengers included, is recorded once and is
  not unloaded into a second leak.
- Shipped stages and waves have no 云梯. “首次到达云梯时释放” and the permanent loss of pickup after that first
  ladder are not implemented. No substitute tile was added.

**[ASSUMED]:**

- When more candidates than seats are in range, boarding order is stable spawn order (`b.enemies`). PRTS names no
  priority.
- “越长尘” passengers keep their own remaining route. PRTS states the route replacement for the camel and names no
  replacement for this variant.
- Unload skips the in-progress checkpoint wait. “剩余路径” does not say that wait is replayed.
- A passenger who is stunned, frozen, sleeping, levitated, unbalanced or feared still boards when not hidden. The
  control list stops the carrier. PRTS 载客条件 names only 非领袖、非机械、地面.
- A carried passenger takes no new damage while hidden (`damage.js` returns 0), not only that nothing selects it.
  The two enemy pages say the related 叠叠乐 passengers “立刻消失”; they do not spell invulnerability. HP already
  lost is kept.

Normative: DESIGN engine.md "Enemy passengers". Movement floor: SIM.md aggregation and geometry-time.md speed.
Regression: `test/content/enemy_passengers.test.js`. Golden: the only scenarios that spawn either enemy are
`roster-006` and `roster-039` (“越长尘”) and `roster-012`, `roster-033` and `standin-05` (木驮兽). Those five move,
because passengers are hidden and the camel slows. Every other scenario, and the bonds, fields, matches and diy
families, stay unchanged.

### 32.14.5 Bots: medic placement, purchases by hand capacity, rehearsals with the real battle inputs (#577, #315; ideas from PRs #606 / #607 / #608)

The maintainer approved all three ports on 2026-10-14. Sources: [#577](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/issues/577),
[PR #606](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/pull/606), [PR #607](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/pull/607)
and [PR #608](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/pull/608), reviewed against the current bot and §32.14.1.
All selection preferences below are [ASSUMED] bot policies tracked in [#315](https://github.com/sganggs/Stronghold-Protocol/issues/315),
not changes to official combat rules. Normative: DESIGN §6.6; implementation reference: META §1.5 and §2.2.

Blocking medics prefer off-road positions, then supported road positions, with a legal fallback on a crowded board.
The guard repair and medic placement share `supportedContact`: every ground entrance needs allied damage covering
both the occupied tile and its preceding contact; different entrances may have different supporters. Boss medics
with wider healing ranges go first; equal exposure scores prefer allied damage not already covered by a placed
medic. This differs from PR #606's total-DPS tie-break, which could concentrate two medics on the same damage group.

Lineup scores count withdrawn BOARD_AND_DECK members only while actual hand capacity permits keeping them. The
purchase probe reserves the new operator's hand slot before withdrawals; deployment frees slots only afterwards.
Purchase scores within `1e-9` prefer the cheaper offer. Pre-emptive hand shedding requires an owned roster larger
than the deploy cap. The existing arrangement-improvement thresholds stay unchanged.

Rehearsal reuses normal battle inputs, the normal field seed, battle-start hooks, enemy replacements, teammate effects
and battle layer gains. Each candidate clones the current meta RNG. Unlike PR #608, isolation is enforced by the
context boundary: state writers are no-ops, reads/source/event objects are detached, teammate/player contexts are
restricted too, and a separate dispatcher owns script budget and error handling. The candidate board never replaces
the live board; no recompute side effects, preview settlement or live diagnostics occur. Actual combat keeps the
original dispatch and random stream. A preview cannot predict later preparation or other players' random choices.

Regressions cover real medic battles, split boss healing, every incoming contact, capacity-aware purchases, actual
battle/rehearsal ranking, zero-state RNG cloning, and intentional state-writing/erroring official and plugin handlers.
The retained new `coop2-NORMAL-1-unowned-roster` golden reliably exercises stand-ins irrespective of purchases;
previous stand-in scenarios remain outcome regressions. Match goldens are regenerated for changed bot decisions and
subsequent combat/economy (17 of the 20 earlier match scenarios move, every one with its team's rounds passed
unchanged). A fixed-seed comparison of 56 bot matches against the §32.14.1 base: teams 8 better / 42 equal / 6 worse
(+2 rounds), players 19 better / 103 equal / 22 worse (−8 rounds) — a policy trade-off, not tuned to those seeds.
The already integrated lobby and docs-path stability changes are not reapplied.

### 32.14.6 Official scene water (#549, idea from PR #555)

GitHub #549 (PR #555 by @xcdoge). The owner took the water into 0.3.0 on 2026-10-14, ported onto §32.14.2's
materials rather than merged: the PR changed the 0.2.6 materials and would have undone #571's lighting. Sources: the
11 extracted `scene.json` files (their `Torappu/Scene/StylizedWater` records: `colors`, `floats`, `env`) and the PR's
parameter mapping. Normative: client §15 (Official battlefield scenes, Water).

Now:
- The board's water tiles and the scenes' water surfaces share `materials.js waterMaterial`, with the active map's own
  record: deep / shallow colours, normal tiling and strength, two scroll speeds, caustics, sun direction, glossiness,
  colour and sky intensity. A surface is recognised by a shader ending `/StylizedWater`, then a `_water<digits>`
  material name — no material name is hard-coded. Several surfaces keep their own records (act1 m05 / act2 m04 have
  a second, yellow-green one); **[ASSUMED]** the board's tiles take the first. `DynamicWaterTile` is a platform decal,
  not a surface: it keeps its own shader (`sceneWaterDecalMaterial`) and never selects the board's water.
- The colour space follows #571: water colour, reflection, highlight and caustics are summed in display (gamma)
  values, clamped, turned linear once (exact sRGB), dimmed by the shared focus falloff and encoded by
  `<colorspace_fragment>`. PR #555's `pow(rgb, 2.2)` followed by sums in linear light is not used.
- The reflection is the scene's decoded cubemap (#571's `env`: the same lookup with board z negated, its range and
  mip count) when the scene has one; only without it the PR's two-stop sky gradient — never both. The board's
  water follows the active, successfully built scene and returns to its defaults for another map, a removed scene
  or no stage; a preloaded scene of another stage does not recolour it.
- Without an official scene the procedural board keeps its old water (`WATER_DEFAULTS`: the old colours in display
  values, motion, sun and fixed view, exponent 40, alpha 0.9; within 10/255 of the old formula, unit-tested).
  `sceneMaterial`, `SCENE_LIGHT` and `sceneEnvTexture` are unchanged.

**[ASSUMED]** (named in `WATER_TUNING`; not the official formula): sky mix 0.15 with the cubemap / 0.22 without,
highlight gain 0.6 / 1.6, caustics gain 0.2 / 0.35, alpha lift 0.2; the deep / shallow blend `0.08 + 0.2 · N·L`
(the client uses shore distance / depth, which the extraction does not carry); Phong exponent
`max(2, glossiness × 128)`; the PR's highlight and caustic tints; the sky gradient `(0.10, 0.12, 0.15)` →
`(0.30, 0.36, 0.44)`; #571's roughness mip curve; `_SunDir` read as pointing to the sun with z negated; the board's
normal and caustic textures instead of the record's own. Not reproduced: flow distortion, `_SkyCurvatureMask`,
`_CausticCtrl`, foam, a separate sun disc.

Measured (dev demo, prep camera, 1600×900; the client's screenshot attached to #549, another framing): the open sea
of act2 m02 (50, 119, 124) against the client's ≈ (62, 105, 122); #571 alone drew (37, 96, 106). act2 m04's in-field
channel now has the sea's tone; the procedural board's channel is unchanged by eye. Client only; golden unchanged.

Tests: `test/render/board3d-scene.test.js` (recognition, the full mapping, defaults for a missing record or field,
both reflection branches, a non-water material left alone, missing textures, preload / switch / replace / unload and
disposal; #571's assertions unchanged).

### 32.14.7 A manual refresh cancels the shop freeze (#584)

GitHub #584 (and draft PR #601 by @Siornya). Seen: with the shop frozen, a manual refresh rerolled every card but the
freeze stayed on, so the new cards were frozen too until the next round start (`rollShop` copies `shop.frozen` onto the
new slots). The texts only say that a frozen shop can still be refreshed; the owner confirmed on 2026-10-14, from the
official game, that a manual refresh cancels the freeze.

Now: `refresh` turns `shop.frozen` off after the price is paid (or a free refresh is spent) and before the reroll, so
the new cards are not frozen. A refresh that fails (not enough funds, the wrong phase) leaves the freeze on. The round
start still keeps frozen cards and unfreezes. Normative: DESIGN §6 (match.md, the economy line). #601's diff also
carried #592's commit and was not merged.

Tests: `test/match/economy.test.js` (a failed refresh keeps the freeze; a paid and a free refresh cancel it; the new
cards are not frozen). Golden: unchanged — the bots freeze last in their prep (`maybeFreeze`) and never refresh a
frozen shop.

### 32.14.8 Community reports of 2026-10-14 (round 40)

PRTS did not answer (HTTP 403). The official lines below are the local skill table, `data/*.json` and the notes already
quoted in the kits. Five reports.

**瑕光 S2, facing down, the sleeping enemies on her left and right.** Seen: with 慑敌辉光 she could not hit those
sleepers. Official: S2 (skill_table, last level) raises ATK, sleeps every ground enemy on her own tile at once, and
gives allies in the skill range 生命回复速度. `rangeId` `x-1` is that skill range. Her attack range stays `1-1`
(`[[0,0],[0,1]]`, self and the tile in front). The trait 仁慈 lets her attack and prefer a sleeping target. The PRTS
备注 already in the kit also sleeps enemies she is blocking. 沉睡 is 不可阻挡, so the block ends and the sleeper stays
on the side tile it was rounded onto (DESIGN §24.9). That tile is in `x-1` and not in `1-1`, in every facing, not only
down. Cause: `chess_char_3_12-blemsh.js` never added it to her attack range. Now: while S2 runs, each sleeping ground
enemy standing on the rotated skill range is extra attack range (`setExtraRange`), refreshed on start and on tick,
cleared when the skill ends. An awake enemy in the diamond is not. [ASSUMED] the skill text does not change her attack
range, so the extra tiles are only those sleepers; `x-1` also contains the tile behind her, so a sleeper there is
hittable only during S2. Tests: `test/content/r40a-blemsh-s2.test.js` (both chess, four facings, left and right, the
tile behind, a sleeping flyer excluded) and the existing S2 case in `test/content/kits_alt_t3.test.js`. Golden:
`roster-039` only (瑕光 S2, normal and elite, on the same board). She now spends attacks on those sleepers, so one more
呼啸 leaks (killed 48 → 47, leaks 4 → 5, `enemy_1381_winman` 21 → 20 killed); healing rises (7573 → 7757) and the bond
layer gains fall (`miraShip` 3 → 1, `raidShip` 31 → 29). The other families match.

**夕, 画卷, 变形同构体.** Seen: a copy of an operator wearing 变形同构体 had neither the item nor the extra bond, and a
copy of 缪尔赛思 did not hand one out. Official: 画卷 copies the operator, elite state included, and the equipment; the
copied items arrive unequipped (PRTS 画卷 备注, DESIGN §27.27). The extra bond counts only while 变形同构体 and an item
with `giveBondId` are both worn (`pieceBonds`). 缪尔赛思's 获得时 garrison grants 变形同构体 (1, elite 2). Measured on
`g.art`: the morph is copied into the hand, the new operator wears nothing, and the bond stays off until that operator
wears the pair. Copying 缪尔赛思 runs 获得时 again (+1, elite +2), also unequipped. 变形同构体 does not merge
(`upgradeNum` 100). A copied hammer does, and that merge takes the hammer off the original, which splits the pair.
Not a bug. The tier-VI stock cap is 3: the scroll's `grantItem` still requires a free copy, and only a fixed operator
gift may overdraw. A fourth morph is refused. Tests: `test/match/r40a-scroll-morph.test.js`.

**三投资, 耶拉, 缪尔赛思 下蛋.** Seen: three investors did not multiply 获得时 effects; 耶拉 fired once; 缪尔赛思's
eggs looked wrong. 下蛋 is her 获得时 变形同构体 (`garrison_76`), not the in-battle 流形. Official bond text: while
active, 获得时 traits run twice, and three times from 100 layers. 标准 lists `investShip` inactive (the 0.1.3 note).
The count is distinct base ids on the board and in the hand, so copies of one investor do not light it. Measured with
`g.buy` after three different investors: 耶拉 normal 1 / lit 2 / 100 layers 3, elite 2 / 4 / 6 in normal-equivalents;
缪尔赛思 the same 1 / 2 / 3 and elite 2 / 4 / 6 (the #590 figures). 标准 stays at 1 (elite 2, her own count, no
multiplier) even at three members and 100 layers. 谢拉格不融冰 merges at two, so a lit normal 耶拉's ×2 is one golden
card. The dispatcher was already doing this. Not a bug. Tests: `test/match/r40a-invest-ongain.test.js`.

**望, infinite 棋子, other operators' redeploy cost stuck.** Seen: she seemed to make endless stones and lock everyone
else's redeploy DP. Official, as already implemented and tested: a placed 棋子 is spent, returns to her stock, and
comes back on its tile for its cost (TRP-X 2 DP) after its redeploy time, and not while a ground enemy stands there.
Measured: seven stones stay seven units through a recycle; DP stays finite and is spent on each return (no NaN); the
other operator's timer finishes and she waits because the stones take the DP first; once every stone is back, DP
climbs at +1/s and she redeploys. The 2026-10-06 choice that a placed stone consumes her stock is unchanged. Not a bug.
Tests: `test/content/r40a-wang-dp.test.js`.

**娜仁图亚 见者有份.** Seen: the two free equips on an even round were never above the current shop level. Official
blackboard (`preparation_start_add_special_goods_every_n_round`): round 2, refresh 2, choose 1, pool
`pool_equip_narant`, and no tier. Cause: the pool was `maxTier: 'shopLevel'`, so `rollItemId` drew only tiers 1..level.
The ordinary shop is not a level+1 table either (it steps up only when the chosen tier and everything below it are
empty). Now: the same shape as 凯瑟琳 — every shop-eligible equip, uniform. [ASSUMED] the weights; the blackboard names
none, and the report is that a higher tier appears. `pool_equip_normal` stays capped. Tests:
`test/match/r40a-narant-pool.test.js`, `test/content/bands.test.js`.

### 32.14.9 Attack commitment, the catapult and 野鬃's push (round 40)

Seen: round 40. The bounty 自制投石机's triple hit was one damage instance, so 三坚守 (thorns on a 0.2 s cooldown) fired
once per volley. 野鬃 with her skill on still pushed an enemy who had walked behind her — about to leave the tile —
back toward the leak. On round 12 of an ultimate simulation the last enemy, a red heavy, sat on 魔王 beside the blue
gate healing the 257 she dealt each hit; 灵知 stood one row above and about one column to the right, facing right,
and never attacked it. Separately, an operator whose target is leaving range on the tick the attack comes off
cooldown dropped the attack, and an enemy whose target left reach during the wind-up cancelled the swing.

Official: PRTS 自制投石机 — 「普通攻击为3连击，向目标发射石块对目标半径1.5范围内的所有我方单位造成物理普通伤害（此弹道不会强制击中主目标，碰撞无视迷彩），索敌不受阻挡影响，且不会因丢失目标而结束攻击」; while 入戏(敌方) is held, the stone aims at a random point of the side-6.0 square and the radius becomes 0.5. Blackboard `Attack.attack@times` 3, `hit_interval` 0.3, `projectile_range` 0.5, `offset_bound` 3. PRTS 野鬃 S2 「攻击会把目标往攻击方向中等力度地推开」; the client template is `knockback[dir]` (`_useSourceDirection`, `_decreaseForceLevelWhenNotInDirection` 2). PRTS 推与拉's 特殊修正 does turn a target more than 45° off that direction, or nearer than 0.25 tile, into a weak radial push — which for a target behind her is back toward the leak. The maintainer's first-hand round-40 report is the rule for her skill: behind, and about to leave the tile, does not reverse. PRTS 作战机制: a shot that has started keeps its target; §AOE伤害判定, a locked attack still hits the units chosen when the wind-up began. PRTS 作战机制 §碰撞体积与位置识别: 「索敌抬手等均使用碰撞箱」 (target search and wind-up use the colliders); 格子判定 — the centre rounded half-to-even (四舍六入五成双) — is the rule of pits, sleep zones, a trapper's mines and marks. A head-on block stops at contact (~0.71 tile), outside the blocker's tile.

Cause: `enemy_10162_mnctpt` dealt one attack. `BattleDisplacement.push` rewrote every off-axis directional push into a radial one, and 野鬃 S2 used that path, so a negative dot product became a push opposite her facing (`feedback1d-push` locked that). Operators have no wind-up (`docs/history/0.2.5.md` §30.7); `updateAlly` searched only after enemies had moved, so a target who crossed out of the range grid on that tick was never fired at. `enemyAttack` rebuilt its target list on the damage frame and returned when the list was empty, which cancelled the swing.

Now: the catapult's kit owns the volley (`deferHit`). Three physical attacks (`isAttack`, tag `catapult:stone`) land `hit_interval` apart. The normal stone splashes at 1.5 and still hits the locked ally when they can be selected; a later stone whose target is gone splashes at the last aim. Scheduled stones have no timer owner, so they land after the catapult dies. 入戏 is `flags.inPlay` or a buff key `inPlay`: each stone aims at `battle.rng()` inside the ±`offset_bound` square and splashes `projectile_range` (0.5), with no forced hit on the main target. No bond, effect, garrison or choice in this mode sets that flag. 野鬃 S2 passes `keepDir`: a target behind her (dot < 0) stays on her attack direction at 受力等级 −2 (0.12 tile). Beside her, and nearer than 0.25 tile, the push stays the official radial −2. `push()` without `keepDir` is unchanged (推击手, 见行者, 琳琅诗怀雅). `noteAttackCommit` runs before the enemy phase and stores the targets of every ally whose cooldown reaches 0 this tick; `updateAlly` uses that list only when the post-move search is empty. `rangeAoe`, `allInRange` and heals still select at the strike. An enemy swing stores `swingTargets` when it starts. Leaving range does not clear them. Stun, freeze, sleep, 浮空, hiding, 缴械, 恐惧 and blocked 战栗 still do. Melee settles on the locked ally; a ranged shot is the projectile that chases. A dead or unselectable target is skipped and the cooldown still elapses.

灵知 (`chess_char_4_13`, range `y-6`, facing right) and the enemy 魔王 held: the video (round 12, at about 8 s) shows
the red heavy blocked on 魔王's gate side, about 0.7 tile from her, and 灵知 (black hair with a red streak, glasses) on
the row above, one column to the right of 魔王. In tiles: 魔王 at (9, 4) facing right, the enemy's centre at x ≈ 3.3 —
tile (9, 3) —, 灵知 at (10, 5): offset [−1, −2], which `y-6` does not contain, so our 灵知 does not attack it, as the
video shows. With 灵知 directly above 魔王 (10, 4) the offset is [−1, −1] and he attacks (four hits in six seconds);
an enemy held on 魔王's front, x ≈ 4.7, is [−1, 0] from (10, 5) and is hit too. The +257 / −257 pair is the enemy
healing what it takes; it is not a targeting matter.

Checked against the official rules found this round: PRTS says target search uses the colliders, and the client's
enemy prefabs (local CN client, read 2026-10-14) give every enemy of this mode a `CircleCollider2D` of radius 0.1
(237 prefabs; the 6 others share them). Under that rule too, a centre 0.21 short of 魔王's tile is not reached by a
0.1 circle, so neither rule puts this enemy in 灵知's range. The owner's official screenshots of the same day show the
case from the other side — a ground operator on the last tile of a ranged operator's range, and the enemy it blocks,
outside that tile, is not hit — and the owner kept this behaviour (round 41). The collider itself went into 0.3.0 in
round 41 (§32.14.20).

```
魔王 at (9,4) facing RIGHT. The video: the enemy held on her gate side, centre x≈3.3, tile (9,3).
y-6 facing RIGHT (灵知). # = covered, . = not, origin on 灵知:
        dc -2 -1  0  1  2
dr -2        .  #  #  .
dr -1     .  #  #  #  #
dr  0     .  #  #  #  #
dr  1     .  #  #  #  #
dr  2        .  #  #  .
灵知 at (10,5), one column right of 魔王 (the video): offset [-1, -2], not covered.
灵知 at (10,4), above 魔王: offset [-1, -1], covered. Enemy held in front (tile (9,5)) from (10,5): [-1, 0], covered.
```

[ASSUMED]: the blackboard `hit_interval` is the gap between the damage instances, not a launch gap added on top of projectile flight. Stone 0 lands on the strike frame. The 入戏 roll is `battle.rng()` in [−bound, bound] on each axis. Nothing in the autochess tables grants 入戏(敌方); the branch is gated on the unused `inPlay` flag.

Tests: `test/sim/round40-catapult.test.js`, `test/sim/round40-gnosis-block.test.js`, `test/sim/round40-attack-commit.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1d-push.test.js` (behind / about to leave the tile stays along her attack at the −2 distance; beside and < 0.25 stay radial), `test/content/community-round30.test.js`, `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` (the catapult's three stones), `test/sim/feedback5-catapult-smog.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback5-enemy-swing.test.js`, `test/plugins/reviewLimits.test.js`. Golden regenerated, all six families, and `node tools/golden.mjs --twice` matched (177139 + 123843 ms): roster 33/49, bonds 11/46, fields 12/22, matches 18/21, standins 9/10, diy 88/140. Committed strikes and finished enemy swings move kills, clear-versus-timeout (standin-06 cleared to timeout; diy-005 and diy-135 timeout to cleared; diy-091, diy-103 and diy-130 cleared to timeout), boss HP left, and match LP / boss-pool left. The catapult's three stones change `enemy_10162_mnctpt` damage dealt in roster-013 and roster-040. 野鬃 `keepDir` moves the scenarios that field her (roster-002, roster-021, roster-022; bond-swiftShip-high fields chess_char_1_19_b). Unchanged matches: solo-FUNNY-1, coop4-FUNNY-6, coop2-NORMAL-70-diy. Normative: DESIGN §5.5 (engine.md, operator attacks, enemy AI, the push helper) and §30.3 (the catapult sentence); SIM.md `push`.

### 32.14.10 The official 射击台 model (round 40)

Seen (community, relayed by the owner on 2026-10-14): 战场#3's raised tiles by the blue gate render wrongly. They are
the 射击台 (`trap_1106_achplat`) at [10,3] / [10,4] of act1autochess_m03 (and the other six of that map). 0.2.6 drew
them as our own slab (GitHub #569): a low (0.26) grey plate with the board atlas's metal sides, standing in front of the
scene's rocks.

Official: the client draws the device with its own model, `trap_1106_achplat` in the 卫戍协议 device bundle
`arts/maps/common/trap/trap_110.ab` (local CN client, read 2026-10-14; the same bundle holds the red door, the plane,
the laser turret and the crate): a 1.05 × 1.05 metal platform 0.465 high at its prefab scale 0.01, a 2 × 2 tread-plate
top in a gold frame over a dark base with yellow dashes, textured by `TX_trap_1106_achplat` with the material
`MT_trap_1106_achplat` (`Torappu/Scene/Trap/Shadow`, `_Ambient` 0.895, `_Diffuse` 0.197). PRTS (射击台, Ranged Tile):
the device rewrites its tile to high ground.

Now: `extract.py` exports that model, texture (with a WebP copy) and material to `mesh/trap_110` (only the 射击台 of
the bundle); `load.js` loads them with the board pack; `scene.js` draws one model per active 射击台 on the procedural
board and on an official scene (`layout.js buildBoard` gets `platformMesh` and leaves those slabs out), lit by
`materials.js trapMaterial` with the scene's own key light when a scene is drawn. Units stand on the model's top:
`style.js TILE_H.platform` 0.465 (was 0.26) for the 3D slab fallback and the 2D board's box too; mounds keep 0.26
(`TILE_H.mound`). Without the local art (or an extraction from before 0.3.0) the slab stays, now 0.465 high. Normative:
DESIGN §15 (client.md, official battlefield scenes).

[ASSUMED]: the lighting formula of `trapMaterial` (inferred from the material's two parameters; the shader program was
not read) and no realtime shadow on the model; the model's top as the standing height (the client places units by the
tile's height type).

Tests: `test/render/board3d.test.js` (with `platformMesh` every map's 射击台 lose their slab on both boards, water
platforms and sealed floors keep theirs, `TILE_H.platform` 0.465), `test/render/board3d-scene.test.js` (one model per
射击台 on the procedural board and on a scene, its top at the standing height, the material's parameters and the
scene's light; without the model the slab), `test/render/board3d-extract.test.js` (the job keeps only the 射击台 of the
bundle). Golden: unchanged (render only).

### 32.14.11 Every official dub: English, Korean and the operators' own-language dubs (PR #455)

Seen: the maintainer asked to fold English, Korean and the remaining official dubs (regional / own-language) into
the 0.3.0 update, together with the existing per-operator voice switch if that was possible. PR #455 (@YuriRestia,
against a pre-0.2.2 master) had already planned those trees and a second per-operator picker.

Official: ArknightsAssets2 ships the same `cn_nnn.mp3` lines under `voice_en/`, `voice_kr/` and
`voice_custom/<wordkey lowercase>/`. charword `voiceLangDict` marks an own-language dub where
`voiceLangTypeDict[type].groupType === 'CUSTOM'` (Italian, German, Russian, French, Spanish, Chinese dialect).
LINKAGE (collab) voices are not that. Some dubs keep the base word key as the folder (`char_196_sunbr`).

Now: `tools/assets/plan.mjs` plans `audio.voiceEn`, `audio.voiceKr`, `audio.voiceNative` and the literal
`audio.voiceNativeLangType` when `optionalVoice` is on (fetch-assets does that unless `--no-optional-voice`;
`buildPlan` itself still defaults the flag off, so a plan of the two 0.2.2 trees is unchanged). `tidyVoiceTrees`
drops an empty extra tree and a type whose native file did not land. `FULL_ZIP_OPTIONAL_VOICE` is **true**: the
0.3.0 full zip ships the dubs (5712 files, 169 MB, 157 MB zipped: the full zip is 730 MB, 975 MB unpacked). `false`
holds the files back the way `FULL_ZIP_JP_VOICE` does, and an update zip does not delete a held file. The client
extends the 0.2.3 per-operator choice (`settings.voiceOverrides`, the roster and DIY dropdown). It does **not**
merge #455's second picker (`ui/voiceLang.js`, `voicePicks`, `sp.pref.voiceLang`). Global `voiceLang` accepts
en/kr. A per-operator value may also be `native`. Playback order, when the manifest is known: the operator's own
choice if this server has that tree, else the own-language dub when 本土语言 is on and the operator has one, else
the global dub if that tree is present, else Chinese. A missing line still falls back to the Chinese file of the
same name. Changing the global language does not clear per-operator choices. The dropdown lists only dubs present
on this server, names the own-language option from the official type, offers 试听 (outside battle: no VoiceGate
cooldown, the voice channel is taken over), and still shows a saved choice whose tree is absent, marked
unavailable. On the merge the three trees joined the cosmetic plugins' tables (`shared/plugins/visual.js`
ASSET_CONTAINERS: `audio.voiceEn` / `voiceKr` / `voiceNative`, PLUGIN-API / PLUGINS) and the Forge importer writes
`voiceLangs.ja` / `en` / `ko` to `audio.voiceJp` / `voiceEn` / `voiceKr` (until then only `cn` was imported).

[ASSUMED]: the first CUSTOM entry in `voiceLangDict` object order is the dub when an operator has several (the
pool has one each). A per-operator choice of a tree this server lacks is kept and shown, and playback falls
through the order above rather than clearing it. 试听 of 跟随全局, or of an unavailable saved choice, uses that
same order; 试听 of an available explicit language plays that language. `voiceNativeLangType` is written only for
operators this game fields who also have a line in the primary `voice` tree (the Chinese tree on a normal run).

Tests: `test/assets.test.js` (native dirs, tidy, the three trees and the type table on a small fake charword),
`test/feedback7-voices.test.js` (the package switch on and off), `test/ui/audio.test.js` (each tree and the
fallback, 试听), `test/ui/voice-prefs-023.test.js` and `test/ui/gameLogic.test.js` (the order and sanitising),
`test/ui/operator-voice.test.js` (only the languages present, the official native name). Golden: unchanged
(listening preference and asset plan only).

### 32.14.12 突袭's landing window measured to the body (PR #614)

PR #614 by @Misyra. Seen (community, relayed by the owner on 2026-10-14, with a screenshot; the owner saw it too): in a
Hidden Core round against 假想敌：管 whose position was shifted one column, the three 突袭 members of the bottom row
(one of them a 突袭 through 变形同构体 and 突袭手雷) stayed home although the opponent's half had empty tiles; in round 14,
the same 管 without the shift, they jumped to the tile in the opponent's top-left corner that overlaps the leader.

Cause: `raidTile` searched landing tiles within `RAID_SEARCH` (2, Chebyshev) of the tile the enemy's position rounds
to. Both 管 occupy the same 15 tiles (rows 3–5 × cols 8–12), but the Final Assault's `enemy_9021_acduml` has `hitArea`
`dx` 0 from the route anchor (3,10) while the Hidden Core's `enemy_9021_acduml_2` has `dx` 1 from (3,9) (official:
up 1, right 1). The window moved with the anchor to cols 7–11 and cut off col 12, the opponent's-half column on the
body. On the official maps of 下半 cols 9–11 are `f` / `O` / `f` (no deployment), so with col 8 taken the members could
only reach col 7's fence (with a range one tile forward) or nothing (a range of their own tile only) — the report's
"they do not jump" (checked with the integration line's code in the review).

Now: a landing tile counts while its Chebyshev gap to the body's tiles is at most `RAID_SEARCH`; the landing
preference, the block preference and their tie-breaks stay relative to the position's tile, so col 12 (on the body,
block preference 0) wins over col 7's fence, and col 8 still wins while it is free. The official `hitArea` offsets are
unchanged. Normative: DESIGN §5 (engine.md, 突袭), SIM §5.5. The PR's history lines and golden numbers were written on
0.2.6; the golden is regenerated on the integration line.

Tests: `test/sim/raid-body-window.test.js` (PR: both leaders land at (3,12) with cols 8–11 taken).

### 32.14.13 枯朽之种's dive is a projectile (PR #612, GitHub #611)

PR #612 by @YinqiMargatroid, issue #611 by the same reporter. Seen: 逻各斯's S3 did not slow 枯朽之种
(`enemy_1269_nhfly`) — the seed walked onto its target at its own movement speed, a unit rather than a bullet.

Official (PRTS 枯朽之种): attack radius 2.0, ATK 750, movement 0.6, flying, its attack projectile flies at 10;
handbook: 「接近干员时，自毁并对其造成物理伤害」. 逻各斯 S3 (`skchr_logos_3`, every level): `projectile_move_scale`
0.05 for enemy projectiles in his attack range, cleared when the skill ends — the slow the integration line already
applies to enemy projectiles.

Now: with an operator within its data range the seed locks the nearest one and launches as an enemy projectile (speed
10, `source` the seed, `hitDead`); its model follows the projectile, and during the flight it cannot be targeted,
blocked or hurt and does not move by itself; the hit deals the ATK cached at launch and removes the seed; 逻各斯 S3
slows and clears it as an enemy bullet (a cleared shot removes the seed). 失衡 before the launch still holds it back.
[ASSUMED]: a target that leaves before the hit lets the seed burst at the shot's last point without damage (the
engine's `hitDead` projectiles land where the target was). Not done: the talent's clause 「普通攻击结束时，若自身没有
晕眩、冻结、浮空、沉睡，则强制自毁」 as such (the seed is invulnerable in flight, so those states cannot land then), and the
launch tick flies at full speed before 逻各斯's next tick slows it (about a third of a tile). Normative: DESIGN §5
(engine.md), SIM. The PR's history section (in the released 0.2.6 chapter) and its golden numbers (written on 0.2.6)
were not taken; the golden is regenerated on the integration line.

Tests: `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` (the launch, the untargetable flight, the hit), `test/content/op_logos.test.js`
(the slow and the clear), `test/sim/unbalance.test.js` (a pre-launch 失衡 holds the launch).

### 32.14.14 假想敌：镜膜 loses its HP bonus for good on the first silence (PR #613, GitHub #481)

PR #613 by @Siornya, issue #481. Seen: after a silence ended, 假想敌：镜膜 (`enemy_9011_acrefr`) got its doubled max HP
back together with its RES, and its model kept the spinning fan.

Official (PRTS 假想敌：镜膜, fetched 2026-10-14): ability 「生命值大幅提升，被沉默后永久失效」; talent 「最大生命值+100%
（受沉默影响后失效，无法恢复）」; blackboard `Refracting.magic_resistance` 70 and `Refracting.max_hp` 1 (the same in
data/enemies.json; no silence resistance). The RES is the silenceable half of 折射 and returns when the silence ends;
the max HP bonus is lost for good.

Now: `refraction` latches `hpLost` on the first silence and sets the form 'stopped' (the manifest's B_Idle / B_Move /
B_Attack / B_Die: the fan stands still; render/units.js FORMS); later 折射 buffs carry the RES only, the current HP keeps
its ratio. The latch is per unit. Normative: DESIGN §5 (engine.md), SIM §1 (forms). On the merge: the PR's history
section (in the released 0.2.6 chapter) was not taken, its SIM sentence moved to the forms paragraph, its tabs became
spaces.

Tests: `test/content/enemies_bosses.test.js` (the latch, RES back after the silence, no HP bonus), `test/render/forms.test.js`
(the 'stopped' clips, also for a view built late). Golden: no scenario silences a 镜膜 and lets the silence end
(regenerated with the round's combat changes).

### 32.14.15 The public-state interval counts from the broadcast (PR #615)

PR #615 by @Siornya (a draft; its checklist was empty, the test was run on the merge). Seen (in the #509 / #592
discussions): `_maybeSendPublic` stamped `_lastPubAt` with the time it was entered, so the time spent building and
serializing the public view was eaten out of the 100 ms interval and the next frame could leave earlier than 100 ms
after the previous broadcast.

Now: the stamp is taken with `sched.now()` right before the broadcast. With the virtual scheduler of a test or a
replay `now()` does not move within a call, so nothing changes there; content that did not change still returns
early without a stamp. Not changed: a forced send skips the interval as before, and a pending timer is not cleared by
it. On the merge its tabs became spaces.

Tests: `test/match/connection.test.js` (a 16 ms build delays the next frame to 100 ms after the real broadcast).
Golden: unchanged (match messaging only).

### 32.14.16 The statistics page after the official one (PR #509)

PR #509 by @Siornya, after the owner's request of 2026-10-11 to bring the page closer to the [official statistics
page](https://ak-webview.hypergryph.com/autoChess/act1autochess); the owner asked again on 2026-10-14 whether it had
been merged. Merged from its head (547d259c, the contributor's commits kept).

What it adds: the overview's `Dr. <nickname>` and trophy total (the settled co-op awards of the kept records, defeats
included, with the official medal of `config.trophies.medals`: 0–9, 10–29, 30–59, 60–99, 100+), solo / co-op rows
(games, successful defences, Hidden Core clears), the six titles as a hexagon (a title lights at 20 awards, as on the
official page), 卫戍成就 (the record with the highest settled bond layers, its lineup), single-match records (most
funds spent, promotions, enemies defeated), the three most used bonds and strategies (盟约&策略, with the strategy
badge after `bands.victorCount` clears), the bond's members, and the accumulated time moved to 战斗累计. The page's
artwork and two fonts come from the official page's own files on the official web CDN (web.hycdn.cn: the images
embedded in its stylesheet and a few image / font files), downloaded by the asset setup (or
`node tools/fetch-stats-assets.mjs`) into `public/assets/ui/statistics/`, never kept in git.

Actual kills: the battle result's `perPlayer[].creditedKills` sums the kills of the player's operators and summons
before `unitStats` is capped (160 rows), whoever's wave the enemy belonged to; the match adds it per phase (normal,
联防, Final Assault, Hidden Core) into `m.result` `players[].creditedKills`. It changes no outcome.

On the merge: the PR's history sections (in the released 0.2.3 / 0.2.4 chapters) were not taken; `creditedKills` is
left out of `resultDigest` (statistics only: in the hash it would change every match's verification digest and split a
client's and the server's digests); its storage fields ride on 0.3.0's one storage step (v2, §32.6) instead of two more
versions (the PR's steps rewrote nothing); records whose room mode is unknown (`roomMode` null) count in the total but in
neither mode row, and a line under the rows now gives their number (the owner's second point of 2026-10-11); the
plugin switch of §32.6 stays at the top of the overview; tabs became spaces and the duplicated Korean entries were
dropped. [ASSUMED] (the PR's): a bond's use is its being active in the player's own settlement, once per match; the
highest-layer record approximates the official peak.

Tests: the PR's (`test/ui/stats.test.js`, `test/ui/{bond-achievement,bond-members,match-records,strategy-badges}-stats.test.js`,
`test/match/match-kills.test.js`, `test/sim/kill-credit.test.js` — now pinning that the digest ignores the credit,
`test/stats-template-assets.test.js`, `test/ui/stats.e2e.test.js`). Golden: unchanged expected (no outcome reads the
credit).

### 32.14.17 The combat meter, an opt-in setting (PR #618)

PR #618 by @robll-v1, ported from the public fork [xinhai-ai/Stronghold-Protocol](https://github.com/xinhai-ai/Stronghold-Protocol)
(author @临夏俨然). Merged from its head (ea4fe48c, the contributor's commit kept) onto 0.3.0, then made opt-in.

Seen: the PR adds a modeless 作战统计 panel on the match's bottom-left. A row is one of the player's operators: effective
HP damage and the HP a heal actually restored, DPS / HPS over the local battle seconds, and a bar split by damage type.
Same-name operators merge per player and a summon counts on its owner. The numbers stay in the browser. The PR attached
the meter to every battle and showed the button whenever the player was not a spectator. The PR did not edit history or
CHANGELOG, so nothing from it was written into an already released chapter.

Now: 设置 →「作战统计」 (`combatMetrics`) defaults off, like 弹药数量. Off, the button is not rendered and `trackCombat`
is not called. On, the PR's panel is what an in-page battle shows. A gameplay-plugin room simulates in a Worker
(`WorkerSimHost`): the object on the page is wire state (time, tick, ids), with no `damaged` hook and no `heal`, so the
meter cannot run there and the button stays hidden. A cosmetic plugin keeps the in-page host. A `hud.right` ui-slot is a
separate node and is not gated by this setting. Turning the setting off mid-battle detaches the hook and restores
`battle.heal`. The meter is not sent and does not enter `b.progress` or `b.result`.

[ASSUMED]: turning the setting on while a battle is already prepared does not backfill that battle. The hooks would have
missed damage already dealt, so the next battle prepared after the switch is the first one counted.

On the merge: both i18n tails were kept. The key `伤害` was already translated, and Korean stays `피해` (the PR's `대미지`
was not written over it). `runner.js` keeps the content-set host, the Worker simulation and the attack-commitment path.

Tests: `test/match/combat-metrics.test.js`, `test/match/runner.test.js` (the PR's assertions, plus default-off, the
settings switch, and a Worker host that never attaches), `test/ui/gameLogic.test.js`. Golden: unchanged.

### 32.14.18 The host browser rehearses the bots' layouts, an opt-in mode (PR #622)

PR #622 by @54shitaimzf, merged from its head (`bc368493`, the contributor's commits kept) onto 0.3.0 as an opt-in.
The 0.2.6 branch built candidates by laying them on the live board and simulating a `rehearse:` seed with layer
gains off. That construction is not used. Candidates stay the §32.14.5 preview: detached board, normal field seed,
start effects, layer gains, isolated meta RNG and script budget, no settlement and no live error log.

Seen: with the flag off, a bot prep still rehearses on the server and the match golden is unchanged. Now: setting
`SP_BOT_REHEARSAL=host` (anything else, including unset and `server`, stays off) offers an AI teammate's candidates
to the current human host. The browser runs them in a same-origin module Worker and answers with the winning index.
The server applies that layout once and does not simulate it again. A timeout (3 s), a missing or replaced host, a
disconnect, an invalid index or `error: true` makes the server run the same inputs. Prep ending cancels the task;
the deadline does not move. A human seat under AI 托管 stays on the server. A gameplay-plugin room (`requireCs`)
never delegates, including when the match host inherits the env var: the browser has only the official data and
must not run that room's scripts. A spec that names a content hash is answered `error` and is not simulated in the
page worker.

[ASSUMED] The contributor's measured server-CPU saving (about a third across five matches) is their 0.2.6 figure,
not re-measured here. The interface — task, index answer, timeout, server fallback — is where a later local or
external compute can plug in. This version makes no external network call. No new combat rule.

Tests: `test/sim/rehearsal.test.js`, `test/match/rehearsal-delegate.test.js` (the host's pick matches the server's
pick of the same candidates; a plugin room does not delegate; a forged, out-of-range or late answer is rejected),
`test/match/rehearsal-client.test.js` (a content-set spec never starts the worker). The browser worker file
`test/sim/rehearsal-worker.browser.test.js` is opt-in (`SIM_E2E=1`) and was not run in this sandbox. Golden:
`node tools/golden.mjs` with the flag off.

### 32.14.19 Reports of the evening of 2026-10-14 (round 41)

**The shop's equipment card under a promotion reward (GitHub #617).** Seen: with an equipment card in the shop, a
promotion's 晋升奖励 row showed its three operators and, next to them, the shop's equipment card, which could still be
bought. Expected (the report, and the official row): the reward offer is the whole row until it is picked or put off.
Cause: `ShopBar` swapped only the operator cards for the offer; the equipment column (`shopbar__item`) was drawn and
armable either way. Now: while an offer is shown (晋升奖励 or a special refresh such as 凯瑟琳's 定向投放), the
equipment column is not drawn and only the offer's cards arm; 稍后 brings the whole shop back. Tests:
`test/ui/feedback1-offers.test.js` (#617 case), the browser suites `test/ui/feedback1-offers.e2e.test.js`,
`test/ui/playtest6-ui.e2e.test.js` and `test/ui/feedback1-gaps.e2e.test.js`.

**贾维's gift on the round's first refresh swallowed the 拉普兰德 it merged (GitHub #627).** Seen: two 拉普兰德 stood on
the board, unrefreshed this round; the round's first refresh was also the match's 6th, so 贾维's 【团伙行动】 granted a
third; the three merged into the elite and 叙拉古 gained nothing, then +8 on the next refresh. Cause: the refresh
dispatch runs the strategy (band) step before the garrisons, and the snapshot of the chess that saw the refresh skipped
any copy no longer owned — the two the merge had consumed. Now: a copy consumed by a merge during that refresh's
dispatch (`mergedInto`, set by PlayerState._mergeChess) still runs its 刷新时 特质 where it stood, and its per-round
counter reads that copy (`ctx.pieceCounter` / `incPieceCounter`); the elite stays a new 拉普兰德 that fires on its own
first refresh (GitHub #169, the owner's decision of 2026-10-06). The round: +8 on the first refresh, +8 on the next.
Tests: `test/match/feedback1-meta.test.js` (#627, hand and board; the PR #196 cases unchanged).

### 32.14.20 Operator ranges use the enemies' 0.1 colliders (round 41)

Seen: after the 灵知 analysis (§32.14.9) the owner sent two official screenshots of 2026-10-14 — a ground operator on
the last tile of a ranged operator's range; the enemy it blocks stands outside that tile and the ranged operator does
not hit it — and asked whether a 0.1 collider would let ours hit it wrongly. The owner then chose the collider for
0.3.0.

Official: PRTS 作战机制 §碰撞体积与位置识别 「索敌抬手等均使用碰撞箱」; the client's enemy prefabs give every enemy of this
mode a `CircleCollider2D` of radius 0.1 (237 prefabs). PRTS names the exceptions in its notes: 格子判定 — the tile of
the position — for 汹涌怒火's 高台 splash (x-5), 真言's 【麻痹付与】 and 谬因's search and attack ("索敌与攻击始终采用
格子判定"); 中心判定 for 珊比's conveyor; 中点判定 for blocking and splash radii. It marks five radius effects 碰撞判定
(机械师 S1's splash, 结构性原理's stop, 提丰 S3's mark, 蕾缪安's shells, 克莱门莎 S2's splash), and 真言's reveal,
机械师 S3's x-6 and 煌's 3-6 burst. Blocking stays the centre-to-centre contact (0.7071): a head-on block holds the enemy 0.71 tile from
its blocker, so its circle ends 0.61 from the blocker's centre, 0.11 outside the blocker's tile — the screenshots'
case stays out of range.

Cause: since 0.1.0 every grid range took the tile of the enemy's position (body.js `bodyInKeys`): a collider of
radius 0.

Now: `colliderKeys` (body.js) lists the tiles an enemy's 0.1 circle overlaps (touching is not overlapping; a
diagonal tile only when the circle passes its corner). The per-tick range buckets (`Battle._eb`) use them, so
`enemiesInKeys`, `anyEnemyInKeys` and `unitsInGrid` — the attack ranges of operators, summons and devices, skill and
trait range grids, the skill triggers, auras over a range and strike areas — take an enemy whose circle overlaps one
of their tiles; kit checks against a unit's range (`unit.rangeKeySet`, a range grid; 45 sites) use `bodyInRange`. A
huge enemy's hit rectangle is its collider, unchanged. Tile rules keep the tile of the position through
`enemiesOnTiles` (a second set of buckets, `_et`) and `bodyInKeys`: terrain and tile states (雪地, 珊比's glue, burning
ground, 黍's sown tiles, 陈's slime), traps and their blasts (望's 棋子, 多萝西's traps, 香槟's bombs), "所在格"
checks (瑕光 S2's sleep, 乌尔比安's anchor tile), the PRTS 格子判定 notes above, and the enemy side's own area
selections. 谬因's profile carries `rangeTiles`, so her searches read the tile buckets. `foesInColliderRadius` serves
the five 碰撞判定 radius effects (radius + 0.1 for a regular enemy); other radius effects keep the centre distance.
The per-tick index is built without per-enemy arrays, and units declare the index's `_ebMulti` flag in their
constructor: written onto enemies mid-battle, it changed their hidden class and the heavy boss field of
`test/sim/robustness.test.js` went from about 91 to 130 µs per tick; declared, it is back at about 91.

Also corrected: the line "officially the collision pushes a blocked enemy to its blocker's front", attributed to
playtest #6 (blocking.js, DESIGN §5, SIM.md, `docs/history/v2.5-playtest6.md`, two test comments). The owner says it
was a wrong record of their words. The rule "阻挡了就一定要能打到" stands: a blocker can always hit what it blocks, where
the enemy stands.

[ASSUMED]: the Chebyshev "周围八格" helper (`cheb`) and the facing-line tests (`onFrontLine`, ARC-Y 正前方) keep the
tile of the position; radius effects PRTS does not mark keep the centre distance; 蕾缪安's 0.8 inner ring stays a
centre distance; zone and strike areas without a PRTS note (异客's storm, 林's burst, the thrown areas) count as
ranges.

Tests: `test/sim/collider-range.test.js` (the geometry; the screenshots' case — blocked 0.69–0.71 past the guard's
centre, the sniper never hits it and the guard does; 0.05 and 0.15 past the last tile; `rangeTiles`;
`foesInColliderRadius`; the inlined index against `colliderKeys` / `bodyKeys` at 400 positions),
`test/content/op_aphris.test.js` (谬因 by tile), `test/content/op_clemnt.test.js` (风暴潮's splash by collider), `test/sim/hit-tile-round.test.js` (tile
and range buckets), and the tests whose geometry moved by 0.1: `test/sim/round40-attack-commit.test.js`,
`test/sim/feedback5-lemuen-s3-wait.test.js`, `test/sim/feedback1e-skillrange.test.js`,
`test/sim/feedback1-tank-triggers.test.js`, `test/content/kits_t6.test.js` (异客's storm), `test/match/funnel-redeploy.test.js`
(a drone launched before the knockout and landing on its tick proves nothing about the reset) and
`test/match/bot.test.js` (the rehearsal pair re-picked: 9,4 3 leaks, 9,5 1). Golden regenerated, all six families:
roster 49/49, bonds 45/46, fields 22/22, matches 21/21, standins 10/10, diy 129/140 changed (克莱门莎 S2's splash:
diy-135 and diy-136); over the 267 scenarios with kills, kills +211 and leaks −166 in all, 146 kill counts unchanged.

### 32.14.21 The bench keeps what overflows its fifteen slots (PR #630)

PR #630 by @mxh1999, merged from its head (`92b97ecb`, one commit on released 0.2.6 `488db49f`) onto 0.3.0.
The note the pull request added under the released 0.2.6 history is not kept; this section is the record.

Seen: once the ten regular slots and the five visible temporary slots were full, a passive gain was lost. A
bounty, a trait, a strategy or effect grant, and equipment returned by a sale, a merge or a transformation were
destroyed or sent back to the pool. The toast 「整备区已满，获得的装备已销毁」 named that loss for equipment. A
shop buy and a reward pick were already refused with `HAND_FULL`.

Official: [PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 §手牌区](https://prts.wiki/w/卫戍协议/帮助#手牌区), checked 2026-10-14. The regular
hand is ten slots. Recruiting or buying that would add a card is refused while it is full. The temporary row shows
five slots; units that overflow it stay hidden and move into a visible slot when one opens. Ready stays blocked
until that overflow is cleared.

Now: `PlayerState.temp` still starts at length 5 and grows when a passive `stow` (`allowTemp`) finds no free
visible slot, so nothing is discarded for passing fifteen. Selling, deploying, merging, equipping or destroying
frees a regular slot, and `_fillHandFromTemp` pulls the next piece: the visible row from the right, then the
hidden queue. Hidden pieces still merge and still count in stock. Private state sends the whole queue, including
on reconnect; the own field and a scouted field draw only the first five. A full regular hand still returns
`HAND_FULL` for a buy, a reward pick, a withdrawal, and an in-place re-orientation whose lifted summon would need
a free null slot. Plugin DSL `gainItem` and a script handler's `ctx.grantChess` / `ctx.grantItem` call
`acquireChess` / `acquireItem`, so they use the same queue. Bot prep-end walks pieces rather than shifting
indices, and under AI 托管 it still does not destroy a human's items to make room. The 0.3.0 lines that do not
overlap stay: a merge remembers `mergedInto` on each consumed copy (#627), a successful manual refresh clears
`shop.frozen` (§32.14.7), and the collider work is untouched.

[ASSUMED] PRTS does not say which hidden piece fills a hole first. The visible row keeps its existing
right-to-left order, then the hidden queue in arrival order (the contributor's choice, kept). Every prep-end
item grant queues the same way, not only Victoria's hammer (the owner's decision of 2026-10-04, META).

Tests: `test/match/bench-overflow.test.js` (the pull request's cases, plus a DSL `gainItem` and a script
`ctx.grantChess` / `ctx.grantItem` on a bench whose fifteen visible slots are already full). The browser file
`test/ui/bench-overflow.e2e.test.js` is written; this sandbox did not run it. Current rules: DESIGN §6.2;
reference: META §3.

### 32.14.22 Q / X act on the hovered unit, W buys the hovered card (PR #629)

Seen: players asked to retreat or sell the operator under the pointer without selecting it first, and to buy the shop
card under the pointer with one key (PR #629, @mxh1999, written against 0.2.6).

Now: Q retreats and X sells the own operator under the pointer when the key is pressed. The target is chosen again at
that moment by the same tile pick as a click (`render/pick.js` through `pieceAt` on the 2D and 3D boards; the
simplified field resolves the piece element under the pointer). An earlier selection is not used. Board operators can
retreat; board, bench and temporary-bench operators can be sold. Blank tiles, the HUD, a plugin slot, items and
summons are not targets. W buys the visible shop card under the pointer (an operator or an item) through the existing
funds and bench checks. A click still confirms in two taps. Empty and sold slots are not targets. While a promotion
reward or a special refresh replaces the shop row (#617), the shop cards are not mounted and the reward cards are not
buy slots, so W does not buy a hidden shop card or a reward card. The keys are rebindable. A saved map that already
uses W keeps that binding, and buy takes the first free default key. The shortcuts do not run while a text field is
focused, a modal or the guide is open, the match-info drawer is open (Esc still closes it), a piece is dragged, the
facing wheel is open, a pointer button is held, the pointer has left the window, the window has blurred, or the key
is repeating. They also do not run for a spectator, an eliminated player, a player watching another board, or a seat
on AI autopilot (local 暂离 or the server's autoplay). Esc still closes overlays. A solo battle's pause stays on the
ready key only for a living player who is not spectating, not watching another board, and not on AI autopilot.

[ASSUMED]: no new gameplay rule. Hover targeting and the W default are this client's input change. The golden files
are unchanged.

Tests: `test/ui/feedback5-hotkeys.test.js` (the buy action, legacy maps, `prepShortcutsSuspended`),
`test/ui/hotkeys.e2e.test.js` and `test/ui/hover-hotkeys.e2e.test.js` (browser, `SP_E2E=1`). Golden unchanged
(client input only).

### 32.14.23 Download or import the full art on the title screen (PR #628)

Seen: the title screen did not offer a way to warm the browser cache with the full art list, and a player who already
had the files (a setup `public/assets`, or a published full zip) still pulled them from the host. PR #628 by
@mxh1999 added the download queue on 0.2.6. The maintainer asked for it on 0.3.0 and, in the same dialog, for an
import of a local pack so those bytes are not sent again.

Now: **下载完整素材** walks the served `/data/assets.json` and the optional `/data/local-assets.json` (0.3.0 voice
banks, statistics art, local device files, Spine pages) and reads each URL into the browser HTTP cache. The queue
pauses, resumes and retries; a 404, an HTML fallback, an empty body or a 206 is not success. Closing the dialog
pauses it. There is no persisted "complete" flag. Import reads a folder (`webkitdirectory`, and `showDirectoryPicker`
when the browser has it) or a zip (central directory in the page; stored entries copied; deflate via
`DecompressionStream('deflate-raw')`). One prefix is chosen for the pack (`…/public/assets/…`, the path as it is, or
a folder that needs `assets/` or `fonts/` in front). A file is stored only when the first 16 hex digits of its
SHA-256 equal the served URL. A Spine model is stored only as a whole group, with the server's model hash (the
members' file hashes joined by newlines; the default `<skel>.png` counts even when the served object has no
`textures` field). Nothing is uploaded. Accepted bytes sit in Cache Storage (`sp-imported-assets-v1`) at the hashed
URL and, for audio, at the `/media/~<hash>/…` URL. `public/sw.js` is a classic same-origin worker registered from
boot on a secure context. It answers only GET `/assets/~<16 hex>/`, `/fonts/~<16 hex>/` and `/media/~<16 hex>/`.
A hit is the cached response. A miss is the network, and that response is not written into this cache. HTML, scripts,
`/data/`, `/sim/` and `/ws` are not intercepted. `worker-src 'self' blob:` was already the site policy: `'self'`
stays, `blob:` is not removed, and no remote worker origin is added. `SIM_WORKER_CSP` does not gain this worker.
The download queue skips a URL that import already stored. On `http://` to a LAN address the import controls say
why and do not throw; download still works. The player can cancel an import and can clear the imported cache after
a confirm. The dialog shows how many files are already imported and how many the list still lacks.

[ASSUMED]: prefix tie-break is strip-`public/`, then as-is, then `assets/`, then `fonts/`; a score of zero does not
guess. A file the manifest does not list is skipped, not failed. An incomplete Spine group is not stored under the
model URL; members that exist only in that group are skipped, while a file that also has its own hash URL can still
match that URL. A group whose served member hashes disagree is ignored. Paths are case-sensitive. Plain URLs with
no hash segment are not imported. `Range` requests are not served from the import cache. ZIP64 entry counts and
offsets are read; multi-disk zips are not. Caps are 500_000 entries, 64 MB of central directory and 256 MB per
entry. CRC-32 is checked when the stored CRC is not zero. Empty files are rejected. `navigator.storage.estimate()`
is the whole origin. `persist()` asks the browser to keep the origin and is not a guarantee; iOS may still drop
the cache after about seven days. Storage is per origin, so a new host name needs a new import. The worker is a
classic script because a module worker is not available on every browser this project already supports.

Tests: `test/ui/asset-download.test.js`, `test/ui/asset-import.test.js` (zip stored and deflate, prefix, bad data,
single-file and Spine-group hashes, a mismatch rejected, `/media/~`, worker scope, CSP `worker-src`),
`test/ui/asset-import.browser.test.js` and `test/ui/asset-download.browser.test.js` (`SP_E2E=1`),
`test/plugins/csp.test.js`, `node tools/i18n.mjs check --all --strict`, `node tools/ci.mjs --only lint,imports,typecheck`,
`node tools/golden.mjs` (unchanged).

Later: §32.14.39 gives the import its own button and dialog beside 下载完整素材 and adds the example asset pack.

### 32.14.24 Displayed equipment stays purchasable; 凯瑟琳's pool holds 突变细胞 (#624, #631)

Both are the maintainer's decisions of 2026-10-14.

**Displayed equipment (#624).** Seen: a shop equipment card whose shared stock another player had already emptied still
showed and could be clicked; the server answered `SOLD_OUT` and the client toasted 已售出. An equipment reward already
on screen (定向投放, 见者有份, and the other item offers) was refused the same way. Operators already on screen have
been buyable since #498 (§30.1): the purchase records a deficit and later rolls skip an empty operator.

Official: [路标月报#2](https://www.bilibili.com/video/BV1eLXXBqEgF/) at 1:30 and 2:30 (equipment from any source
occupies stock) and at 2:50–2:57 (an operator card already shown can be bought after the pool is empty). The video
settles the operator case. It does not show an equipment card bought after the stock is empty. The maintainer decided
on 2026-10-14 that the same rule applies to an equipment card already shown: it can be taken, and a card not yet
shown is not drawn once the stock is empty.

Now: `SharedItemPool` keeps a `debt` count per base item, beside ownership. `left` stays the clamped `cap − held`
(never negative), so rolls, `canGain` and `rollItems` still treat an empty item as unavailable. `buy` of an equipment
shop slot and `pickReward` of an equipment reward pass `acquireItem(..., { displayed: true })`, which no longer
refuses on `canGain`. Once the piece is actually kept, `owe` records how many of its copies did not fit (`need` minus
`left` from before the gain): a normal records 1 when nothing is free, a golden whose second copy did not fit records
