1 (a stow always keeps the piece since the bench queue of §32.14.21). Fixed `SERVER_GAIN_EQUIP` gifts still overdraw with
no debt entry. 整备 (`upgradeItem`), ordinary and random grants, 机变 item cards (`soldOut`, and the 库存不足 label on
those cards only) and strategy grants that are not an already-shown card stay stock-limited. Destroying, consuming, a
temp wipe and elimination call `repay` for that piece's copies first. Equipment that comes off a carrier that is sold,
merged or transformed always finds a place (§32.14.21) and stays owned, so those do not repay equipment debt. `collectViolations` allows `held` above `cap` only up to `debt`; only the fixed-gift
families may sit above `cap` with a smaller debt. The shop equipment card has no stock label. `SOLD_OUT` is only a
slot or a reward that was already taken.

[ASSUMED] Debt is a count on the base id, not a signed raw left like the chess pool, because equipment stock is
derived from ownership and the fixed gifts must be able to overdraw without a debt entry. A copy that leaves play
repays debt before any of that item can be rolled again, because `left` stays 0 while `held` exceeds `cap`. No weight
in the stock table changes.

Tests: `test/match/shared-stock.test.js` (a displayed shop card and a displayed reward, including a golden that only
partly fits; 整备 and a random grant still refused; buying the same card again is `SOLD_OUT`; destroy, a temp wipe
and elimination repay; invariants), `test/match/community-round30.test.js` (fixed gifts still overdraw with no debt).

**凯瑟琳's 定向投放 (#631).** Seen: `pool_equip_kathe` was every shop-eligible item (`rule: shopEligible`), so
突变细胞 (`chess_item_5_08_e_a`, `shopExcluded`) never appeared. The maintainer checked the official client on
2026-10-14: 凯瑟琳's 定向投放 can roll 突变细胞, and the 调度中心 level does not matter.

Official: the effect `up_shop_add_special_goods` names the pool `pool_equip_kathe`, with `count` 3 and `choice` 1.
The official table names the pool and not its contents. PRTS does not list the weights.

Now: `tools/build-data.mjs` sets `pool_equip_kathe.also` to 突变细胞, and `data/choices.json` carries that list.
`Match.rollItemId` appends `also` to that pool's draw. The shop item slot, 见者有份, 道具补给 and 机密商店 still read
shop-eligible items only, so 突变细胞 is still not sold. 突变细胞 has no shop cap (`itemPoolCopies` is null for a
`shopExcluded` item), so the draw does not wait on a stock count. The shop-level cap stays off, as it has since 0.2.0.

[ASSUMED] The pool stays uniform: 突变细胞 has the same weight as each shop item. The official table does not give
weights.

Tests: `test/match/pool.test.js` (the `also` list; with every shop item refused, 定向投放 at shop level 1 draws the
cell and the shop slot, 见者有份 and a plain item roll do not), `test/match/feedback5-cathy-pool.test.js`,
`test/content/bands.test.js`, `test/ui/feedback1-offers.test.js`. Golden: roster, bonds, fields, standins and diy
are unchanged, and so is every match that does not draft 凯瑟琳. `coop4-ABYSS-7` (ai_3 is `band_cathy`) moves
because her 1→2 level-up 定向投放 now draws from a pool that also contains 突变细胞: that seat's board hash
first differs at round 4, and the shared coop fight drifts after it (`rng.shop` 792 → 819, `rng.meta` 67 → 71,
`rng.bots` 22878 → 23114; `end.virtualMs` 136500 → 136850; ai_2, eliminated at round 13 before, is alive at
the end). Updated with `node tools/golden.mjs --update --family matches --only coop4-ABYSS-7`.

### 32.14.25 Official battle effects from the local client

Seen: the field view drew every battle effect itself (DESIGN §17.3): one spark set per damage type, one projectile
sprite per sim kind, generic auras and bursts. In the client every operator, enemy and summon has its own attack,
projectile, hit and status effects. 0.3.0 extracts them from the player's own client (Phase 1a / 1b, DESIGN §33: 4,253
effect prefabs for the mode's units); this section is the runtime that plays them (Phase 2).

Official: the effects are Unity prefabs — ParticleSystems with their renderers, MeshRenderers, TrailRenderers,
LineRenderers, skinned meshes and animation clips — driven by the client's behaviours (ParticleEffect,
ProjectileEffect, FxDelay, FxUVTweener, FollowMountPoint, …) and drawn with the Torappu particle shaders under the 30°
battle camera. The extracted files keep them in Unity's terms (DESIGN §33). The shaders were read from the client's own
programs in a scratch folder outside the repository and written anew in GLSL from what they do; no official shader
source is in the repository. Phase 1b checked the ability events, the custom vertex streams, FxUVRotation,
PauseEffectIf and the texture-sheet frame against the client (§33's changelog); the runtime follows those readings.

Now: `public/js/render/vfx/` (DESIGN §9 "Official battle effects"): a ParticleSystem interpreter, the clips and the
behaviours, GLSL ES 3.0 programs for the client's shader families, a WebGL2 renderer that draws in two passes of the
Pixi stage with the board's camera, and the wiring from index.json to the battle's events — normal attacks (the cast
effects at their ability events: ON_CAST_START ones with the attack's wind-up, ON_SPELL_ON ones at the strike; the
projectile in the sim's flight time with its hit on arrival; melee hits when the damage lands), heals, status loops,
deaths and deploys. 设置「官方特效」 (关 / 精简 / 完整; auto: 完整 on a desktop, 精简 on a touch device) appears only when
the server's effect file list names effects; the effects draw on the 3D board with WebGL2. An official hit replaces
the procedural spark and slash; the damage numbers stay. Skills kept the procedural effects in this phase; Phase 3
(below) plays them (index.json `skills` roles and modes, §33.12). Coverage of the full set: every one of its 38 shaders maps to a ported
family except `Torappu/Particles/SimSpine/AlphaBlend-ZDistorted` (four materials of 琴柳's skill flags
`sleach_skill_02_flag_01` / `_03_flag_01`), which draws with the basic program; 21 behaviour classes cover all but
about 40 rare ones (13 prefabs or fewer each, mostly skill and buff-state logic: PauseAnotherEffect, FunnelHitEffect,
SwitchableEffectByBuffStackCnt, PauseEffectIfDisappeared, …), which are not run; the CollisionModule (66 systems) is
ignored, and so is the attack-speed playback scale (`_usePlaybackSpeed`).

Performance (`tools/perfbench.mjs --fx`: Apple Silicon, headless Chrome on ANGLE Metal, 844 × 390 at DPR 2, quality
high; a 4× CPU throttle stands in for a mid-range phone): the normal battle (`hard-normal-r13`, 20 units, 5 effects) and
the boss battle (`hard-boss-r14`, 24 units, 33 effects / 144 particles / 42 draws) hold 60 fps at both tiers, at 4× too.
The 联防 battle (`hard-unite-r13`, 42 units, about 75 effects / 470 particles / 145 draws) holds 60 fps at 1× (view CPU
2.95 ms and Pixi render 2.23 ms a frame, against 2.47 / 1.78 ms without the effects); at 4× two runs gave 55.8 and
58.5 fps at 完整, 58.3 and 59 fps at 精简, and 60 and 56.7 fps without the effects — single dropped frames (at most 2.1 % of
the frames over 33 ms, no long task), within the runs' noise. Textures on the GPU: 7–13 MB a battle at 精简, 29–52 MB at
完整.

[ASSUMED]:
- Unity behaviour the client's data cannot show (§33.5): the billboard roll's sign, the Limit Velocity dampen once per
  sub-step, FxUVRotation's matrix sign; also a looping system's emission count starting from 0 each loop, the cone /
  hemisphere / box-shell conventions as Unity documents them, horizontal billboards in the world XZ plane, and a
  stretched billboard's texture u along the stretch with u = 1 at the head.
- Direction: a `_rotationZ` helper's authored rotation is replaced by the direction relative to `_mainDir`
  (Torappu.SharedConsts.Direction UP 0, RIGHT 1, DOWN 2, LEFT 3; `ifrit_attack_01_start`'s 90° is a leftover);
  FLIP_TWO_SIDE turns a `_rotationY` helper 180° and mirrors an effect that has none about its anchor; the facing a
  directional emitter row reads (`wiring.js _dirIndex`); `delayIfFirstAttack` is not applied (the field view's first
  attack has no lead-in clip).
- The cast's end (a looping cast row's end event): the attack clip's end at the clip's own speed, sped up only when
  the attacks come quicker than the clip (the field view may stretch the clip over a slower rhythm); after 0.6 s for an
  attack no wind-up started. A looping prefab on a one-shot row (`aroma_skill_02_range_02` on 阿罗玛's S2) stops there
  too, instead of never ending; a looping effect nobody owns ends after 8 s at the latest.
- Skins whose points lie whole tiles away from the feet (84 of the 709 skins' hit points: 72 enemies, 10 summons,
  2 operators — the prefab's placement inside its composition, e.g. `enemy_1007_slime` at (3.01, 3.03), `char_446_aroma`
  at x −1.9) are moved back over the feet (`mounts.js skinShift`); the extractor should drop that placement.
- PauseEffectIf hides the whole effect while it pauses; the conditions no view shows (immunities, buffs, modes,
  states) never pause it.
- Renderers lying flat near the ground draw under the units and everything else over them; the client sorts effects
  and units in one 3D scene, so a unit standing in front of an effect is not drawn over it here.
- The effect canvas is transparent over the three.js board: the rewritten blend factors are exact for alpha and
  additive blending over the opaque board, approximate for the Multiply family.
- The budget: the caps per tier, dropping a one-shot over the caps or loaded late, the time cap of a one-shot nobody
  stops.

Tests: `test/render/vfx-curves.test.js` (curves, gradients, the MinMax modes), `test/render/vfx-psys.test.js`
(emission, bursts, shapes, the modules, sub-emitters, trails, prewarm, spaces), `test/render/vfx-frame.test.js` (the
frame map, the view-projection against `Camera.project` for every camera kind, Euler order, clip sampling, mount
points, the heightfield), `test/render/vfx-effect.test.js` (lifecycle, pooling, placement and direction helpers, draw
items, materials, the library, the setting, the wiring: emitter rows, cast events, PauseEffectIf),
`test/ui/gameLogic.test.js` (the setting's sanitising) and the browser test `test/render/vfx.browser.test.js`
(`RENDER_E2E=1`: blending on a grey board, an attack from index.json, the light tier, a clean console; pictures in
test/e2e/out/). `tools/perfbench.mjs --fx` measures the tiers. Golden unchanged (client only).

**Phase 3: the skills, the passive skills and the 秘术师 charge.** Seen: with 官方特效 on, a skill still drew the
procedural activation burst, the gold aura under the unit and the kits' generic fx (blasts, chevrons, rings), and a
秘术师's stored energies showed nothing unless 设置「秘术师能量」 was on (GitHub #633: the maintainer's decision of
2026-10-15 is that the official effect is their default display). Official: a skill prefab (`[uc]skills.ab`) holds the
skill's emitters, abilities and buffs, and the client's buff templates their actions by buff event (§33.12 `skills`):
the effects play at the abilities' events (cast start, spell, cast end), at the buffs' events (start, finish, damage
taken, periodic trigger) and while the skill is ready (ReadySkillEffect); a skill's mode switches the owner's attack
effects; 深靛's ChargeRangedAttack lights one charge effect per stored energy at the unit's Special points and fires them
from there with the next attack (her charpack, transcribed in `skills.js CHARGE_EMITTERS`). Now: `render/vfx/skills.js`
(Phase 3a) turns every skill's uses into a plan — start, loop, end, ready, range, each attack's cast / range / aim / hit
groups, the `each` rows of any attack, trigger, the buffs on others, the pulse rows, and what stays unmapped and why —
and `render/vfx/skillfx.js` (Phase 3b, owned by the wiring) plays it on the signals the field view has (DESIGN §9
"Official battle effects", Skills; §33.16 for what it reads): the skill's events, the snapshot's UF.SKILL for a view
built mid-battle, the deploy for a passive, the SP for `ready`, the owner's attacks and their damage order for its
attack rows and hit groups, its damage taken for `trigger`, b.snap `energy` for the charge, and the kits' own fx where
they stand for an official effect. A skill the official layer shows draws no procedural burst or aura. Coverage (the
full set, `tools/vfx-skill-coverage.mjs`): of the roster's 549 operator (unit, skill) pairs 547 are in index.json and 501
have a plan the battle view plays (91.3 %), 26 only switch the attack mode, 8 only put buffs on others, 12 name no effect
(they keep the procedural burst); 1,127 of their 1,233 effect keys are placed (106 unmapped: 72 at buff events the client
never sees, 11 periodic ticks, 5 created at the skill's end, 5 paths, 4 hits of non-attack abilities, 4 buffs on summons,
4 channel ticks, 1 tile set); of the 79 entries on other units 38 sit on the skill attacks' targets (drawn there), 2 are
generic status loops (drawn when the sim reports the status) and 39 have no carrier the client knows; 19 pulse rows of 17
skills play where their kit sends its own fx. Summons: 2 of 78 token skills are in the index; enemies: none of 126.
Through the sim (every chess skill and every 自选 operator's three, 394 battles of 30 s fed to the wiring): no error,
213 cast their skill, 202 of them with official effects (the other 11 have none extracted), 33 had kit fx bound. The kit
fx left procedural: everything away from the owner that no pulse row or FX_BINDINGS entry stands for (a blast at a
target or a tile, a zone, a wall), other kits' reticles and locks, beams and bolts to targets, volleys (黑键's release:
no stored count reaches the client), summons, displacements, DP and coins, unit states (dodge, crit, sleep, lift),
蕾缪安's shells. Performance (`tools/perfbench.mjs --fx`, as in Phase 2: Apple Silicon, headless Chrome on ANGLE Metal,
844 × 390 at DPR 2, quality high, 1× and 4×): the normal battle (`hard-normal-r13`, 20 units: 12 effects, 69 / 130
particles, 29 draws at 精简 / 完整) and the boss battle (`hard-boss-r14`, 24 units: 35 effects, 392 / 737 particles, 67–69
draws) hold 60 fps at both tiers, at 4× too. The 联防 battle (`hard-unite-r13`, 42 units: 50 effects, 274 / 515
particles, 154–157 draws — fewer effects than Phase 2's about 75, as one attack on several targets now casts once)
holds 60 fps at 1× (view CPU 2.61 ms and Pixi render 2.06 ms a frame at 完整, against 2.89 / 1.47 ms without the
effects); at 4× two runs gave 58.5 and 56.7 fps at 完整 (0.9 % and 1.2 % of the frames over 33 ms), 59 and 59.3 fps at
精简 and 60 fps without — Phase 2's band (55.8–58.5 fps at 完整). Textures on the GPU: 9–17 MB a battle at 精简,
37–67 MB at 完整 (Phase 2: 29–52 MB; the skills' prefabs).

[ASSUMED] (Phase 3):
- the buffs a skill's attacks put on their targets last our record's blackboard duration (`duration`, `…_duration`,
  `….duration`), else while the skill lasts; an instant skill without one draws none;
- a skill turned on and off in one frame with no attack of its owner between plays its first attack's cast and range
  once (23 of the roster's 61 instant casts in a survey of the kits: the kit deals the damage itself);
- a passive is on while its unit is deployed (a PASSIVE skill its kit runs as a timed one follows its events once
  UF.SKILL shows); `ready` for a multi-charge skill whose stored charges the snapshot does not show;
- the k-th HP 'dmg' (or 'heal') on a target in one frame is the attack's k-th hit (past the last group, the last
  again); an element gauge fill is no hit; one attack on several targets ('atk' per target in one tick) casts once;
- a skill's attack ability replaces the mode's rows only for an attack of its kind (a heal, a shot, a blow), and a
  ranged one flies the skill's projectile named after the skill (the only one, else `_s<n>` / `_sk<n>`, else the first);
- held rows of a skill's single cast stop after 0.6 s; a trigger plays at most once a frame; one effect reached two
  ways in a plan plays once;
- the start flash (blast, buff, wave, pillar, strike, element, shield, heal, healAoe, sp archetypes on the owner in
  the frame of an official start) and a skill-tagged flash while the official loops play are the official effects'
  own; a pulse row stands for a skill-tagged kit fx (on the owner: its buff's ticks; away: its other hits);
- 克莱门莎 S3's mark lasts the blackboard's `projectile_life_time` and stands for her three strikes on that tile; the
  release's projectiles leave with the shot; 深靛's slot loops sit at the skin's static points (the client's bones move
  them).

Tests (Phase 3): `test/render/vfx-skills.test.js` (the plans), `test/render/vfx-skillfx.test.js` (the wiring on a
synthetic index: on / off, mid-battle loops, passives, ready, the mode, the skill's own attack rows and projectile, a
blow of another kind, hit groups, `each`, buffs on targets and the status hand-over, trigger, the charge's stores,
release and death with no double draw, an instant skill without an attack, the kit fx bindings, a tier switch,
preloading, the layer off), `test/render/vfx-skills.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`: 克莱门莎 S1 and S3's mark,
伊芙利特 S2 and S3, 深靛's charges and release, 砾's passive, 阿罗玛's ready loop, the light tier; pictures in
test/e2e/out/vfx-skill-*.png), `test/render/forms.test.js` and `test/ui/official-damage-numbers.test.js` (the field view's
calls). Golden unchanged (client only).

**Phase 3c: index version 2.** The Phase 1c extractor's index (§33.15) lists every emitter of an attack's node, a mode's
blow on a blocker (`combat`), the sub-abilities of group attacks, the 秘术师's chargeable groups, the skills' root classes,
the enemies' and summons' skills and where each prefab sits; the runtime now reads them (DESIGN §33.16; a version 1
index plays as before). Attacks (`wiring.js`): a melee blow of a unit that is blocked or blocks, and every attack of a
mode without `attack`, plays the mode's `combat` (the only attack of 171 enemy modes); a group attack plays the
sub-abilities that fire (a Parallel group all, a Random group a seeded pick, the others the one fitting the attack's
kind) and flies the first one's projectile; a MultiHit emitter's hit groups play by the hit's order in the blow's frame
(min(k, last), in turn, seeded, or the first only, as the emitter says; the attack's other rows on every hit); an
UberAnimation replace group's rows play with their animation; an InCertainMode emitter's only in its mode; a VigilWolf
row bound to a buff never. Skills (`skills.js`, `skillfx.js`): the uses' exported hit groups replace the `_range` rule
(an empty group kept), a ReplacementSkill is one cast, the plan entries take `spawn` / `follow` from the prefab summary,
深靛 / 黑键 / 维伊's charge plans come from `units[id].charge` (the transcribed table stays for version 1; on the three units
the two agree), a summon without a record of its skill plays its unit's only one. Enemy skills: by the data record's
`skills[].prefabKey`; a window opens on ['cast', id, slot] (PR #275's event, which the sim on this line does not send)
or, for an enemy with a single skill, on the snapshot's SKILL anim (the sim's 0.5 s after an ability fires), plays the
skill's start, range and loops, gives the enemy's attacks in it the skill's attack rows and ends 0.3 s after the anim
leaves SKILL (or on death or leak), with the procedural flash of that frame dropped as for an operator; a form whose
name is one of the enemy's modes picks that mode for its attacks and its skills' copies. `mounts.js skinShift` is off
for version 2 (its skins have the root transform). Coverage (`tools/vfx-skill-coverage.mjs`, version 1 → 2): operator
pairs 547 → 548 of 549 in the index, 501 → 502 drivable (91.4 %); summon pairs 2 → 71 of 78, 2 → 23 drivable; enemy
pairs 0 → 123 of 126, 0 → 86 drivable (68.3 %); units with attack rows: operators 189 → 202 of 210, enemies 75 of 243 →
201 of 249 (the 6 aliases are units now), summons 19 → 21 of 57. Through the sim (fed to the wiring as in Phase 3b): the
394 battles of the roster's skills raise no error, 213 cast a skill, 203 an operator's with official effects (202
before) and 34 with kit fx bound (33); in 7 a summon cast its skill, with official effects in 4 (W's and 维什戴尔's
summons); every enemy of data/enemies.json (249 battles of 25 s against two blocking operators) raises no error and 187
play official effects of their own. Of the 58 roster enemies with a skill plan, 23 show the snapshot's SKILL window in a
battle and 11 of them have a single skill (enemy_1209_sfden and its _2, enemy_1273_stmgun_2, enemy_1275_dwlock_2,
enemy_1321_wdarft, enemy_1404_msnip, enemy_1513_dekght_2, enemy_2003_rockman, enemy_2008_flking, enemy_2017_csphts and
enemy_10085_hllevi_2): their windows open on this line; the other enemies' skills, the skills an enemy casts on its Nth
attack and the enemy skills' effects on others stay procedural until a signal names them. Performance
(`tools/perfbench.mjs --fx` as before): the normal battle (12 effects, 66–69 / 134 particles, 28–29 draws at 精简 / 完整)
and the boss battle (45 effects — 35 before: the enemies' combats and skills —, 404–411 / 763–770 particles, 71–78
draws) hold 60 fps at both tiers, at 4× too; the 联防 battle (50–51 effects, 293–310 / 521–557 particles, 156–162 draws)
holds 60 fps at 1×, and at 4× gave 55.5–56.9 fps at 完整 (0.9–2.1 % of the frames over 33 ms), 57.5–58 at 精简 and 60
without — an A/B against the Phase 3b runtime on the same index in the same hour gave 55–58.7 fps at 完整, and in both
profiles the wiring's own script time is under 0.5 % of the frame's CPU. Textures on the GPU: 10–18 MB a battle at 精简,
38–72 MB at 完整.

[ASSUMED] (Phase 3c):
- an UberAnimation emitter of `loopType` 1 plays its group animations in turn by the unit's attack count; `loopType` 0
  the animation the model plays, else its `sourceAnimKey` (superseded in Phase 3d: index v3 read the enum — 0 LOOP,
  1 RANDOM);
- a Random group's pick and a `useRandomHitEffect` group come from a seed of the unit's id and attack count (the
  client's random source is not exported), so a replay shows the same;
- a single-skill enemy's skill window is the snapshot's SKILL window and ends 0.3 s after it; an enemy form's mode is
  the mode of its name (case aside; `form2` is `Reborn`);
- a summon without a record of its skill casts its unit's only skill.

Tests (Phase 3c): `test/render/vfx-index2.test.js` (a synthetic version 2 index through the wiring: MultiHit groups —
min(k, last) with an empty group, loop, random, once, the rows without a group, `keepBaseHitEffect` —, UberAnimation in
turn and by the model's clip, Random / Parallel / Selectable sub-abilities, a blocked enemy's combat and a form's mode,
InCertainMode and VigilWolf rows, a heal's own rows, an enemy's window from the snapshot and from the 'cast' slot with
its attack rows and mode copies, a summon's only skill, the charge from the index, the exported hit groups, the root
class, the prefab placement, skinShift off, preloading), `test/render/vfx-skills.test.js` (the roster coverage by index
version), `test/render/vfx-skills.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`: enemy_1209_sfden's skill by its 'cast' slot,
enemy_10001_trslim's window from the snapshot and its end, char_4009_irene's two MultiHit groups, enemy_10027_vtsk's
combat when blocked and its shot when free; pictures in test/e2e/out/vfx-skill-*.png). Golden unchanged (client only).

**Phase 3d: index version 3, talents, the enemy skill event.** The maintainer asked for every official effect that can
be done ("敌人技能、多段命中分组这些一块做"). The Phase 1d extractor's index (§33.17) adds the units' talents and abilities, the skills'
abilities' projectiles, the buffs' lives, the groups' choice and the UberAnimation loop type; the runtime reads them
(DESIGN §33.16; versions 1 and 2 play as before) and the sim now names each enemy skill use. Talents
(`render/vfx/talents.js`, `talentfx.js`): the talents in force (the record's talents through `talentKeys`, a module's,
the trait's, a node valid with one skill; an enemy's all) and the abilities are planned like skills and bound by `when`
— deploy talents at the deploy / spawn once per life with their loops while the unit stands, passive ones while it
stands (a mode's copy in its mode), toggles on the snapshot (HP ratio, blocked, 隐匿, a restore delay), an enemy's 重生 from
the knock-out form fx (the handler's loop) to the stand-up (its end, the 无敌 loop for the enemy record's blackboard time,
the buffs it leaves), the nodes a template casts on the kit fx that stand for its trigger (a fatal blow's 'substitute' /
'undying', a stand-in's end, the skill start, the deploy), a talent attack on 'extraAttack'; the sim's forms set the
unit's mode from the data (风丸's 替身 fights in Stand, a 重生's second form in its `reborn.mode`). Skills: an attack flies
its ability's projectile (the naming guess only without one) with its mount and follow-up shots, the buffs on its
targets last their `life`, and a held row whose prefab does not loop plays once at the skill start, for its own life (a
tier switch or a view built mid-battle replayed it before). Attacks: `select` picks a group's sub-ability, `mount` /
`mountGroup` place the shot, `additionalTimes` flies the follow-up shots, and the UberAnimation `loopType` is 0 LOOP / 1
RANDOM (Phase 3c had the meaning of 1 the wrong way round). The sim: `content/enemies/helpers.js castSlot` sends
['cast', id, slot, dur?] — display only — as an enemy uses a skill: the ability engine's cooldown casts (an ability
built with `skill()` or carrying `sk`), the SP skills' attacks (from the attack's `before` hook: the event comes before
its 'atk'), the kits' own moments (an entrance barrage, a self-destruct, an activation, the egg lost, the bosses'
decrees); the slot is the skill's index in data/enemies.json `skills[]` (an override's skill keeps its prefabKey's),
`dur` its blackboard duration (the client holds the window that long); `render/interp.js` treats it as cosmetic. Golden:
the digest counts client events, so 101 scenarios change — roster 44, bonds 9, fields 19, standins 5, diy 24, matches
none — and the only differing value is `.events.cast` (no snapshot hash, kill, damage or random draw moves); the golden
files took the new counts once that was checked (only `.events.cast` differs). Determinism, the event buffer and
`test/sim/perf.test.js` / `robustness.test.js` hold. Coverage (`tools/vfx-skill-coverage.mjs`, `--sim` for the enemy
duels): of the 58 roster enemies with a skill plan 46 open their skill windows in a 75 s duel against two operators (11
before) and all 46 play official skill keys — the other 12 need what a duel does not make or have no skill use in this
sim; talents and abilities bound / in force: operators 52 / 152 (33 of 122 units), summons 28 / 37, enemies 181 / 262
(125 of 249: passive 84 / 122, toggles 41 / 47, 重生 13 / 13); the rest name no effect the client can place or have no
signal. Through the sim the roster's 394 skill battles raise no error and 61 play talent or ability effects; every enemy
of data/enemies.json (249 duels) raises no error and 190 play official effects of their own (187 before). Performance
(`tools/perfbench.mjs --fx` as before): the talents' loops raise the effects on screen — the normal battle 24–27 (12
before), the boss battle 46–48 (45), the 联防 battle 73–74 (50) — and the particles a little (86 / 177, 426 / 806, 323–342
/ 610–681 at 精简 / 完整); every battle holds 60 fps at 1× at both tiers, the normal and the boss battle at 4× too, the 联防
battle at 4× 57.7 fps at 精简 and 58.7 at 完整 (56.3 without the effects in the same run: the machine's load). Textures on
the GPU: 10–18 MB at 精简, 41–72 MB at 完整.

[ASSUMED] (Phase 3d):
- a group's `random` pick ignores its `probKeys` weights (equal odds, seeded); a BurstAttackGroup's burst attack is
  left out; `sequential` takes the configs in turn;
- a multi attack's follow-up shots are drawn only (the sim deals the attack's hits with the attack); `triggerDelta` 0
  flies them together;
- an enemy skill window lasts the 'cast' event's `dur` (else 0.3 s) after the snapshot's SKILL anim; a skill of attack

- a template-triggered node's loops last TRIGGER_HOLD_S (3 s); the fatal blow is 'substitute' or 'undying', a stand-in
  buff's end 'swap' or 'dollEnd';
- a 重生 talent's own buffs (`RebornTalent._buffs`) start at the stand-up; a husk's ManualTriggerRebornTalent plays at
  its revival.

Tests (Phase 3d): `test/render/vfx-talents.test.js` (a synthetic version 3 index: the talents in force by talentKeys, a

### 32.14.26 Reports of the night of 2026-10-15 (round 42)

**The Hidden Core with 1 LP left (GitHub #645).** Seen: a co-op run whose activated layers were well above 1200 beat
the round-14 leader with the team down to 1 LP, and the match ended there as a clear instead of entering the Hidden
Core. Official: PRTS 卫戍协议：盟约 下半 §隐秘核心 gives 「已激活盟约总层数 > 350 且 剩余生命值 > 1」 (solo) and
「所有玩家的已激活盟约总层数 > 1200 且 剩余生命值和 > 1」 (co-op), and marks the trigger conditions as user-contributed
and not guaranteed; the client data holds no such condition (the check is the server's). The maintainer first chose
LP ≥ 1 that morning; the same day a community measurement was posted on the issue (Bilibili 路标月报#2:
「已激活盟约层数和 > 350」, 「剩余生命值 > 1」), which matches PRTS, and the maintainer kept LP > 1. Now: unchanged
from 0.2.6 — `config.hiddenCore.minTeamLpExclusive` 1 (`tools/build-data.mjs`, `gamedata.js` DEFAULTS), so a team
with 1 LP left ends the match at round 14 as a clear. [ASSUMED] the co-op threshold 1200 is PRTS's (the measurement
shown covers the solo 350). Tests: `test/match/finalAssault.test.js` (1 LP does not qualify and 2 does, solo and
co-op). Golden: unchanged.

**A lone 联防 helper's camera (GitHub #635, PR #637 by @zasx77771).** Seen: in a 联防 with one helper the battle
opened on 全景, the whole two-half map, while the other half holds no player and no enemy walks there
(act1autochess_escaped_single routes every enemy through the helper's half, §25.17.2). Now: entering such a field
starts the ‹ › pill and the camera on the helper's half (`observe.js loneHalf`, `screens/game.js`); 全景 still shows
the whole field. Two helpers still start on 全景. Merge note: the PR mapped 全景 itself to the half camera, which left
no way to see the whole map; the port keeps its commit and moves the rule to the starting layer. [ASSUMED] the
official framing of the one-helper map is not documented; the half follows the official camera table's
`left_battle_camera_param` like the ‹ › half view. Tests: `test/match/observe.test.js` (`loneHalf`; 全景 unchanged).

### 32.14.27 The gate boxes after the client's Particles shaders (PR #640 by @andrew05060414, GitHub #639)

Seen: on the 3D board the start gate's red box looked pink, its dashes and warning glyph white, as if glowing; the
objective's blue box was too dark (#639; a #571 comment: 「红蓝门像在发光」). Official: the boxes use
`Torappu/Particles/Additive` (`[opt]start_end_add`) and `/AlphaBlend` (`[opt]start_end_ab`), whose GLES source (Android
`[uc]shaders.ab`) draws 2 · vertex colour · _TintColor · texture with alpha clamped to [0, 1], blends SrcAlpha One /
SrcAlpha OneMinusSrcAlpha and runs in gamma space. Both materials have _TintColor (1, 1, 1, 1); the 2 s clip
`UVAnimation` (`arts/effects/[pack]map.ab`) sets one renderer's rgb to (1, 0, 0) and the other's to (0, 0.0897, 1)
(its constant curves) and animates their alpha: 0.134 → 0.229 → 0.134 and 0.16 → 0.097 → 0.16, keys at 0 / 1 / 2 s
whose streamed coefficients (a, b, c, d) = (−0.19, 0.285, 0, 0.134) and mirrored are cubic segments with flat ends.
Cause: an untinted texture times a gain tuned by eye (`GATE_GAIN` 0.6 / 0.18) let the texture's white parts through.
Now (the PR, kept as its commit): `gateMaterial` follows the two shaders on the texture's stored values (a non-sRGB
gate texture, no colour-space conversion on output); `scene.js GATE_TINT` holds the clip's colours and alpha ranges.
Merge note: the curve is the clip's own cubic, a smoothstep between the two values (`gatePulse`), instead of the PR's
cosine (at most 0.0008 alpha apart); the blue is the clip's 0.0897. Compared with a client screenshot of
act1autochess_m01 from #549: the red box, its dashes and glyph are red; the objective's frame is light blue with the
white warning glyph and corner marks of its alpha-blended face. [ASSUMED] the clip's renderer paths are hashes that
match no name in the bundle: red is read as the start gate and blue as the objective's additive part; the box meshes
carry no vertex colours (white). PR #638 (xcdoge) drew the same boxes alpha-blended with a per-gate tint and a
tangent reading of the keys, which the client data do not support, and PR #619 asked for the same data; both are
closed in favour of this one. Tests: `test/render/board3d.test.js` (the tint, both curves and the cubic at 0.25 s),
the browser suite `test/render/board3d.browser.test.js`.

### 32.14.28 A 秘术师's stored energies: an optional count (PR #636 by @zasx77771, GitHub #633)

Seen: a 秘术师 stores one attack's energy whenever an attack finds no target (`professions.js` installMystic:
`trait.stored`, at most `profile.storeMax` — 3, 4 for 深靛's MSC-X module) and empties the lot into the next attack;
nothing on screen showed how many were stored. Official: the client draws the stored energies as an effect on the
operator (深靛's prefab: `MultiChargeRangedAttack` with `indigo_attack_charge_01_start` and `_02`–`_05`); it shows no
number. Now (the PR, kept as its commit): b.snap carries an optional `energy` readout `[[id, stored, cap]]`
(`snapshot.js mysticView`, display only like `ammo` / `wolves` / `coins`, zero included, allies only; kits with a store
of their own — 维伊, 黑键 — have none), interpolated with the other counts (`render/interp.js`), and `UnitView` draws
`stored/cap` with a violet orb under the purse. Merge note: the maintainer decided on 2026-10-15 that the count is an
option, 设置「秘术师能量」 (`mysticEnergy`), off by default like 「弹药数量」 (§31.9), and that the official effect is
the default display once the official battle effects can play it (§32.14.25). Tests: `test/sim/hud-readouts.test.js`
(the PR's cases), `test/render/hud-readouts.test.js` (the PR's cases with the setting on; off by default, no row and
no gap, live), `test/ui/mystic-energy.test.js` (the setting, the toggle, the four language packs, the diagnostics).

### 32.14.29 纯烬艾雅法拉's blink (GitHub #647)

Seen (#647, @CurlyZhu): 纯烬艾雅法拉 blinks in Idle and keeps her eyes shut while her S3 is on, yet her red eyeballs
stayed over the closed lids — only the brows and lids moved — whenever the stencil masks were off (more than one
operator with clipped eyelids on the field, or below high quality: render/app.js pickClipping; 0.2.2 §27.40, GitHub
#177). Her blink: the lids `F_L_Eyecut` / `F_R_Eyecut` are 8-point clipping polygons on the bones `F_*_Eye`, each
covering one slot, the eyeball `F_*_Eyeball` — a region (25 × 29, 18 × 26) on a bone that does not inherit scale
(NoScale). Idle (2.67 s) squashes `F_*_Eye` to 0.05 across the eye at 2.17 s and back by 2.53 s, and swaps
`F_*_Eyelash` to the closed-lid region `F_*_Eyeclose2` from 2.17 to 2.30 s: the polygon becomes a slit 1.7 units high
through the eyeballs, which keep their size — only the mask hides them. Skill_1_Idle blinks the same way,
Skill_3_Idle (her S3, skill index 2) keeps the slit and `Eyeclose2` throughout, Attack, Start, Die, Skill_2 and
Skill_3_Begin / _Loop / _End shut them for a few frames; no attachment, colour, draw-order or deform timeline hides
the eyeballs. Her back view has no eyes and her only battle skin is the default one. Cause: (1) `slotCentre` measured
a region through `bone.a … d / worldX / worldY`, spine-ts fields that pixi-spine's bones lack (they keep the world
transform in `bone.matrix`, a PIXI Matrix): the centre was NaN and no region slot was ever hidden — 0 of 33,175 region
slot-frames in the survey below, 53 operator regions under clips. (2) Measured, the centre test still misses a lid
that closes to a slit through the slot's centre (黍's right eye in her attacks and knock-down keeps 6–8 % of its area
inside the lid but its centre too; 予愿安洁莉娜's blink, 烛煌's knock-down …) and hides a slot wholly inside a concave
clip whose mean vertex falls in the notch: 予愿安洁莉娜's weapon light `F_W_Light_C` (under the blade-shaped clip
`F_Wepaon_AC`) was hidden in every frame of 24 of her clips. Now: `clipHides` measures the share of the slot's drawn
area inside the clip polygon — each mesh triangle, or a region's attachment rectangle through `bone.matrix`, clips the
polygon (Sutherland–Hodgman, exact for a concave polygon) — and hides the slot below a quarter, keeps it from a half,
and in between (or with no measurable area) its centre decides as before. Her eyeballs hide while the lids are shut
(Idle 2.17–2.27 s, all of Skill_3_Idle); open, 80–84 % of each is inside and nothing changes. Survey (the 417 local
operator skeletons, 74 with clips, every clip at 30 Hz, the shipped and the new SpineActor side by side on the real
runtime): 58 skeletons of 54 operators change, all on shut or closing eyes — region eyes now hidden at blinks and
knock-downs (圣约送葬人, 荒芜拉普兰德, 司霆惊蛰, 望, 协律, 海霓, 林, 锏, 乌尔比安, 妮芙, 凯瑟琳, 雪猎, 维伊, 珊比,
Raidian's always-shut eyes …), slits through the centre (黍, 予愿安洁莉娜, 烛煌, 霍尔海雅, 左乐, 娜仁图亚, 赫德雷 …),
佩佩's knock-down one frame earlier (0.10 s, three quarters under the lids) — plus 林's glass blade in her S1, hidden
while less than half of it is inside its clip; shown again: 予愿安洁莉娜's weapon light, 凯尔希·思衡托's machine arm
(10 frames) and 百炼嘉维尔's chainsaw (1 frame), each at least half inside its clip. No open eye is hidden: the lowest
share of an open eye is 44 % (荒芜拉普兰德's eye white), and every operator slot now hidden in most frames of a clip is
a shut eye (her S3, Raidian, 黍's attacks, 乌尔比安's right eye in his skills, knock-downs). Two enemy skeletons change
too (澪's eyes, “萨米的意志”'s crystal). Browser renders (masks on / 0.3.0 / now) of 纯烬艾雅法拉, 乌尔比安, 圣约送葬人, 维伊,
珊比, 荒芜拉普兰德 and Raidian match the stencil's shut eyes (乌尔比安's narrowed left eye, a thin slit under the
stencil, stays hidden as in 0.3.0). Cost: 3–10 µs per clipped actor per frame (2–3.5 µs
before). [ASSUMED] the two shares and all-or-nothing per slot: a half-shut eye between them is drawn whole when its
centre is inside (her 2.13 and 2.30 s frames), and a slot a non-eye clip reveals bit by bit pops in at a half (林's
blade); a region's drawn area is its whole attachment rectangle. Tests: `test/render/eyemask.test.js` (+9, one
changed: a synthetic skeleton read by the real runtime that always runs — a region and a mesh eyeball under a lid
shut to a slit through their centres, the middle band decided by the centre, a strip wholly inside a concave clip
kept; her skeleton — regions on NoScale bones, the Idle blink, Skill_3_Idle directly and through setSkillIndex(2) +
setSkill, every clip at 30 Hz hiding only her two eyeballs (4 Idle frames, every Skill_3_Idle frame), nothing with the
masks on; 佩佩's knock-down hidden from 0.10 s), the browser suite `test/render/eyemask.browser.test.js` (new,
RENDER_E2E=1: her shut eyes with the masks off differ from the stencil by 0.06 / 255 per channel on average, ≈ 20
without the fallback).

### 32.14.30 Deploy fold and the enemy pen in 休整期 (PR #646, GitHub #602; the maintainer's decisions of 2026-10-15)

Both come from the maintainer's own recording of the official game (休整期: deploying operators while the next round's
enemies wait at the top right; frames `handoff/wip-r44/official-prep-pen.png` and `official-drag-zoom.png`, kept out of
the repository).

**The deploy fold (GitHub #602, PR #646 by @Euqorab).** Seen: #602 「部署干员、装备时棋盘缩放」 — the remake kept the
shop bar open while an operator or an item was dragged to deploy, so the board stayed small and phone players
mis-dropped. Official: the recording — picking a piece up folds the shop away and moves the camera in on the board (it
also tilts it); the maintainer decided on 2026-10-15 to do the zoom only (「我们做到放大就行了」). Now (the PR, kept as its
commit): while a piece, an item or a summon is dragged on the own editable prep board, or a legal drop's direction
wheel is open, the shop bar shows folded and the camera takes the shop-collapsed prep framing of public issue #5
(`left_prepare_camera_param` / `*_boss_prepare_camera_param`; `gameLogic deployFoldsShop`, `screens/game.js`
`deployFold` / `barCollapsed`) at once; a cancelled drag, a drop, or a chosen / cancelled direction gives the
player's own 收起 / 展开 state back — the drag never writes it; never over the pen, on a teammate's board, in battle or
for a read-only seat. The fold flights (收起 / 展开, 准备就绪 and the deploy fold) take `FOLD_CAMERA_MS` 350 ms instead of
the generic 0.75 s flight. Merge notes: the PR merged without textual conflicts into 0.3.0 (the camera flow `setCam` /
`foldCamera` / `prepCameraFor`, the pen camera, #403's drag snap, the hover hotkeys and the plugin UI did not overlap its
lines); on top of it, (1) `setCam` remembered the request with its flight time, so the way back from the pen
(🔍◀◀, which repeats that request) after a fold flew the fold's 350 ms instead of the pen's 0.25 s — it now remembers
the framing only; (2) the pickup zoom moved the board under a resting finger while the drag kept the target and the
dragged piece of the old camera until the finger moved: the view judges the drag again at the last pointer on every
frame of a camera flight (`render/drag.js refresh`); (3) the browser tests' drags on the render engine read their
target before the press, so with the zoom they landed on another tile (`feedback1-placement.e2e` dropped 伺夜 on (11, 4)
instead of (10, 4); three engine drags of `mock.e2e` and `playtest2.real.e2e`'s placements failed the same way; the PR
had tested on the DOM fallback, whose grid does not move): `test/e2e/client.mjs aimedDrag` picks the piece up, waits
for the camera to hold still and ends on the same ground spot under the new camera, as a player aims at the board
they see — the real-server `Client.drag`, `real.e2e`, the engine drags of `mock.e2e`, `coop.e2e` and
`feedback1-placement.e2e` use it; a drop meant for the shop bar (no drag-to-sell) is released over the folded shop's
tab instead. [ASSUMED] `FOLD_CAMERA_MS` 350 ms (the PR's value; no source
gives the official timing); no tilt (the maintainer's choice); the summons and Arts (画卷) fold like operators.
Tests: the PR's `test/ui/issue602-deploy-fold.test.js` (the choice, the wiring, the phone projection; + the two port
fixes) and `test/ui/issue5-fold-camera.test.js` (its updated wiring assertions), `test/render/drag.test.js`
(`refresh`: a resting pointer over a moving camera gets the tile now under it), the browser suite
`test/ui/issue602-deploy-fold.e2e.test.js` (SP_E2E=1: the PR's three DOM-fallback cases and a render-engine case with
touch input — the zoom with the pickup, the target under the resting finger after it, the exact shop camera after the
wheel is cancelled; it fails without `refresh`).

**The enemy pen in 休整期.** Seen: in the recording the next round's enemies stand in the pen during 休整期 and show at
the top right of the prep view, beyond the board, behind the board's red gates — 🔍▶▶ is a short pan to what is
already in view; the remake's prep view showed no pen. User playtest #2 item 6 (2026-09-29) had asked for that: "the
battlefield with the waiting enemies above it looked wrong — after 返回战场 and in every battle", so only the pen
camera built and showed it. Official: research 09 §2.1 already had it from prep screenshots ("At the normal prep
camera, the pen's front rows with their enemies are visible at the top-right edge, behind the upper red gate"), the
recording confirms it, and its battle start shows the board without them, the enemies then coming out of the board's
gates. Decision of the maintainer, 2026-10-15: show the pen in 休整期 as the official game does, and at the battle start
hide what stands on the top-right enemy tiles as before (「最后进入战斗再像我们现在这样把右上角出怪地块的东西隐藏」) —
it supersedes user playtest #2 item 6 for the prep camera; battles stay as they were. Now: the prep camera — the own
board, its shop-folded framing (the deploy fold included), a scouted teammate's board (their pen, from their
`m.field`), back from the pen view (返回战场) — and the pen camera build the pen block on the 3D board (`boardArea` →
`AREAS.normal`: the field and rows 13–18 × cols 6–14, the pen's two red gates (15, 7) / (18, 7) with it), draw its rows
on the 2D board (`bandFor` / `fieldRows` 6–18: the pen looks the same in both, only the lit rect moves) and show its
figures (`render/app/view.js showsPen` / `penShown`; `view.stats().penHidden`); the battle, 联防, boss and hidden
cameras do not, nor does the Final Assault / Hidden Core prep — its camera frames the boss field and pen row 14
projects about 170 px above the top of a 1280 × 720 frame (the round's leader stands on the boss field there, §23.18). Camera
flights (`restView` / `flightView`) keep both ends' areas and rows; the pen flies along when either end shows it,
except a flight from the prep camera into a battle camera, which drops it at once — a real battle also empties its
figures (`enterBattle`) — instead of at the end of the 0.75 s flight; a flight away from the pen camera keeps it while
it lasts (the camera starts there) and the next prep's flight brings it back as it begins. The framing is the official
one as before: at 1280 × 720 the pen's front row stands in the top right corner (feet y ≈ 50–100 px, x ≈ 720–1130), where
the recording's crowd stands (x ≈ 700–1050 at the same scale; with fewer enemies the pen fills from its gate column 7);
its gate (15, 7) lies under the top bar there, the board's red gates (12, 10) / (9, 10) stand at the top right as in the
official frame. Cost: entering a battle from the prep rebuilds the 3D area once (13–24 ms for `enterBattle` and the camera
request on 战场#05(下半) at 1280 × 720, 9–15 ms on the 2D board; two runs on a loaded machine); prep ⇄ pen no longer
rebuilds it; the pen's figures in the prep view animate through the impostor atlas every 3rd frame as in the pen view
(the 4× throttled prep-pen scene at 1920 × 1080: 60 fps on the 3D board, 54.5 on the 2D board). [ASSUMED] the pen block
keeps the remake's margin around `enemy_place_rect` (rows 13–18 × cols 6–14), so 战场#05(下半)'s partner blower (13, 13)
on its front wall now shows in the own prep view, as it always did in the pen view; a pen enemy tapped from the prep
view opens its card as in the pen view; the DOM fallback (simplified view) keeps its pen behind 🔍▶▶. Tests:
`test/ui/playtest2.test.js` (item 6 rewritten: the views that show the pen, the flights, the Final Assault prep),
`test/render/playtest5-maps.test.js` (the prep view builds the pen rows and (13, 13); the 3D = 2D device audit
unchanged), the browser suites `test/render/fieldview.browser.test.js` (new, both boards: the pen's rows, block, gates
and figures at the top right of the prep view, gone at once with a battle start while the camera still flies, back as
the next prep's flight begins, kept by the pen view and 返回战场, none with the Final Assault prep; screenshots
`test/e2e/out/pen-prep-topright-3d.png` / `-2d.png`), `test/render/board3d.browser.test.js` (the area keys),
`test/ui/playtest2.e2e.test.js` item 6 (rewritten: prep, pen view, 返回战场, the boss prep, every battle camera) and
`test/ui/playtest2.real.e2e.test.js` 'observe' (the preps before and after a 前往查看 → 返回战场 show the pen, the battles
do not). Golden: unchanged (client only).

### 32.14.31 自选 stock in co-op and 维多利亚's hammer on lighting up (GitHub #650; the maintainer's report of 2026-10-15)

The first is the maintainer's decision of 2026-10-15; the second follows the maintainer's report of the official game
the same day.

**自选 stock in co-op (#650, @wocenm).** Seen: in co-op the shared operators have twice the single-player copies
(§29.8), while each player's 自选 stock kept the single-player 8 (tier 5) / 5 (tier 6): `gamedata.js poolCopies()`
skipped `coopPoolMultiplier` for an `isDiy` slot (0.2.4 §29.8: "Private DIY stock keeps its per-player 8/5
capacities"). A shop chess slot draws one copy uniformly from all remaining copies (copy-weighted, §6.2), so against
the doubled shared pool a 自选 operator came up about half as often as in a solo run. Official: no table gives the
自选 stock (research 0.2.0 §2.5; 8 / 5 stay [ASSUMED]); the maintainer decided 「自选也翻倍吧，不然概率不是稀释了」. Now:
`poolCopies` applies `coopPoolMultiplier` (2) to every chess in co-op, the DIY slots included, so
`PlayerDiy.buildDiyStock` (the match start and the setup reroll) gives each slotted piece 16 copies at tier 5 and 10
at tier 6; a solo run keeps 8 / 5. The stock stays the player's own (never in the shared pool, drawn by that player
only), and a content set's `coopPoolMultiplier`, or a `poolCopiesOverrides` entry of a slot, scales it like the shared
pool. Nothing else sizes it: bots field no 自选 piece (AI 托管 reads `left`), the client shows no stock, and a plugin's
自选 operators fill the same four slots. [ASSUMED] the factor is the shared pool's own `coopPoolMultiplier`, so a 自选
operator's share of a roll stays what it is in a solo run; the base 8 / 5 are unchanged. 0.2.4 §29.8 points here.

**维多利亚's hammer when the bond lights up.** Seen: a player had 25+ 维多利亚 layers before the bond was active (「无需激活」
layers such as 烛煌's); when 维多利亚 lit up, no 维式重锤 came. `payHammers` (`bonds/core.js`) paid owed milestones on the meta
hooks that see the bond active — onLayers, onRoundStart, onPrepStart, onPrepEnd, onBuy, onGain, onSold, onMerge — and
no hook ran when a board edit changed the active bonds: lighting 维多利亚 by deploying its third member paid at the next
such hook, often the prep end, where the hammer then waited unmerged for the next prep (§23.31). Official: the
maintainer reports that in the official game the hammer arrives as soon as the bond lights up. Now:
`PlayerState.recompute` compares the active set of the bonds it computes with the previous computed state and, when
they differ, dispatches the new meta hook `onActiveBonds` `{ activated, deactivated }` (bond ids in data order;
docs/META.md §2.2) — every path that changes what the bonds count ends in a recompute: placing, moving, swapping or
withdrawing a piece, buys, sells, merges, transformations, temp → hand moves, equipment (变形同构体), effects
(`setBondCountBonus`, grants), evictions; never for an eliminated player (`eliminate` clears the board) nor a detached
preview copy (`preview.js previewPlayer`). 维多利亚's hammer handler and the settle handlers of 远见 / 奇迹
(`bonds/addon/meta.js`: 远见's 2 funds per 10 layers and its 80 / 150 discounts, 奇迹's 20 funds per 100 layers — the same
"caught up once active" rule, research 02 §8 Q7) subscribe it, so what a bond owes for layers gained while it was off
pays at the recompute that lights it up: in prep at once, and the hammer merges at once like any prep gain (a
milestone a <休整期结束时> gain crosses still pays at the prep end and waits, §23.31). Re-entrancy: each recompute reports
the difference to the state it replaces, read right before the write, so the recompute of a hammer's own grant
(acquireItem → onGain) reports nothing new; the payers read their counters (`bond:victoria:hammers`,
`bondaddon:visi:paid` / `:mira:paid`, the discount latch) before each payment, so a milestone pays once however often
the bond goes off and on, and the nested onGain of a grant never pays it twice. The AI places pieces through the same
intents: a placement in its prep pays the owed hammer, which its equip step can use that prep. The other layer
milestones (迅捷 / 灵巧 40, 突袭 50, 投资人 100) are read live and owe nothing. [ASSUMED] 远见 / 奇迹 pay at lighting up like the
hammer (the report names the hammer; they share its rule); the moment is the action's recompute (inside one action, a
swap, only the state after it counts); a dispatch nested deeper than `MAX_DEPTH` still drops the hook, and the next
catch-up hook pays.

Tests: `test/match/bond-light-up.test.js` (new: 30 layers while 维多利亚 is off, the third member placed with `g.move` in
prep — one hammer and one toast at that moment, one onActiveBonds activation; withdrawn and put back twice — no second
hammer, the events report off / on; a board move that keeps the active set reports nothing; a second milestone while
active and a third owed while off each pay once; two milestones owed at lighting up pay two hammers and the prep end
finds nothing owed; the event's bond lists in data order, none on elimination; 远见 / 奇迹 lit by a temp piece moving into
a freed hand slot pay their owed funds at once, one toast, not again after a sale and a regain, and each later
milestone (on, or owed while off) once; the AI's prep lights 维多利亚 with 40 layers owed and takes exactly one hammer),
`test/match/feedback3-prep-end-item.test.js` (the prep-end payment now comes from a <休整期结束时> gain crossing a milestone
while the bond is active — the remaining way to pay at the prep end), `test/match/diy-shop.test.js` (co-op
16 / 16 / 10, solo 8 / 8 / 5, the shared pool's factor), `test/match/feedback5-we2-diy-grants.test.js`,
`test/match/shared-stock.test.js`, `test/golden-diy.test.js` (the stock checked against the co-op cap).

Golden: roster, bonds, fields, standins and diy are unchanged, and so are 19 of the 21 matches. Only the two co-op 自选
matches move, both by the stock alone: their digests are the same with the hook disabled, and no golden match lights
维多利亚, 远见 or 奇迹 with a milestone owed. `coop2-NORMAL-70-diy`: h_0 (the human seat, 调度中心 5 from the first prep) rolls
against 16 / 16 / 10 自选 copies instead of 8 / 8 / 5; its board hash first differs at round 2, it draws 推进之王 in round 3
and fields it in rounds 3–9 (it fielded no 自选 piece before); ai_0's board first differs at round 3; `rng.shop`
579 → 524, `rng.bots` 16543 → 16299, `rng.meta` 16 → 13, team LP 55 → 43, `end.virtualMs` 119900 → 121400, stock left
8 / 8 / 5 / 5 → 14 / 16 / 9 / 10. `coop2-NORMAL-5-coverage-diy`: h_0 draws 推进之王 in round 1 (board hash and gold first
differ at round 1) and fields it in rounds 1–6, 9 and 11–13 (rounds 9–13 before), and 领主·Sharp (the second tier-6
slot) in round 7; ai_0 first differs at round 6; `rng.shop` 683 → 539, `rng.bots` 19229 → 19207, `rng.meta` 10 → 22,
team LP 51 → 72, `end.virtualMs` 121400 → 119900, stock left 7 / 8 / 5 / 5 → 14 / 16 / 10 / 10. Updated with
`node tools/golden.mjs --update --family matches --only coop2-NORMAL-70-diy,coop2-NORMAL-5-coverage-diy`.

### 32.14.32 风丸's 替身 hits air units; queued reward offers last until picked (community reports of 2026-10-15)

**风丸's 替身 (community report relayed by the maintainer).** Seen: in the trait's <替身> state 风丸 kept ignoring flying
enemies in her range. Official: PRTS 风丸 特性备注 「<替身>状态下可对空，由技能产生的召唤物不可对空」 — the trait's 替身
attacks air units, the 替身 her S2 summons does not; 归溟幽灵鲨's 替身 makes no normal attack at all (PRTS, already
`dollNoAttack`). Cause: every melee profile has `canHitFly` false, and the substitution changed only her stats. Now: her
kit declares `trait: { dollHitsFly: true }` and `targeting.js canTargetEnemy` lets such a 傀儡师 target flyers while
substituted (`Unit.trait.doll`); her 本体 and S2's summoned 替身 (a token with its own profile) still do not. Tests:
`test/content/kits_t1t2.test.js` (the substituted form hits a flyer in range, the 本体 and the summoned 替身 cannot).

**Reward offers (community report relayed by the maintainer).** Seen: a promotion's pick (晋升奖励) left with 稍后 was gone
in the next round; the strategies' special refreshes (定向投放, 见者有份) and item offers lapsed the same way. Cause:
`PlayerState.endPrep` emptied the offer queue at every prep deadline. Official: PRTS 卫戍协议/帮助 says the shop refreshes
three operators temporarily after a promotion and "购买其中1个后商店退出临时状态" — the temporary state ends with a
purchase, nothing says it expires with the round. Now: every queued offer (promotion, special refresh, item offer) stays
until picked, across rounds; the shop underneath rerolls as usual; elimination still clears them. Bots take offers at
once as before, so their games do not change. The match audit (server/match/audit.js) now checks that no queued offer is
dropped instead of that every offer lapses. [ASSUMED] the temporary state also survives the Final Assault prep and the
Hidden Core prep (no source treats them apart). Tests: `test/match/merge.test.js` (an unpicked promotion offer is still
queued in the next prep, the same cards, and can be taken there; the displayed-reward test keeps it through the round).
Golden: roster, bonds, fields, standins and diy unchanged; three match scenarios move, each from an offer that is now
kept instead of lapsing at a prep end (logged): `coop2-NORMAL-70-diy` (h_0, the scripted human, leaves an item's
operator pick of round 3 unpicked; it is taken later), `solo-NORMAL-2` (ai_0's item offer queued at the round-6 prep end,
after the bot's turn) and `coop4-FUNNY-6` (ai_1's promotion offer from a merge at the round-9 prep end, then ai_3's
garrison special goods at round 11). Updated with `node tools/golden.mjs --update --family matches --only
solo-NORMAL-2,coop4-FUNNY-6,coop2-NORMAL-70-diy`.

Seen: in the Hidden Core the leader 假想敌：管 stood one tile left of the boss block and its lower body reached about
two and a half tiles onto the left half, over the operators (the report's prep screenshot; in co-op it leaned over one
side). The round-43 reading (the official spawn tile is (row 3, col 9), so the drawing is right) was wrong about the
look: the maintainer's official recording of 2026-10-15 shows it over the boss block with its HP bar in the middle.
Official: act1autochess_h08_03 (and `_s`) spawns 管 and 弦 at (3, 9) (spawnOffset 0, one WAIT_FOR_SECONDS checkpoint)
while the Final Assault's h07_03 puts 管 at (3, 10). The local client's prefabs enemy_9021_acduml_2 and
enemy_9021_acduml (read with UnityPy, 2026-10-15) share the skeleton and the Graphic node (0, −0.2, −0.06) and differ
only in the BoxCollider2D offset ((1, 1) against (0, 1): data/enemies.json `hitArea`) and the boss HUD offset
`_bossHudOffset` ((1.4, 0) against (0, 0)). The model is authored facing right (`ENEMY_MODEL_FACES_LEFT` false): drawn
that way at (3, 9) its body covers columns 8.3–11.6, under the shifted collider and HUD; mirrored to the left, the way
every enemy starts here, it covered 6.5–9.9.
The maintainer also saw the Hidden Core's 假想敌：胄 face right (2026-10-15). enemy_9013_acstmk_2 uses the Final
Assault 胄's prefab enemy_9013_acstmk, and the two 管 prefabs differ in the collider and the HUD alone.
Now: `render/units.js` `FIXED_FACING` keeps both 管 (enemy_9021_acduml, enemy_9021_acduml_2) and both 胄
(enemy_9013_acstmk, enemy_9013_acstmk_2) facing right in the prep (the leader on the boss field) and in battle — a turn
towards a target, a walk or `faceLeader` leaves them — and `BOSS_HUD_DX` draws the Hidden Core 管's HP bar 1.4 tiles
right of its model. Positions, hit areas, the end point of projectiles and every radius stay the sim's (PRTS:
projectiles reach the 判定中心). [ASSUMED] they never turn (self-bound): their FaceSwitcher `_defaultLOrR` is 1
(regular enemies 3), read as "right". 阿利斯泰尔, “萨米的意志” and 假想敌：弦 share that 1, but nothing has shown their
look yet, so they keep the regular start (left) and their turns. Tests: `test/render/unitview.test.js` (the facing
kept for the four keys, another leader still turning, the HUD offset, the model on its tile). Golden: unchanged
(client only).

### 32.14.34 删除所选: removing several plugins in the studio (the maintainer's request of 2026-10-15)

Seen: the maintainer asked on 2026-10-15 for a button in the web studio that manages and deletes plugins in batches
(「网页的插件编辑器界面可以加一个批量管理删除插件的按钮吗」) and took it into 0.3.0. The studio could remove one file of a
plugin (its 文件 tab) but never a plugin: an owner deleted folders in `plugins/` by hand. Official: none — the studio is
this project's own (§32.9).

Now: the plugin list of `/studio/` has a checkbox beside each plugin, 全选 (mixed while some are checked) / 清除选择,
and 「删除所选（N）」, disabled with nothing checked (`public/studio/js/remove.js`, `main.js`). The confirmation — the
page's confirm dialog: focus kept inside, Escape cancels — names the plugins by name and id, says that they move to
`plugins/_removed/` and how one comes back. Confirmed, the page calls `POST /studio/api/plugins/remove` `{ ids }`
(`server/studio/index.js removePlugins`; the gate of every studio change: this machine or the LAN, the studio's key).
Each id is decided alone, once and in order: a plugin id (a `_` or dot folder is none: `PLUGIN_ID`), a real folder
right inside `plugins/` under exactly that name (`files.root`, lstat'ed: a symbolic link is `PLUGIN_STUDIO_PATH`; a
missing folder, or another case of the name on a case-insensitive disk, `PLUGIN_UNKNOWN`), not in use (new
`PLUGIN_STUDIO_IN_USE` `{ id, rooms, matches }`); it then moves to `plugins/_removed/<id>-<UTC YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-mmm>/`
(-2, -3 … when taken) with the forced install's move, factored out of `install.js moveAside` as `movePluginAside(pluginsDir,
id, into)`: the entry itself renamed, never a link followed, `_removed/` made when missing and refused when it is not a
real folder, nothing deleted. A move that fails is new `PLUGIN_STUDIO_REMOVE` `{ id, detail }` (a code such as
`EACCES`, never a path). The answer is 200 `{ removed: [{ id, to }], refused: [{ id, error, params }] }` — also when
some or all are refused —, `to` relative to `plugins/`; a request it cannot read (no list, an empty one, more than
`REMOVE_MAX` 100, a value that is no text) is 400 `PLUGIN_STUDIO_REQUEST`. Then the registry reads the folders (the
watch's rescan) and the change event names the removed ids, as after any studio change (a folder the registry had not
read yet is named all the same). "In use" is what the lobby knows: an open room's plugin set, `room.content.plugins`
(the plugins its host switched on and the 「自动」 ones they need). A running match plays its room's set — a gameplay set
never changes during a match and a cosmetic room is re-applied with the same chosen plugins (`refreshPlugins`) — so the
room covers it too. The two codes are in the error catalogue (Chinese and English, the symptom tables of PLUGINS.md §15
and PLUGIN-GUIDE.md), the route in `STUDIO_ROUTES` and the frozen API table (PLUGIN-API.md, `surface.stable.json`);
`GET /studio/api/info` gives `limits.removeMax` and the page sends more ids in turns. A plugin named `remove` keeps
its own routes (`POST …/plugins/<id>` itself is none). After the answer the list refreshes, the plugin open on the
right closes when it is one of them (as it now does quietly when a change event finds its folder gone, instead of an
alert), the note above the list (`role="status"`) says how many moved and how many stayed and takes the keyboard
focus, and the plugins left in place are shown in an alert, each with the catalogue's words (the room's code for one
in use), and stay checked. The checkbox of a folder that is a symbolic link is disabled. New strings in the four language
packs.

[ASSUMED]: at most 100 ids per request; 200 for a well-formed request answered per id (like S3's multi-object
delete); `plugins/_removed/` grows until the owner empties it. A player's personal cosmetic and interface plugins live
in that browser alone (localStorage), so the server sees no use of them: a removed one stops applying there (the catalogue no
longer offers it) and applies again once restored. `plugins/config.json`'s `default` list and a trial running on the
bench are not "in use" either: a room created afterwards logs that a default plugin cannot be applied (as for any
missing default), and the trial fails like one whose folder changed.

Tests: `test/studio/remove.test.js` (new: a folder moved to `_removed/<id>-<time>` with every file, gone from the
registry and the list, back after being moved back; several at once in order, an id named twice once,
`limits.removeMax`; an invalid id, `../`, a missing folder, another case of a name, a `_` and a dot folder and a
symbolic link refused with their codes while another goes, nothing of them touched, all refused still 200; a plugin an
open room uses refused with the room's code, while its match runs too (`matches` 1), removed once the room is gone; a
forwarded public client, no header or another origin → 404, a wrong key → 403 `PLUGIN_STUDIO_KEY`; malformed bodies →
400; `_removed` a symbolic link → `PLUGIN_STUDIO_REMOVE`, nothing written through it; a read-only `plugins/` →
`EACCES` only, no path; the change event names the removed ids; a plugin named `remove` keeps `GET …/plugins/remove`),
`test/ui/studio.e2e.test.js` (new test, `SP_E2E=1`: checks by click and Space, 全选 mixed / all, 清除选择, the
confirmation's names and ids, Escape moves nothing, confirmed: two moved, the open one closed, the one a room uses
refused with the room's code and still checked, the note focused), `test/plugins/surface.test.js`,
`test/plugins/errors.test.js`, `test/plugins/install.test.js`, `test/studio/catalog.test.js` (the forced install's
`_replaced/` unchanged), `node tools/i18n.mjs check --all --strict`.
Golden: unchanged (the studio and its page only; the sim is untouched).

### 32.14.35 Starting with the script sandbox on: `--scripts`, a double click on Windows

Seen: the script sandbox (§32.11, P3b) is switched on only by the environment variable `SP_PLUGIN_SCRIPTS=sandbox`. On
Windows that means `set` in a command prompt before `start-windows.bat`, `$env:` in PowerShell, or `setx` — a player
who double-clicks the start script has no place to put it (the maintainer's question of 2026-10-15).
Now: `scripts/launch.mjs` takes `--scripts off | sandbox | trusted | trusted-force` and passes it to the server it
starts as `SP_PLUGIN_SCRIPTS` (`off` removes an inherited value; any other word stops with exit 2 and the list).
`scripts/start.sh`, `start-windows.bat` and `start-windows.ps1` already pass their arguments on. The new
`scripts/start-windows-sandbox.bat` is `start-windows.bat --scripts sandbox` for a double click and ships in the
release zips (`tools/package.mjs` PLAYER_SCRIPTS). The Windows bundle (`scripts/make-windows-bundle.mjs`) gains
`启动游戏-插件沙箱.bat`, its `启动游戏.bat` with `--scripts sandbox`. When the server already answers on the port, the
launcher opens the browser as before and says that `--scripts` does not change the running server. [ASSUMED] the flag
name. Nothing else changes: the sandbox and its gates are §32.11's, the trusted modes §32.10's. Tests:
`test/launch-scripts.test.js` (the flag, `off`, the environment, the `.bat`), `test/package.test.js` (shipped).
Golden: unchanged.

### 32.14.36 Where official shots and weapon effects leave a unit (the maintainer's report of 2026-10-15)

Seen (the maintainer, playing 0.3.0 with 官方特效 on): 灰毫's shell (char_431_ashlok, 要塞) 「不是从武器里发射的，而是凭空出现一个
很奇怪的弧线」, and 安洁莉娜's S2 effect 「应该从法杖位置发出」; check 号角 (char_4039_horn, the other 要塞), her normal attack,
S1 and S3, and every operator whose attacks or skills fire projectiles, beams or chains.

Official (the local Windows client, read 2026-10-15: its prefabs with UnityPy, its skeletons with @pixi-spine/runtime-3.8,
its native code — GameAssembly.dll mapped to method names through the il2cpp metadata as in research 12 and
disassembled; the notes, the roster audit, the disassembly excerpts and the scripts are kept with the maintainer's
local handoff notes, `handoff/wip-r46/origins/`):
- **Mount points follow bones.** An operator skin's Muzzle / MuzzlePoint (125 of the 126 operators that have one) and
  every Special point (276) carry a spine-unity BoneFollower (`F_Muzzle`, `B_Muzzle`, `F_Weapon` …). The transform the
  extractor composes (§33.12 Skin `pos`) is that bone in the prefab's edit-time pose, the 0-length `Default` clip:
  灰毫's Muzzle sits at local (3.359, 0.159) under Front = F_Muzzle (335.9, 15.9) × the SkeletonDataAsset scale 0.01,
  under FaceSwitcher (0, −0.16, −0.1) × 0.27 — her cannon lowered, 16 skeleton units above her feet. In battle the
  client reads the point's live transform: `MountPoint.get_mapPositionV3` = `MapUtil.WorldToMapPosV3(transform.position)`
  (or `SpineAnimator.GetBakeMountPoint`, the same bone baked per frame), and `BasicMovement.OnInit` takes a projectile's
  origin from the start mount point's map position — the muzzle where the attack clip holds it when it fires (its
  OnAttack event). Measured: 灰毫's F_Muzzle at Attack01's OnAttack (0.533 s) is at (312.7, 195.2), 180 units (0.56 tile
  as drawn) above the static point; 安洁莉娜's static MuzzlePoint is 13 units up, her S2 attack clip (Skill2_Loop) holds the
  staff's tip at (110.7, 423.8) — over her head — and her S1 clip at (26.6, 519.9); 溯光星源's static Muzzle is her Idle
  parking spot 907 units (2.45 tiles) to her right, her Attack holds it behind her drawing hand (−169.8, 340.7). Of the
  roster's 354 attack and skill-mode abilities with a projectile or cast rows (116 operators), 226 leave from a
  bone-followed point; at the strike frame 141 of them (55 operators) are more than 0.1 tile from the static point and
  98 (40 operators) more than 0.3 tile (in the client's 0.0027 tile per skeleton unit).
- **Effects take the mount point's rotation.** `Effect._InitLocationFromEntity`: FOOT_POINT, HIT_POINT, HEAD_POINT and
  MUZZLE_POINT_WITHOUT_ROTATION take the point's position and the unit's own (unturned) rotation — their prefabs are
  pre-tilted +30° about X (§33.2) —; MUZZLE_POINT and the MP_SPECIAL points (`_SetPosAndRotToMountPoint`) take its
  position and its world rotation: the Graphic's −30° about X, the FaceSwitcher's turn for a unit facing left (180°
  about the skeleton plane's up axis — `FaceSwitcher._DoSwitchFaceInternal` tweens that local rotation) and a
  BoneFollower's bone angle. `FollowMountPoint._FollowMountPoint` sets the position every LateUpdate and the rotation
  unless `_keepOriginRotation` (the sign of the point's scale with `_followScaleSign`, its scale with `_followScale`).
  The status loops keep theirs (buff_stun, buff_frozen, common_root_01: `_keepOriginRotation` 1); the weapon effects
  take it and are authored along the bone, unturned about X: 安洁莉娜's aglina_skill_02_start / aglina_attack_01_start
  / aglina_skill_03_start follow SPECIAL_0 (F_Weapon, her staff) with their parts 0.33–0.6 along local +x — up the staff
  —, 灰毫's ashlok_attack_01_start spawns at MUZZLE_POINT, 号角's horn_attack_01_start_02 follows MUZZLE. The roster's
  effects: 296 follow a point with its rotation, 36 more spawn with it.
- **ParacurveMovement** (OnInit, OnTick, _CalculateNextPosition): the shell crosses the ground at `_speed` (5 for 灰毫,
  号角) homing on the target's point, and climbs ballistically: the raise h = `_raiseHeight` (`_useRandomHeight`: a value
  in `_randomHeightRange`), 0 when the ground distance is not above `_noRaiseHeightThreshold` (1.3); H = max(0, start
  height + h − end height); T = distance / speed; gravity g = 2(√h + √H)²/T² and the initial climb √(2gh): it tops out h
  above the muzzle; after the top g is re-solved each tick to land on the target's current height; `_inverseParacurve`
  (`_randomInverseParacurve`: a coin) flies the mirror image. The full set's 64 Paracurve projectiles, 25 of the
  roster's attacks: 灰毫 1, 号角 0.7, 缪尔赛思, 纯烬艾雅法拉, 流明 0.23–0.5, the generic arrow (流星) and 赫默's heal 0.3 ….
- **AmiyaS2Movement** (安洁莉娜 S2's projectile_aglina_s2): straight and homing in `_time` 0.2 s (after a start phase of
  `_startDuration` towards `_startDistance`, both 0 here), its body at (0, sin φ · `_yAnimateOffset` 0.1, 0) across the
  flight, φ advancing 2π · `_yAnimateSpeed` (5) per second from a random start.
- **The flying effect** is the projectile behaviour's `_mainEffect` (SimpleProjectile, HarpoonProjectile,
  LassoProjectile …). A ChainLightningEffectBehaviour's is the lightning between the chain's links, a HarpoonRenderer's
  the rope from the shooter's point to the head: LineRenderers whose points the game sets, authored in world space
  (leizi_attack_02_line at (2.8, 4, 8)–(3.6, 4, 8)). The extractor's `main` keeps the last `_mainEffect` of an entry's
  behaviours: 32 projectiles of the full set name a line there (惊蛰, 异客, 莎草, 歌蕾蒂娅, 溯光星源 S1 / S2, Mon3tr …).

Cause: (1) `mounts.js` put every point at the skin's static `pos` — for a bone-followed point the edit-time pose, and
in the client's frame while the field view draws the skeleton at `UNIT.modelScale` (1/320 tile per unit) from the feet,
against the client's 0.0027 from FaceSwitcher's −0.16 (号角's F_Muzzle does not move in her clips, and still drew 0.3
tile above where her shell left); (2) `playOn` honoured FollowMountPoint only for held loops, and no mount rotation at
all: an effect with spawn NONE (安洁莉娜's S2 glow, 号角's muzzle flash) sat at the feet, the weapon effects lay flat and
unturned; (3) the flight lobbed a Paracurve shot raiseHeight × distance × 2 over the line — 6 tiles for 灰毫 at 3 tiles,
1.8 for an arrow — from the static point under her feet: the arc out of nowhere; (4) the flight flew index `main`,
chain lines and harpoon ropes as projectile heads, drawn at their world-space points. Found on the way: 灰毫's S2 modes
are S2(U) / S2(D) / S2(LR) (her skill names no mode, and no plain S2 exists): her S2 attacks played her normal attack;
号角's S2 BurstAttackGroup took its BlockedRangedAttack — the first ranged sub-ability, whose projectile flies nothing —
for every shot.

Now:
- `render/units.js` `UnitView.boneOffset(names, out, strike)`: a bone where the model draws it — `UNIT.modelScale` ×
  the enemy factors, the draw sign (`modelFlip`) —, as tiles right of and up from the feet, with its angle in the
  skeleton; `strike`: posed by the attack clip at its strike frame (`SpineActor.strikePose`, its first hit) on a scratch
  skeleton of the same data (`bonePose`), so a shot leaves where the clip fires whatever frame the model was last posed
  in (an impostor's refresh, 安洁莉娜's staff swinging 0.3 tile a frame); a clip started since the last pose (an attack
  nobody wound up) is read at its own pose, not the cross-fade's.
- `render/vfx/mounts.js`: a skin point with a `bone` is that bone (the facing's name first, then the other skeleton's),
  its `up` along the plane the camera sees face-on (the camera's tilt, 30°); without a model or the bone, `pos` as
  before. `mountRotation`: `graphic.rot` · (facing left: 180° about Y) · Rz(the bone's angle). `spawnRotationKind`: the
  spawn locations that take it.
- `render/vfx/effect.js`: `place.mountRot`, the root's rotation.
- `render/vfx/wiring.js`: `playOn` places every effect that has a FollowMountPoint at its point from the first frame
  and follows it (its rotation too unless `_keepOriginRotation`; FollowOwner / FollowOwnerRootTileHeight /
  FollowTileHeight effects as before: at their spawn location, kept on the unit as held loops), turns MUZZLE_POINT /
  Special-point spawns, and adds no FLIP_TWO_SIDE flip on an effect that took the rotation; the strike's cast rows
  (the skills' too: `skillfx.js`) and a shot read the strike frame; `_fly` starts at that point, flies the projectile
  behaviour's effect (`projectileParts` — one reader with §32.14.38's, which also gives a timer's life), lobs along
  `paracurve` / wobbles by `wobble` (`render/vfx/flight.js`, new), and stretches a HarpoonRenderer's rope from the
  shooter's point to the head (`effect.js linkLines`, §32.14.38); `pickAttacks` takes a `Blocked…`
  sub-ability only while the unit blocks (UF.BLOCKED), first then; `modeVariant` picks a mode's facing variant (DOWN:
  [Down] / [D] / (D), UP: (U), the sides: (LR)) — for a skill whose plan names no mode, `S<n>`'s variants.
- Before / after, frozen frames of a battle against hard-normal-r13's wave with the path the runtime flew (yellow), the
  shot's start (green) and the skin's static Muzzle (red), in the handoff folder above: 灰毫's shell from the static
  point under her feet with an arc off the top of the view → from her barrel with a one-tile arc; 号角 likewise; 安洁莉娜:
  normal, S1, S2 and S3 shots from the staff, the S2 glow at the staff's head instead of lying at her hand; 溯光星源 from
  her drawing hand instead of 2.45 tiles to her right; 歌蕾蒂娅's harpoon with its rope, 惊蛰's trail instead of a line;
  隐现, 能天使, 新约能天使, 深靛, 远牙, 至简 from their weapons.

[ASSUMED]:
- Bone-followed points in the drawn model's frame (`UNIT.modelScale` from the feet), so a shot leaves the weapon as it
  is drawn; the static points (HitPoint, HeadPoint) keep the client's offsets — hits and status loops sit lower on the
  remake's larger model than in the client (an open point).
- The strike frame is the clip's first OnAttack time (the manifest's hits); a follow-up shot (`triggerDelta` > 0) and a
  rope's end on the shooter read the pose drawn then.
- A point no bone moves has no rotation of its own (the skins do not export the points' local rotations; HitPoint and
  HeadPoint read as the identity); FLIP_TWO_SIDE adds no flip to an effect that takes the mount's rotation.
- The AmiyaS2 wobble runs across the flight in the plane the camera sees; a random height or coin is seeded by the unit
  and its attack count; an end above the arc's top is reached by a linear share; the sim's flight time stays (the arc's
  shape does not depend on it).
- Facing variants by their names; a `Blocked…` node is the blocking unit's sub-ability (the configs' triggers are not
  exported); a harpoon's rope ends with the flight (the client's holds while it pulls).
- Not done: the lightning of a chain's bounces (the sim's 'chain' events stay procedural); the procedural shots (官方特效
  off) keep their own launch point.

Tests: `test/render/vfx-origins.test.js` (new: a bone-followed point where the model draws its bone — the facing's name
first, the strike flag, the camera's tilt — and the static points; the mount rotation with the tilt, the facing and the
bone's angle; the spawn locations that turn; the projectile behaviour's effect against a chain line or a rope; the
Paracurve arc — `raiseHeight` above the start, none within the threshold, the inverse sag, a moved end, a seeded random
height —; the AmiyaS2 wobble; through the wiring: a shot from the strike-frame muzzle lobbing one tile and its MUZZLE_POINT
flash turned along the barrel, a chain caster's trail, a harpoon's rope between the muzzle and the head, a staff glow
on its bone laid along it and following it, a status loop keeping its rotation, FLIP_TWO_SIDE on a turning mount, 号角's
Blocked sub-ability, 灰毫's S2(LR); `UnitView.boneOffset` on a stand-in skeleton — as drawn, at the strike frame from a
scratch skeleton, a just-started clip, mirrored —; with the local art and extraction, 灰毫's and 安洁莉娜's static Muzzle
equal to the Default pose and the strike frame's bone far above it), `test/render/vfx-origins.browser.test.js` (new,
`RENDER_E2E=1`, a real battle: 灰毫's shell from 0.4–1 tile above her feet with an arc of 0.25–0.9 tile on screen, 安洁莉娜's
S2 orbs from over her head and her S2 glow on the staff pointing up it, 惊蛰's trail and never her line, a clean console;
it fails on the code before this change; frames `test/e2e/out/vfx-origin-*.png`), `test/render/vfx*.test.js` and the
browser suites `vfx.browser.test.js`, `vfx-skills.browser.test.js` unchanged (but for the chain line, §32.14.38).
Golden: unchanged (client only).

### 32.14.37 The 驭械术士's floating units (the maintainer's report of 2026-10-15)

Seen: 「荒芜拉普兰德的浮游单元没做，还是用的光柱，其它驭械术士分支的干员也都排查一下，他们的浮游单元都不一样」. With 设置「官方特效」 on,
荒芜拉普兰德 had no wolf at all: her normal attack flew her own shot only, and the kit fx of her S3 — 'drones' at the release,
'drone' at every unit's hit and once a second at each free unit, 'droneLock' as one picks an enemy — drew the
procedural 'summon' archetype, a cyan light pillar with a hex ring, and a reticle (`render/fx/kinds.js`). 洛洛 S2's and
耶拉 S2's 'drone' fx drew the same pillar on the enemy their units hit, and so did 澄闪's skills (a 自选 驭械术师). Their units
