were not drawn either: the home loops TalentFx plays for the passive general abilities (`rockr_token_01`,
`kjera_token_default`, `malist_drone`, `gdglow_token_01`) start in the battle's first frames, before their prefabs
have loaded, and `wiring.js playOn` placed a prefab it had not loaded at the HitPoint without following — inside the
model, for good; the funnel shots of their normal attacks (general MultiFunnelNormalAttack abilities, cast at the
owner's ON_ABILITY_CAST_ON_TARGET) and the skills' units at their targets had no reader; FunnelHitEffect was among the
behaviours not run (§32.14.25).

Official (the local client's `charpack/char_1038_whitw2.ab`, `char_4040_rockr`, `char_4054_malist`, `char_4013_kjera`,
their skill prefabs and `[uc]projectiles.ab`, read with UnityPy on 2026-10-15; DESIGN §33.18 has the structure and a
table of the five): the floating units are no unit of their own and no part of the Spine models (洛洛's drone shows in
her skeleton only while `Skill_Begin` launches it). Each is an effect prefab that an attack ability shows at a Special
point of the skin — a BoneFollower point on F_Funnel_Muzzle_1–3 (荒芜拉普兰德's wolf heads), F_UVA_01 (洛洛's drone), F_Fly
(至简's drone), F_Rock_1 / F_Rock_2 (耶拉's ice tokens) —, and its attack is a projectile. 荒芜拉普兰德's normal attack
(MultiFunnelsNormalAttack) is her own shot plus up to three units (1, + S1's passive, + the 头狼 talent's third stage),
each a FunnelEffectEmitter (idle `whitw2_token_0X`, appear / disappear `whitw2_token_end_0X`) whose projectile attaches to
the target and plays `whitw2_token_hit_0X` — the wolf lunging through the enemy —; S1 the same units on the far target,
S2 six units with no home loop, one per lock, S3 four dispersed flights (`whitw2_skill_03_token_trail_01`: spread from
her, chase at 2 → 4, latch on and attack, the red wolf-eye trace mark `whitw2_skill_03_mark_01` and the latched mark
`_02`). 洛洛, 至简 and 耶拉 attack with their own shot and a funnel shot from the unit (`rockr_token_trail_01`,
`malist_drone_trail`, `kjera_token_attack_trail_01` with `kjera_token_attack_start_01` on the token); their skills
release the units: 洛洛 S2's drone beside its target, left then right (`rockr_token_skill_02_hit_L_01` / `_R_01`, the
overload's own groups and home loop), 耶拉 S2's two tokens (their own left / right groups), 至简 S2 a funnel shot of its
own from SPECIAL_2. The Special points are the bones in the prefab's edit-time pose — for these, the Idle clip's first
frame — (× 0.0027 Unity units per skeleton unit), and the units move with the bones (洛洛's, 至简's and 耶拉's bob with
the idle).

Now: `render/vfx/funnels.js` reads each unit's floating units from index.json (version 3; no new field and no new art:
the full set already holds all 98 prefabs of the four, 5.4 MB of textures) and `render/vfx/funnelfx.js` (owned by the
wiring) plays them on the events the field view already gets — the home loops on their points following the bone
where the model draws it (merged with §32.14.36: its FollowMountPoint path, `mounts.js` / `UnitView.boneOffset`, with
the point's rotation; this round's own `bones.js`, the bone's offset from its Idle place × 0.0027 added to the skin's
point, went in the merge), the attached units leaving with the owner's 'atk' and landing with its 'dmg' after the body hit,
the funnel shots from the units' points, the remote units' hits on the kits' 'drone', 荒芜拉普兰德 S3's flights from
'drones' / 'droneLock' / 'drone' with the sim kit's speeds —, and the kit fx they stand for ('drone', 'drones',
'droneLock', 'overload' of a funnel unit) are no longer drawn procedurally while its effects are there (DESIGN §33.18,
§9 "Official battle effects"). TalentFx leaves the passive looks to it. `playOn` places a prefab it has not loaded by
index.json's summary (`prefabs[key].spawn`, and its `follow` point from the first frame, as §32.14.36 places every
FollowMountPoint) and takes up its rotation, direction and pause behaviours once it loads — for every effect, not only
the funnels' (an idle loop of any unit started in the first frames sat at its HitPoint).
Without the effects (设置「官方特效」 关, the 2D board, a lite install, the Phase 1a sample) or without a unit's prefabs, the
procedural look stays, the light pillar included. The sim and its events are unchanged.

[ASSUMED]: the units' hits at the sim's damage moment (AttachToTarget's 0.5 s delay is not added); an attached unit is
home again 0.85 s after its hit (the lunge clip's length) and a remote one 2 s after its last; the appear / disappear at
a skill's start and end; before the first attack the home count is 1 + the equipped skill's passive "浮游单元+N" (its
text), then the hits the attacks show; units of one skill in step with the same groups (澄闪's) start one group apart; S3's
flight height (0.3 over the ground), its pulse correction and its hover (the sim kit's 0.9 radius — the client's
HoveringInPlaceMovement says 1.25, an open point for the sim); the units on their bones as the remake's model draws
them, with their point's rotation (§32.14.36: FollowMountPoint without `_keepOriginRotation`) — checked after the merge
in a real battle: 荒芜拉普兰德's wolves, whose points follow F_Funnel_Muzzle_1–3's 87°, now face forward (this round's own
frames show them pointing down), 至简's and 澄闪's drones and 耶拉's tokens as the points turn them; 洛洛's drone, which
the merge stood upright (turned by F_UVA_01's 90°), lies flat again: her SpecialPoint1's BoneFollower does not follow
the bone's rotation (`followBoneRotation` 0 in chararts/char_4040_rockr.ab) and the point keeps its own 30° about X,
cancelling the Graphic's tilt, as 令's SpecialPoint2 does — index.json's skins now carry each point's local rotation
`rot` and its BoneFollower's `followRot` (fx.py `skin_points`), `mounts.js mountRotation` keeps a non-following point's
own rotation (50 more points of 31 units keep the identity or a small Z turn instead of their bone's angle; an index
without the fields reads as before; DESIGN §33.12 Skin, §33.16 Placement). Not drawn: 澄闪's self-destruct (the kit's
'explode' keeps the procedural blast) and a unit hidden while its owner is stunned.

Tests: `test/render/vfx-funnels.test.js` (a synthetic version-3 index shaped like the client's: the plan of attached,
remote and dispersed units, funnel shots, a skill's own shot and the passive looks; home loops on their points with the
point's rotation, the S1 passive's count, the passive look drawn once; an attached attack — leave, the lunges after the body
hit, the count growing, the return —; several targets of one attack; funnel shots from their mounts with the cast on the
wind-up; remote hits in turn, the overload, the return and the skill's end; two remote units and the added unit's
appear; the dispersed units — release, lock, latch, hit, a lost target, the end, a release without its fx —; nothing
official and the procedural kit fx without the layer or the prefabs; a late prefab placed by its summary — on its
FollowMountPoint from the first frame, its rotation once loaded —; a home loop on the drawn bone, followed and
mirrored), `test/render/vfx-origins.test.js` (a point whose BoneFollower does not follow the bone's rotation keeps its
own — 洛洛's drone flat facing right and left, any bone angle —; an index without `rot` / `followRot` reads as before),
`test/render/vfx-funnels.browser.test.js` (`RENDER_E2E=1`, the real units in the dev viewer: 荒芜拉普兰德's wolf
at home and its lunge, S3's flights, marks and hit, 洛洛's drone, shot and S2 hits with the overload, 耶拉's tokens, 至简's
drone and shot, 澄闪 S3, the light tier; pictures in test/e2e/out/vfx-funnel-*.png). Golden: unchanged (client only).

### 32.14.38 Official skill effects after the maintainer's play of 2026-10-15: 溯光星源 S3, 铃兰 S3, 野鬃 S2 (round 46)

Seen (the maintainer, playing 0.3.0 with 设置「官方特效」 on): 溯光星源 S3 「3技能特效角度不对」, with an official recording of her S3 next
to 仇白's and another operator's; 铃兰 S3 「会有一条奇怪的光条」 — a long bright strip across the board in his screenshot; 野鬃 S2
「期间周身有奇怪的锥体特效」. Three shared causes in the runtime and two in the extractor, each found on one of them and then audited
across every effect of the set.

(1) 溯光星源 S3 — a projectile's effects on its target. Official: her S3 attack (mode `S3`) casts `halo2_skill_03_start_01`
at ON_CAST_START (FxDelay 0.45 s: her shot rising straight up the screen) and fires `projectile_chr_halo2_s3`, a timer
— a SimpleProjectile with no flying effect and a LIMITED life of 0.517 s — whose EffectBehaviour puts
`halo2_skill_03_trail_01` on the target as it starts (`_effectsToTargetWhenStart`): that prefab's `trail` node runs by
its clip from (−2.82, 7.13, −0.29) to (0, 0.167, 0) in 0.517 s, two TrailRenderers behind it — the lightning falls on
the target from the upper left (FLIP_TWO_SIDE: from the upper right for a shot from the right) —, and the hit
`halo2_skill_03_hit_01` comes at the end of the projectile's life (`_alwaysHitTraceTargetInTheEnd`). The recording
shows exactly that (8.1–8.3 s and 9.2–9.3 s: the bolt some 20–25° off the vertical onto each of the two locked
enemies). The runtime never read index.json's `toTargetOnStart` / `toTileOnStart`: what was left of her S3 was the shot
going straight up and the hit at the sim's flight time. Also missing: the S3 mode's attach row
`halo2_skill_03_range_01` (her y-4 area, FOUR_DIRECTION by her deploy direction) — the runtime played no attach row of
a mode —, and her bubble's back half (order −2, cause 3) was drawn over her. Now (`render/vfx/wiring.js` `_fly`,
`_startOnTarget`): a projectile's `toTargetOnStart` plays on its target as it starts — turned by the side the shot
comes from, kept on the target while it lives — and `toTileOnStart` on the target's tile; a projectile without a flying
effect holds its hit for its life when that is longer than the sim's flight (`TIMER_HIT_MAX` 1.5 s), the target's
damage meanwhile drawing no procedural spark. 33 projectiles carry `toTargetOnStart` and 6 `toTileOnStart`: 14 roster
operators (德克萨斯 and 缄默德克萨斯's sword rain, 迷迭香's every attack, 银灰, 蕾缪安, 洛洛, W, 澄闪, 琴柳, 司霆惊蛰, 提丰, 望, 机械师, 溯光星源). The skill
plan takes the attach rows of the mode a skill switches to (`render/vfx/skills.js` `attachUses`: held for the skill,
`range` for a `*_range*` key, else `loop`; a preview-dummy row left out): 8 operator modes (溯光星源 S3, 薄绿 S1, 普罗旺斯 S2,
百炼嘉维尔 S1 / S3, 推进之王 S3, 酒神 S3, 司霆惊蛰 S3).

(2) 铃兰 S3 — skinned meshes. Official: `lisa_skill_03_buff_02` holds her nine fox tails, SkinnedMeshRenderers on bone
chains animated by a legacy clip. The client's meshes store 1 or 2 bone influences per vertex (a Unity 2019+ mesh keeps
the BlendIndices / BlendWeight channels' own dimension and drops BlendWeight when every vertex follows one bone).
`tools/local-extract/fx.py` wrote the rows as UnityPy returned them: the ninth tail (`Plane011`) with 2 per vertex, the
other eight with no skin at all (it wrote bone data only with weights). The runtime read four per vertex: the ninth
tail's vertices took other vertices' bones and weights — weights summing to 2 doubled their position, missing ones
dropped them to the world origin —, a strip from her across the board; the other eight were never drawn. In the used
set 2 of the 64 skinned renderers (20 prefabs) were right: 21 of 12 prefabs wrote 2 per vertex (琴柳's flags in S2 and
S3, 森蚺 S2, 煌 S3, 艾雅法拉 S2, 歌蕾蒂娅 S2, 早露 S3, 斯卡蒂 S3, 铃兰's ninth tail), 41 of 9 prefabs none (伊芙利特 S3's flame tongues, 凯尔希
S1's 20 pieces, 水月 S2, 浊心斯卡蒂 S3, 星熊 S3, 斯卡蒂 S3, eight of 铃兰's tails) — 12 roster operators. Now: `fx.py skin_rows`
writes four (index, weight) pairs per vertex — weight 1 for a one-bone mesh — with the bind poses (DESIGN §33.9,
unchanged: it always said four); `render/vfx/geom.js skinStride` reads the influences the file has (an earlier
extraction's 2 per vertex draws right; a mesh without bone indices still draws nothing until it is extracted again).
The local extraction was updated the way `fx.py --fx-keys` writes it (a staged run of the 20 prefabs, 2026-10-15): 46
new mesh files (the skinned meshes under their new keys; the old files stay), the 19 descriptors that name them
rewritten (only their mesh keys change), `index.json` and `textures.json` untouched (a staged index's summaries were
identical); a checkout's `data/local-fx.json` lists the new meshes after it is copied from an updated one or the set is
extracted again.

(3) 野鬃 S2 — renderers that sort before the units. Official: `wildmn_skill_02_buff`'s `huan` is a half cone (Cone002)
flattened by its zero y scale (as Unity draws it) into a fan of lance streaks behind her, at sorting order −3 (`andi`
−4, `wenli` −2, `suipian` −1; the speed lines and the glow +2). The units' Spine renderers (her chararts: Front, Back,
Spine) sit on the Default sorting layer at order 0, and Unity draws transparent renderers by sorting layer, then order,
then distance: a negative order on that layer is drawn before every unit, behind her. The runtime drew every renderer
that does not lie flat near the ground over the units: the white lances covered her body. Now (`render/vfx/effect.js`
`behindUnits`): a renderer on a lower sorting layer, or on the Default layer with a negative order, draws in the ground
pass, under the units; order 0 keeps the flat-near-the-ground rule, a positive order stays over them. 5,637 of the
set's 34,061 enabled renderers (in 1,916 of 4,518 prefabs) sort before the units — auras, under-glows and the back
halves of bubbles (溯光星源 S3's) among them.

The audit (every effect of the set, the shared causes above and the classes the reports named): projectiles — 30 of the
541 had `main` naming their link line (a ChainLightningEffectBehaviour's chain or a HarpoonRenderer's rope: fx.py took
the last behaviour's `_mainEffect`, and its `lifeTime`), so the flying effect was lost and the line flown as the shot;
`projectileParts` (`render/vfx/flight.js` since the merge with §32.14.36, which found the same cause) reads the
projectile class's own `_mainEffect` and life from the behaviours index.json lists, the extractor writes them (and
`link`) from now on, and of the 29 link lines (异客, 惊蛰, Mon3tr, 歌蕾蒂娅, 崖心, 莎草, 伊内丝, 早露, 溯光星源 S1 …) a harpoon's rope
(14) runs from the shooter's point to the head while it flies, then fades. A chain's lightning (15) is not flown: this
round first stretched it from the muzzle to the shot too, but the client's code, read when the round's branches were
merged, draws it between the chain's targets as the shot reaches them — ChainLightningEffectBehaviour has no tick of
its own, ChainLightningHitBehaviour calls its PlayLineEffect from OnProjectileReached and DoSelectTargetToHit, and
all 17 chain behaviours of the set have `_linkSource` 0 (no line from the shooter) —: the sim's 'chain' bounces, which
stay procedural. LineRenderers — 32 of the 34 line
prefabs are world-space with the editor's placeholder points (fixed spots of the authoring scene: 溯光星源 S1's chain at
world (5, 4) → (6, 3)); `effect.js` draws such a line only once the game set its end points (`linkLines`), so a skill
loop or range outline whose end points no signal gives (`aphris_skill_02_line_01`, `aglna2_skill_03_range_line_01`,
`enemy_acloon_skill_loop_1_line`) draws nothing instead of a strip; node-space lines (`lmlee_skill_03_range`) as
before. Zero scales — 835 nodes have a zero scale axis (flattened, as Unity draws them; 野鬃's fan is one), and a
headless pass over every prefab (each played at the origin, its draw geometry measured) found 珊比's attack sparks 24,000
tiles away after a second: under a parent scaled (1.16, 1.16, 0) the Local scaling mode's frame had an empty axis, its
inverse fell back to the identity and a world-space system's limit-velocity round trip multiplied the velocity every
step; `effect.js` `basisOf` now rebuilds an empty column from the other two (Unity's Local mode ignores the parents'
scale) and `psys.js` inverts a singular frame with unit axes (262 systems of 24 prefabs under a zero-scaled parent:
左乐's slashes, 珊比, 新约能天使's trail, 机械师 S3, 缇缇 S3); the pass's remaining far geometry is authored so (a flash 48 tiles off
in `mcmstr_skill_01_hit`) or the CollisionModule's (缄默德克萨斯 S3's swords fall through the ground plane the prefab names —
open, below). Checked and unchanged: mesh particles (the alignment frames, the 3D size, the Euler rotation — the
billboard roll's sign stays §33.5's assumption, and nothing in the recording shows a mesh particle's); stretched
billboards (5,573: the 2,075 with a negative length scale extend ahead of the particle as Unity's do); TrailRenderer
alignment; a range or directional effect's turn — FOUR_DIRECTION with `_useBodyRotation` follows the deploy direction
(溯光星源 S3's area rendered for UP / RIGHT / DOWN / LEFT), FLIP_TWO_SIDE mirrors by the side.

[ASSUMED]: a `toTargetOnStart` effect is turned by the side the shot comes from (the recording: from the upper left for
a shot from the left) and kept on its target; a timer projectile's hit waits for its life up to 1.5 s while the sim
deals the damage at its own flight time; a harpoon's rope fades once the head lands (the client's holds while it pulls); a
renderer at order 0 on the Default layer keeps the flat rule (Unity sorts it with the units by distance); a world-space
line without end points draws nothing; the attach rows of a skill's mode last the skill. Open: the 4 enemy attach rows
(Default / combat modes: held from the spawn would need the talents' mode bindings); 溯光星源 S3's link between her two
locked enemies (`halo2_skill_03_line_01`, LockTargetAbility) and its buff on them — no client signal names the pair
(her muzzle point, the skin's `F_Muzzle` 2.45 tiles ahead in its setup pose, was §32.14.36's: the drawn bone at the
strike frame); a stretched
billboard's pivot (2,506 renderers) and a freeform stretched one's rotation (20) are not applied; `Thorn2S3Renderer`'s
polygon; the CollisionModule (68 systems, every one colliding with planes the prefab names: debris, shell casings,
缄默德克萨斯 S3's swords) is still ignored, so those particles fall through the ground. Tests:
`test/render/vfx-skill-fixes.test.js` (new: skinning with 4, 2 and 1 influences per vertex and without bone indices;
the ground pass by sorting layer and order; world-space lines before and after `linkLines`, a node-space line;
`projectileParts` for a chain, a harpoon, a timer and an index of the fixed extractor; a timer projectile's lightning
on its target, its tile effect, its hit at the end of its life with the damage covered, mirrored from the right; a
chain's trail without its line, a harpoon's rope from the muzzle to the head, fading once it lands; `attachUses` and a plan's attach row; `basisOf` / `completeAxes` and a world-space system with limit
velocity under a zero-scaled parent), `test/render/vfx-skills.browser.test.js` (+5, RENDER_E2E=1: 溯光星源 S3's bolt
above-left of the dummy, its range and its hit at the end; 铃兰 S3's tails and no strip behind her; 野鬃 S2's `huan` in the
ground pass, its speed lines over; 异客's trail, never his chain line in flight; 歌蕾蒂娅 S2's rope from her to the
harpoon), `test/local-extract-fx.test.js`
(`skin_rows`; an extraction's skinned meshes have four influences per vertex and bind poses; an index written by the
fixed extractor names `main` and `link` as the behaviours do). Golden: unchanged (client and extractor only).

### 32.14.39 导入素材包 beside 下载完整素材, and an example asset pack (the maintainer's request of 2026-10-15)

Seen (the maintainer): the import of a local pack sat inside the 下载完整素材 dialog (§32.14.23), and that dialog starts the
download as it opens — 「要是要点到下载完整素材界面那就已经浪费服务器流量了」. The maintainer also asked for a packaged asset
pack of this version as an example, neither committed nor attached to the GitHub release: they upload it to a cloud
drive.

Now — the title screen: under the login box, two buttons side by side, each with a hint line: 下载完整素材 (unchanged: its
dialog starts the queue, closing it pauses; 「含模型、贴图和音频，建议使用 Wi-Fi 下载。」) and 导入素材包
(「读取自己的素材包，美术不占服务器流量。」; on a page that cannot import, 「导入需要 HTTPS 或 localhost。」), which opens its own
dialog (`public/js/ui/assetImport.js`): the folder (`showDirectoryPicker`, else `webkitdirectory`) and zip pickers, drag and drop,
cancel, the progress and the counts, 清除已导入的素材 after a confirm, the persist note, the HTTPS / unsupported-browser
explanation. Opening it sends no request — no art, no manifest — and leaves the download queue idle; the importer reads
the three manifests only once an import starts, as before. The download dialog has no import controls any more; an
imported file still counts as ready there and is not requested. A label too long for its half wraps inside its button
(a larger 文字大小); at 1280×900 and 844×390 both buttons stay in view and their hints above the footer. The two hidden
file inputs leave the tab order (`tabindex="-1"`), so the dialog's focus trap moves between the visible controls.

The example pack: `node tools/package.mjs --assets [--out <dir>] [--force]` (and `--dry-run [--list]`, `--keep-stage`,
`--allow-dirty`, `--allow-dev`) writes `Stronghold-Protocol-v<version>-assets.zip`, one folder of that name holding
`public/assets/…`, `public/fonts/…` and `README.txt`. The selection is the importer's own plan (`importableArt`:
`assetPack.js` planFromManifests / mergeLocalManifests over the manifests as `server/http/assetViews.js` serves them, every
URL with a stand-in hash — a host with every listed file): the files data/assets.json, data/local-assets.json and
data/local-fx.json list, whole Spine models (the default `<skel>.png` page counted), with the full zip's voice and effect
switches. Not in it: the manifests (the browser reads the host's), code, and the 85 files under public/assets/local
that the full zip still ships but no manifest lists (65.9 MB, PNG copies of pages now served as WebP: `artPlan` takes the
whole local tree), which an import would skip. Every path holds one `public/`, so `detectAssetPrefix` picks the strip
strategy for the whole pack, and the README is the one file it skips (「跳过 1 个」, as the README says). The README (Chinese,
then English; UTF-8 with a BOM) says what the pack is (not an installation package), the version and the three manifests
it matches — on a server of another version a changed file fails the hash check and a file that version does not list
is skipped, and the game loads those from the server —, how to import it (HTTPS or localhost; title screen → 导入素材包 →
选择 zip, or 选择文件夹 for the unzipped folder), and the notice (non-commercial; NOTICE.md at the release tag). The full
zip's checks run: every listed file on disk, no case clash, the personal-info scan of every file (art included),
data/assets.json committed (`--allow-dirty` skips it), a development version only with `--allow-dev`, the stage holding
exactly the plan and the README. No `npm ci`, no MANIFEST.json. `zipFolder` takes the folder to zip (default
`Stronghold-Protocol`): the full, lite and update zips are unchanged, and the release keeps its three attachments. The
0.3.0 pack: 30,936 files, 990.4 MB, a 711.1 MB zip (745,659,750 bytes), built in about a minute.

Found by importing that pack through the dialog in headless Chrome on 127.0.0.1 — each fixed, since a whole pack is
what the example invites:
1. Chrome refuses `Cache.keys()` for a cache of more than about 15,000 entries (「AbortError: … Operation too large」); a
   whole pack stores 30,936 files and 12,064 `/media/` aliases. `listImportedPaths` took the error for an empty cache:
   the dialog said 已缓存 0 个文件 and 下载完整素材 requested every imported file again. Now each import adds the pathnames it
   stored to one JSON entry of the import cache, `/sp-imported-index.json` (`assetCache.js` IMPORTED_INDEX_URL,
   `addImportedPaths`), as it ends — finished, cancelled or failed, before the end is published; `listImportedPaths`
   reads it and falls back to the keys for a cache without one. The worker never answers that path (no hash segment).
2. The worker matched with `ignoreSearch: true`, for which Chrome scans every entry: about 4 s per request at 43,000
   entries (154 ms at 2,000), against about 1 ms for an exact key. Now it matches `origin + pathname` (an import stores
   no query; a request with one still finds its entry).
3. The dialog counted the cache (`countImportedFiles`, the whole key list) on every published progress step, ten times
   a second: quadratic — the import fell to about ten files a second after 1,500. Now it counts as the dialog opens and
   as an import ends.
4. A picked or dropped zip was read whole into one ArrayBuffer (711 MB for this pack). `openZip` now reads a Blob in
   slices — the tail for the end record, the central directory, then each entry's header and data, a deflated entry
   streaming from its slice through `DecompressionStream`; bytes already in memory are read in place as before.
With those: 30,936 accepted, 0 rejected, 1 skipped (the README), 0 unreadable, in 91 s on an idle machine (142 s beside a
lint run, longer on a busier one); the server saw the three manifests, two health probes and no art request; 下载完整素材
afterwards counted 30,936 of 30,936 ready and requested no art; imported URLs answered from the worker (40 in 101 ms).
Opening the import dialog sent no request. When `public/assets/local/fx/index.json` was extracted again between building
a pack and importing it, that one file came out 校验不符 and 下载完整素材 fetched exactly it: a pack has to be built from
the art the release serves (DEPLOY §7 says so).

[ASSUMED]: the hint lines' wording; the import button keeps one label (the download's follows its state); closing the
import dialog still cancels a running import; the README's text and its BOM; the pack follows the full zip's switches
(all on for 0.3.0) and leaves held-back voices out; the index is written once per import, so a tab closed during an
import leaves that import's entries out of it (the download queue then fetches them again; the worker still answers
them). Open: the full zip still carries the 85 unlisted local files (`artPlan`).

Tests: `test/package.test.js` (+6: `importableArt` on sample manifests — files, Spine models with and without textures,
local groups, effects; a query URL and local art outside `groups` left out; the plan on a temporary checkout — the
effects, the default page, leftovers and an unlisted font out, the effects switch, missing art, an account name in art,
an uncommitted manifest; `--assets --dry-run --list` and the flag conflicts; the README; a build zipping exactly the plan
and the README in one folder, `--force` and the out-dir guard, then the real importer accepting every file of that zip
against the checkout's served manifests with the README as its one skip; a development version needs `--allow-dev`),
`test/ui/asset-import.test.js` (+3: a pack too large for `keys()` keeps its count and the download queue's skips through
the index; the index merges, seeds itself from the keys and never lists itself; a zip File read in slices, never whole,
and imported; the worker test: exact keys, a query still hits, no `ignoreSearch`), `test/ui/asset-import.browser.test.js`
(opening the dialog sends no art or manifest request, keeps the download idle and holds the focus; a pack-layout zip
through the dialog's zip picker — 1 imported, 1 skipped; the download dialog has no import controls; plain http says why
on the button and in the dialog), `test/ui/asset-download.browser.test.js` (both buttons in view at 1280×900 and
844×390, uncovered, on one row, the hints above the footer); `node tools/i18n.mjs check --all --strict` (5 strings added,
3 the split left unused removed). Golden: unchanged (client and tools only).

### 32.14.40 Effects on walking units and projectile bodies at the hit (the 0.3.0 effects audit's R1 and R5, round 46)

Seen (the audit of every operator, summon and a sample of enemies, rendered in the real field view): loops on units that
move stay where they started — 锏's AtkUp aura 1–3 tiles behind her, 鼠王's shield egg and 重弩突袭者's 隐匿 at the spawn
gate, 21 of the 25 generic status loops (burn, slow, sluggish, silence, fear, weaken, fragile, the def / res / aspd
downs, shield, invincible, stealth, freeze, sleep, poison, disarm …) drifting off walking enemies —; and the flying body
of a projectile stays on its target for 0.6–0.8 s after the hit (崖心's hook, 灰毫's shell; 60 roster projectiles have the
same make).

(1) Loops on walking units (R1). Client: ParticleEffect's `_holdByOwner` is 1 on 2,193 of the set's ParticleEffects —
the attack starts, the buffs and the status loops (`buff_burn`, `buff_slowdown`, `common_shield_01`, 锏's
`enemy_blkswb_buff_01`, 鼠王's `mousek_buff_0n_start`, `common_invisible` …), 1,540 of them without a FollowMountPoint —
and 0 on 1,938: the hits and the ground ranges (`mousek_skill_01_range`, 锏's S3 crater). The client's metadata names it
beside `_SetOwner` / `_InitLocationFromEntity`, with `Entity.m_holdEffects` and `CreateEffectHoldBySource(…, setAsParent)`;
the code was not decompiled. The runtime moved an effect only by a FollowMountPoint (or, for a held loop, a FollowOwner
to the feet): `wiring.js _placing` gave every other held loop no point to follow, so `playOn` installed no track and the
loop stayed at its first placement. A prefab's chained effects (OnPlayEmitter: `system.js FxHandle._start` hands them
their parent's track) inherited that: placed where their parent started and never moved, with or without a
FollowMountPoint of their own — 51 chained effects have one on another point than their parent's (能天使 S2's
`angle_skill_02_buff_02`, 纯烬艾雅法拉 S3's five weapon effects, 拉普兰德 S2, 泥岩 S3, 扎罗's `enemy_wlfmster_*_buff_01` …). Now
(`render/vfx/wiring.js` `playOn` / `_placing`): an effect its unit holds — a held one (`follow`: a status loop, an
aura, a skill's or a talent's loop, a 驭械术士's) or a `_holdByOwner` prefab — without a FollowMountPoint moves with the
unit, its offset from the feet as it started (a ground spawn stays on the ground); a hit and a range not held stay where
they started; a FollowOwner one keeps to the feet only when held, as before. A prefab with chained effects always gets a
track; a chained effect keeps to its own FollowMountPoint's point, else moves with the unit when its parent or its own
`_holdByOwner` says so. The audit's probes (a stream of 锏, 鼠王 or 重弩突袭者 against 角峰, every 2 s; 波登可 S2's statuses):
loop samples more than 0.6 tiles from every unit 8 → 0 (锏's aura), 18 → 0 (鼠王's shield), 3 → 0 (隐匿); a status loop's
offset from its unit drifted up to 0.21 tiles under 波登可's slow and now stays (0.00).

(2) Projectile bodies (R5). Client: 172 ProjectileEffects name their body in `_inactiveOnFinish` (崖心's
`slchan_attack_01_trail` the hook `gonggaoban` and its trail, 灰毫's `ashlok_attack_01_trail` the shell, 空弦's arrow
`gongjian`), 164 of them with a `_delayToFinish` of 0.5 s; none has an `_inactiveOnHit`. The projectile finishes its
main effect as it is reached (灰毫's SimpleProjectile: `_clearMainEffectWhenReached` 1), which hides that list at once
and lets the trail fade through the linger. The runtime's flight ends with `stop({ hit: true })`, and `effect.js stop()`
hid only `_inactiveOnHit`; `_inactiveOnFinish` waited for `_finish`, after the last trail particle — the body sat on
the target through the stop's fade (`max(STOP_FADE 0.6, _delayToFinish + 0.3)` s). Now `stop()` — the game finishing the
effect: a projectile landing, a status or a skill ending — hides `_inactiveOnFinish` at once; the trail keeps fading as
before. Only ProjectileEffects have such a list in the used set, so no loop changes.

[ASSUMED]: `_holdByOwner` means the owner carries the effect (from the names and the data's pattern; the client's code
was not read); a held effect keeps its offset from the unit's feet as the client's child of the unit's root would (it
does not ride a bone, and does not mirror when the unit turns); a status loop whose prefab says 0 (`common_stealth`,
`buff_ fragile_01`, `common_invincible_01`) and a held skill range follow the unit all the same (a status left at the
gate is what the official game never shows). Open: a chained effect still takes its parent's direction and pause rows
and its parent's spawn point when it has no FollowMountPoint (the client may place it by its own `_spawnLocation`,
OnPlayEmitter `_useSourceEffectPos`). Tests: `test/render/vfx-follow.test.js` (new: status loops with `_holdByOwner` 1
and 0 on a walking enemy, with its height; a held one-shot moves, a hit and a range not held stay; a held range on the
ground under a hover; chained effects on their own FollowMountPoint and by their own `_holdByOwner`; a projectile body
hidden at `stop()` while its trail fades), `node --test test/render/vfx*.test.js` 192 of 192, `vfx.browser.test.js` 7
and `vfx-skills.browser.test.js` 21 (RENDER_E2E=1) unchanged. Golden: unchanged (client only).

### 32.14.41 Official hits against the sim's timing: shots, heals, direct attacks, chains, 领主 blows, 蒂比 (round 46 audit R3 R4 R9 R12 R14 R15 R16)

Seen (the round-46 effects audit, `handoff/wip-r46/audit/FINDINGS.md`, every operator rendered with 设置「官方特效」 完整):
the procedural glow and spark burst on the hits of 44 of 57 ranged operators, the official hit 1–3 frames later (R3);
a procedural tracer, orb or white beam over 22 operators' official attacks that have no projectile — 能天使, 协律 and 12
more normal attacks, 深巡 S2, 初雪 S1 / S2, 银灰 S2, 纯烬艾雅法拉 S3 and every 阵法术师 beam (R4); heals flashing twice, the
generic `common_heal_hit_01` first and the healer's own hit 0.13–0.15 s later (R9: 赫默, 哈洛德, 莎草 …); 能天使 S2 / S3,
录武官 and 烛煌 S3 without their own hits (R12); every chain bounce of 惊蛰, 异客 and 莎草 the procedural purple arc (R14);
拉普兰德, 银灰, 断崖 and 仇白 never swinging their Combat at the enemy they block (R15); 蒂比's projectile never flying, her hits
the generic one (R16). Causes and fixes, all in the renderer (`render/vfx/wiring.js`; `render/app.js` routes the
bounces), the sim and the golden unchanged:

* **R3 / R9 — the sim's timing.** The sim moves a shot in its firing tick and lands it about a tick before the drawn
  flight (distance ÷ the sim's speed) ends, and heals at the 'atk'; `damage` / `heal` counted an event as official only
  after the visual arrival, so the procedural spark (or the generic heal hit) came first. Now an official hit on its way
  is registered on its target (`_expect`, from `_fly`; a drawn follow-up shot excluded) and the first HP 'dmg' or 'heal'
  on it in the attack's own frame (a heal, a chain bounce, a blow flown for show) or from `CLAIM_LEAD` 0.2 s before the
  drawn hit until 0.35 s after is that hit's — events of the same frame with it; one entry per target and frame. A hit
  earlier in the flight (a 灼燃 or poison tick: 'dmg' carries no source) stays procedural. Measured (the audit battle,
  `handoff/wip-r46/fix-hits/{before,after}/analysis`): 隐现 0 → 10 of 10 hits official, 空弦 0 → 11 / 11, 拉普兰德 0 → 36 / 36
  (22 s), 断崖 0 → 38 / 38, 仇白 0 → 36 / 36, 伊内丝 0 → 24 / 24, 能天使 S2 0 → 36 / 36 and S3 0 → 50 / 50; 赫默, 哈洛德, 录武官 and
  莎草 play no `common_heal_hit_01` (5, 4, 4, 4 before), their own hits unchanged. The arrival time of a timer projectile
  is now kept on the effects' clock (it was the render time plus the flight compared with the effects' clock).
* **R4 — direct attacks.** `attack` returned false for a ranged attack without a projectile of its own ("the procedural
  shot still flies"), so `app.js` drew `fx.attack` over the official cast and hit. Now such an attack is drawn by its
  official rows when it has any with a loaded key (`_drawable`: the casts of either phase — a wound-up attack played its
  first ones already —, hit rows, hit groups, the skill's cast / range / aim / hit rows); its hit rows wait for the
  damage for the sim's flight plus 0.35 s (it was 0.4 s whatever the range). Without official rows — or when its
  ability names a projectile the index lacks — the procedural shot stays. Measured: 能天使 12, 协律 4, 深巡 S2 12, 初雪 S1 10, 蜜蜡 S1 11, 薄绿 S1 11, 圣聆初雪 S2 11 procedural shots → 0.
* **R12 — a projectile attack's own hit rows.** For a projectile attack only the projectile's `onHit` played; 5 attacks
  of the index have hit rows and a projectile without one (能天使 S2 / S3 `angle_attack_02_hit`, 录武官 `reckpr_attack_hit`,
  烛煌 S3 `blaze2_skill_03_hit` / `_02`, 预备干员-术师, 死芒 S3; the last two not in the roster) and none has both. Now the attack's rows (not its MultiHit
  groups, which still wait for the damage) play with the projectile's hit, a key the `onHit` plays already skipped.
  Measured: `angle_attack_02_hit` 0 → 9 (S2) / 10 (S3), `reckpr_attack_hit` 0 → 4, `blaze2_skill_03_hit` 0 → 21.
* **R14 — chain bounces.** The sim sends each bounce as ['atk', previous target, next target, 'chain' | 'chainHeal'] in
  the tick its shot hits (a heal's at once); `app.js` skipped the wiring for them. Now `wiring.chain` draws a bounce of a
  chain an official shot opened (its projectile has a ChainLightningEffectBehaviour: projectileParts `chain`; the chain
  is kept on each target it reaches): the line from the previous target's HitPoint to the next one's
  (`effect.js linkLines`, kept on both while it shows) and the projectile's `onHit` with the attack's rows on the next,
  when the shot reaches its first target (the client's ChainLightningHitBehaviour → PlayLineEffect), the bounce's
  damage / heal covered meanwhile; any other bounce keeps the procedural arc. Measured: 惊蛰 and 异客 14 of 14 bounces
  official (`leizi_attack_02_line` / `pasngr_attack_line` 14, their hits 5 → 19), 莎草 4 of 4 (`papyrs_line_01`).
* **R15 — 领主 blows.** A 领主's mode holds a ranged Attack and a melee Combat (also 情报官 伊内丝's); the client strikes the
  enemy it blocks with the Combat and shoots the others, but the sim names every attack by the ranged kind ('arrow') and
  `_attackSet` took `combat` only for a melee kind. Now `_combatKind` draws a ranged-kind 'atk' of an operator on an
  enemy as a melee blow when both show UF.BLOCKED, the enemy stands within the sim's block radius of its centre (0.75 /
  0.95 tiles ground / air) and the unit's mode has both — in the wind-up (`castStart`) and the strike alike. Measured
  (22 s, the enemy blocked from 10.5 s): 拉普兰德 10 Combat swings (`whitew_attack_01_start` 0 → 10, its hit 0 → 20), 断崖 0 →
  11, 银灰 0 → 10, 仇白 0 → 11, 伊内丝 0 → 6.
* **R16 — 蒂比.** Her abilities are RangedAttack / MultiRangedAttack with `projectile_chr_tippi` (`_s1`, `_s2`); the sim
  makes her (天空行者) melee, so the wiring took the melee path and played the generic hit. Now an attack the sim makes
  melee whose official ability is a ranged one with a projectile — of a unit of the player's side — flies that
  projectile for show at its own `movement.speed` (10 tiles/s; `_displayTime`), its damage covered until the drawn hit.
  Measured: `tippi_attack_01_trail` / `_hit` 0 → 7 and `common_hit_01` 7 → 0; S1 `tippi_skill_01_attack_01_trail` /
  `_hit` 0 → 6. 古米 S2's heal projectile at an enemy is no ranged attack (/Heal/) and is not flown.

[ASSUMED]: a 'dmg' within 0.2 s of an official hit's arrival on its target is that hit's (the event names no source);
the snapshot does not say who blocks whom — an enemy within the block radius of a blocking operator is its own; a
display-only flight for a sim-melee attack (the enemies' attacks keep the melee path); a chain's bounces all play when
its shot reaches the first target (`_interval` 0), its line between HitPoints for `_playEffectOnHitPoint` 0 as for 1,
shown 0.2 s (`CHAIN_LINE_S`; the prefabs loop and linger `_delayToFinish` 0.5 s). Open: the splash targets of a projectile
attack (烛煌 S3's circle) keep the procedural spark (their 'dmg' names no shot); 拉普兰德's and 断崖's Spine clip still
follows the sim's kind. Tests: `test/render/vfx-hits.test.js` (new: R3 a shot's damage a tick before it lands covered,
one long before not, a later frame's not; R9 a heal at the attack covered and no generic heal hit; R4 rows draw no
procedural shot, none keep it; R12 a projectile's and a timer's hit rows; R14 bounces drawn at the first hit, the line
between the targets and stopped, another chain procedural; R15 the Combat blow on the blocked enemy, the shot at others
and while not blocking; R16 the flight for show, its damage covered, an enemy's melee path kept). Golden: unchanged
(client only).

### 32.14.42 Buff-state effects of the official-effects audit: shield layers, 卡涅利安's sand armour, 泡泡's counter (round 46: R2, R6, R17)

Seen (the round-46 audit, `handoff` FINDINGS R2 / R6 / R17): every variant of a stacked shield played at once for the
whole battle — 泥岩 (char_311_mudrok) a grey dome in every frame, at 0 layers too; 鼠王 (enemy_1509_mousek) an opaque
golden egg; the 泥岩 boss (enemy_1511_mdrock) three dark crescents —, and so did 塑心 S3's range over her talent's aura and
调香师 S2's over hers; 卡涅利安 (char_426_billro) showed no official effect in her 17–25 s skills; 泡泡 (char_381_bubble) S2's
counter drew the procedural flash, never `bubble_skill_02_hit`.

Official (the local client: config/buff_template_holder.ab and the prefabs, read 2026-10-15). (1) **PauseAnotherEffect**
(`_effect`, `_preDelay`, `_checkUpdate`; 21 prefabs): while an effect plays it pauses its owner's effects of that key.
泥岩's talent (`mudrok_t_1`) gives a buff per 护盾 layer — `[init]` at the start and `[recharge]` at each trigger create
`[shield_c]` with `mudrok_buff_03_start` (the first layer; `_01_start` when `max_times` is 1), `[shield_b]` with `_02`
(the middle ones), `[shield_a]` with `_01` (the last, which ends the recharge) and play `mudrok_buff_01_begin`; a blocked
hit (event 10: BlockDamage, HealViaMaxHpRatio, `common_heal_hit_01`) finishes `[shield_a]`, else `[shield_b]`, else
`[shield_c]` with `mudrok_buff_01_end`. `_01` pauses `_02` and `_03`, `_02` pauses `_03`: 1 / 2 / 3 layers show `_03` /
`_02` / `_01`. 鼠王's `mousek_shield[a]`–`[c]` and the boss's `enemy_mdrock_s_2[shield_a]`–`[c]` (each BlockDamage until
its blackboard is zero; `[c]` of 鼠王 switches to NoShield) show their `_01` / `_02` / `_03` the same way as the parts
break. `cello_skill_03_buff_range` pauses `cello_buff_01`, `flower_skill_02_range` pauses `flower_buff_01_range`. (2)
`billro_s_1/2/3[eff_cond]` (ON_BUFF_TRIGGER every 0.1 s) keep the sand armour of the state: the skill on
(CheckCharSkillAffecting) with `billro_talent_enhance_mark` → full + half, on → half (S3: its two halves), off with ≥ 2
charges (CompareCharSkillAvailableCnt) → full + half, ≥ 1 → the idle half `_buff_01`, else none (FinishDerivedBuff).
index.json lists the keys of these buffs without the branches, so skills.js left them unmapped ('conditional'). (3)
`bubble_s_2` at ON_TAKE_DAMAGE deals DamageViaAttr to MODIFIER_SOURCE and CreateEffect there with the blackboard's
`effect` (`bubble_skill_02_hit`, HIT spawn, FLIP_TWO_SIDE) — on the attacker; the planner put it in `each.hit`, which
needs an attack of hers, and she makes none in S2.

Cause: PauseAnotherEffect was on the not-run list (§32.14.25), and the per-layer loops of 泥岩's `[init]` start were bound
as permanent passive loops; the sim's barrier enemies already ended their talent loops at 'shieldBreak'; the client
had no layer count or stored charges; the `[eff_cond]` graphs and 泡泡's counter had no binding.

Now: `effect.js` keeps a descriptor's PauseAnotherEffect keys (`def.pauses`), FxWiring.playOn gives every effect its unit
(`owner`), and `system.js _pauseOthers` hides, each frame, an owner's effects of a key a playing effect names (after its
`_preDelay`) until that effect stops. TalentFx treats a binding's loops created by CreateBuff(s) at a buff event whose
prefabs pause one another as a layer stack (bottom: pausing none): an ally plays the lowest n for the n hit-negating
layers of the new display-only snapshot list `layers` [[id, n]] (sim `snapshot.js layerView`: the sum of its buffs'
`shieldHits`), and a layer gained plays the binding's start one-shots again (`mudrok_buff_01_begin`); an enemy keeps the
stack while its barrier holds. `skills.js COND_BUFFS` transcribes the three `[eff_cond]` graphs; their buffs go to the
plan's `cond` list, and SkillFx plays the state's loops each frame (`condState`): the skill on — charged when its start
fx says `charged` (卡涅利安's kit adds it to 'shell', 'sandChains*' and 'devour'; display only) —, else the stored
charges of the new display-only list `charges` [[id, stored, maximum]] (`chargeView`, an ally skill with maxCharges > 1).
FX_BINDINGS gains `skchr_bubble_2` 'counter' → `bubble_skill_02_hit` once on the attacker (`keys`, `at: 'target'`; the
kit's fx carries `target`); the procedural flash is skipped. Both lists ride beside the tuples like `wolves` (interp.js
keeps them per snapshot, `sample().layers` / `.charges`, UnitView `layers` / `charges`); the golden corpus is unchanged
(its digest hashes the tuples, counts the events and fx kinds, and does not read these lists or the fx payloads).

[ASSUMED]: a paused effect is hidden, not frozen, and `_checkUpdate` 0 is treated like 1; 鼠王's and the boss's barrier is
one amount in the sim (`shield.dynamic` 4667 / 5500) where the client has three parts of it, so their top variant shows
until 'shieldBreak' instead of stepping down part by part (whether the sim's barrier should be three times as large is
open for the sim); the enhance mark is a cast with every charge stored (the sim's `billroCharged`). The other
PauseAnotherEffect pairs change with it: 帕拉斯 S3's `pallas_skill_03_buff_03` hides her talent's loop, 琳琅诗怀雅's trait
shows `swire2_buff_01_start_02` over `_start` (both still play from the deploy: the trait's condition has no signal),
enemy_2001_duckmi's and enemy_2002_bearmi's death effects hide their buff loops, 薄荷 S2's `mint_skill_02_range_02` hides
`_range` after 0.2 s. Tests:
`test/render/vfx-buff-layers.test.js` (new: the descriptor's pauses; the pause pass by owner, pre-delay and stop; a paused
effect draws nothing; 泥岩's stack for 0–3 layers and the begin on a gain; an enemy barrier's stack until it breaks; the
`[eff_cond]` plan and states, the loops for 1 / 2 charges, a charged and a plain cast, death; 泡泡's counter on the
attacker; `layerView` / `chargeView`; interp's lists). Golden: unchanged.

### 32.14.43 Skill plans: one facing of 仇白's sword wind, 缄默德克萨斯's appear strike once, the hits of skills whose kit deals the damage (round 46 audit R7, R8, R13)

Three findings of round 46's effects audit (`handoff/wip-r46/audit/FINDINGS.md`) were in the skill plans
(`render/vfx/skills.js`) and the code that plays them (`render/vfx/skillfx.js`). DESIGN §33.16 states the new rules.

**R7: 仇白 S2 played all four facings of her sword wind.** The skill's buff template `qiubai_s_2[sword_wind_effect]`
creates one buff at its start, chosen by CheckCharacterDefaultDirection branches (client 26-09-13,
`config/buff_template_holder.ab`): UP `qiubai_skill_02_start_01_up`, DOWN `_down`, RIGHT the plain
`qiubai_skill_02_start_01`, else `_left`. index.json writes each branch's CreateBuff as a separate use without the
condition, so the plan looped all four for the whole skill. `skills.js dirFamilies` now merges the uses of one template
action whose keys are facing variants of one effect by name (`_up` / `_down` / `_left` / `_right`, `_back` for UP,
`_front`, the plain key). The result is one entry with `slots` and directionType 5 (the deploy direction), which
`SkillFx._key` and `TalentFx._key` already pick by facing. The rule was checked against the client and the index:
- The client's buff templates have 28 with direction branches (CheckCharacterDefaultDirection, CheckFaceDirection …).
- Index v3 holds 4 same-template families by suffix, and each is one of those branches:
  - 仇白 S2;
  - 号角 S2 / S3's overload switch (UP `_back`, else `_front`). It acts at ON_BUFF_TRIGGER, so it stays unmapped as
    'conditional', now as one family;
  - 鸿雪's token buff (`_up` / `_down` / plain);
  - the `nasti_nstchr` token's buff.

No family is a pair of effects that play together.

**R8: 缄默德克萨斯 S2 replayed her appear dome on every attack.** S2 and S3 are passive MultiAppearSkills. Their
AbilitySequenceGroup runs the appear strike once: S2's `Damage` MeleeAttack (`texas2_birth_02_start` / `_02`), S3's
`AppearSwordRain`. The plan treated that strike as the skill's attack while it is on, with three results:
- every normal attack (the sim's melee 'atk') played the dome;
- the strike's ability, which has no hit rows, replaced the S2 mode's rows;
- the hits fell back to the generic `common_magic_hit_01`.

Two new tags (`when` on `plan.attacks`, `tagCasts`; our record must give the kind):
- the attacks of a lasting or passive skill whose root ability is a sequence get `'start'`. They play once at the
  skill's start; a passive's start is its deploy;
- a final attack (`FinalAttack`, `EndAttack`) gets `'end'`.

Tagged attacks never stand for a normal attack: the mode's rows play instead (S2: `texas2_skill_02_start` / `_02`,
`texas2_skill_02_hit`). The same mistake affected:
- 缄默德克萨斯 S3;
- 仇白 S2: her sword split on her blows;
- 余 S2: the teleport strike;
- 焰影苇草 S2: her fire ball instead of her heal shot;
- 歌蕾蒂娅 S3: the final pull's rows and projectile on every shot.

In the roster, 9 skills get a tag: 6 `'start'` (缄默德克萨斯 S2 / S3, 仇白 S2, 余 S2, 焰影苇草 S2, 歌蕾蒂娅 S3's chant
attacks) and 4 `'end'` (灵知 S3, 薄荷 S2, 歌蕾蒂娅 S3, 锏 S3); 歌蕾蒂娅 S3 has both.

**R13: skills whose kit deals the damage showed no hits.** Two kinds of skill were affected:
- Instant casts with no attack between the skill's 1 and 0. The sim sends ['skill', id, 1], the kit's damage or heals,
  then ['skill', id, 0] (the audit's logs, `audit/raw/ops/*/s*.json`). Damage: 圣聆初雪 S1, 凛御银灰 S2, 焰尾 S2,
  忍冬 S2. Heals: 流明 S2, 纯烬艾雅法拉 S2. The old fallback played the first attack's cast and range and nothing on
  the targets.
- 灵知 S3's end and 锏 S3, covered below.

'dmg' carries no source (`server/sim/damage.js`: ['dmg', id, amount, type]), so the only link that needs no protocol
change is the order of events within a frame:
- SkillFx opens a kit-hit window for the rest of the frame at an instant activation (closed at its 0), at a `'start'`
  cast and at a kit fx bound to a plan attack;
- `FxWiring.damage` / `heal` report each HP 'dmg' and 'heal' through `SkillFx.seen`. An event that no attack owns (no
  waiting blow, no melee attacker, no floating unit's hit) is a hit of the window's attack, on the owner's enemies; for
  a heal ability it is a heal on the owner's side;
- hit k of a target plays the attack's hit group k. The target's first hit also plays the aims and starts the attack's
  buffs. These hits replace the procedural spark and the generic heal.

The other fixes:
- **Final attacks.** Their hits are the damage dealt in the frame of the skill's 0, before the 0 event, because the
  kit deals it first. 灵知 S3's 400 % on the frozen enemies now plays `gnosis_skill_03_end` and `_end_hit`.
- **An attack of another kind in an instant activation.** 阿罗玛 S1 and 夕 S1 send an 'atk', but as a 'beam' / 'bolt'
  while the skill's ability is a MeleeAttack / MultiMeleeAttack, so the kind check dropped the skill's rows. In an
  instant activation's frame the attack is now the skill's whatever its kind; a heal stays a heal. Its cast plays at
  the strike when the wind-up already played the mode's. 夕 S1 now aims `dusk_skill_01_hit` / `_02` at her target.
- **华法琳 S1.** It is a ReplacementSkill, and its replaced attack comes right after the 0. An attack in that frame now
  carries the skill (`bldsk_skill_01_start`, `_hit` on the heal), and the fallback waits for the frame's end.
- **锏 S3.** The sim runs it for 3 s, with a kit 'slash' every 0.3 s and a finale. `FX_BINDINGS` binds the slash to her
  Damage ability (`blkkgt_skill_03_start_01` / `_02`). The finale is her EndAttack: `blkkgt_skill_03_end` and
  `_start_03` / `_04` on her, `_hit_2` on the enemies it hits.

Evidence: the audit harness rendered the base and the fix for 仇白 S2, 缄默德克萨斯 S2 / S3, 锏 S3, 圣聆初雪 S1, 灵知 S3, 夕 S1, 流明 S2 and
阿罗玛 S1 (local handoff notes, `handoff/wip-r46/fix-plans/`: contact sheets before / after and the official spawns per
job). Spawn counts, base → fix:
- 仇白 S2: the four sword winds 1 each → only `qiubai_skill_02_start_01` (RIGHT); the sword split's hit 0 → 1;
- 缄默德克萨斯 S2: the dome 5 → 1 (at the deploy); `common_magic_hit_01` 10 → 0; the S2 mode's casts and hits 0 → 5 each;
- 锏 S3: her six rows 0 → slashes 11 (`_start_01` / `_02`), finale 1 (`_end`, `_start_03` / `_04`), `_hit_2` 2;
  procedural calls 15 → 4;
- 圣聆初雪 S1 `sbell2_skill_01_hit` 0 → 2; 灵知 S3 `gnosis_skill_03_end` 0 → 1, `_end_hit` 0 → 5; 夕 S1's aims 0 → 1;
  流明 S2 `lumen_skill_02_hit` 0 → 1 (the generic heal hit 3 → 2); 阿罗玛 S1 `aroma_skill_01_hit_01` 0 → 3.

[ASSUMED]:
- the facing variants are told apart by their names: index.json does not carry the condition, though the client's
  templates confirm every family in index v3;
- final attacks are found by their node names;
- a sequence root's attacks are cast once at the start: the skill descriptions say 部署后立即 / 立即 and 技能结束时;
- a kit hits in the frame of its activation. A kit that hits a frame later gets no official hit, for example 夕 S1's
  area 0.1 s on. Unowned damage from another unit in that frame would be taken for the skill's.

Open:
- 锏 S3's Pull projectile (`projectile_chr_blkkgt_s3`): its 'displace' fx names the enemy, not her;
- 缄默德克萨斯 S3's sword rain (EmitSword: `projectile_chr_texas2_s3_sword_rain`, the kit's 'swordRain' fx);
- 仇白 S2's end damage, which has no ability of its own in the index;
- the procedural kit fx (R11: `aoe`, `frostNova` …) are still drawn beside these hits.

Tests: `test/render/vfx-skill-casts.test.js` (new) covers:
- `dirFamilies`, and one sword wind per deploy direction;
- the appear strike once, with the mode's casts and hits on the attacks while the skill is on;
- an instant CastSkill's hits on the enemies its kit damaged, skipping a claimed hit, its own side's units and a later
  frame;
- a kit heal, and a normal heal after the 0;
- a final attack at the 0;
- a 'beam' that is the skill's MeleeAttack;
- a ReplacementSkill's attack after its 0, and its fallback at the frame's end;
- 锏 S3's bound slash and its finale.

Golden: unchanged (client only).

### 32.14.44 Procedural skill bursts and kit fx under the official effects (the round-46 audit's R10 / R11)

Seen (the official-effects audit of every operator, handoff `wip-r46/audit/FINDINGS.md`): with 设置「官方特效」 on, the field
view still drew its own skill looks over the client's. R10 — the golden light pillar and hexagon at the cast of 赫默 S2,
巫恋 S2, 古米 S1, 银灰 S1, 塞雷娅 S1, 瑰盐 S1, 流星 S1 / S2, 华法琳 S2, 塑心 S3 and 魔王 S2 (and the gold aura through 塑心
S3 and 魔王 S2): their official plans have no start, loop or range row and no attack cast row, so `SkillFx.skill`
returned false and `render/app.js` called `fx.skill`. R11 — the kit fx archetypes of `render/fx/kinds.js` (rings,
pillars, beams, arrows) on top of the official skill effects of 41 operators and enemies in 133 skill windows, because
`FX_BINDINGS` bound only 克莱门莎 S3, who is not in the roster.

Official (index.json, each case): a skill without cast rows shows nothing at its cast. The kit's areas are the skill
projectiles that stay where they land (AuraHitBehaviour) with their reached effects for the area's life — 锡人 S1 / S2
(`tinman_skill_0N_range_01` ring, `_token_01` flask, `_start_01` / `_02`), 引星棘刺 S1 / S3 (`thorn2_token_start`, the
unit `thorn2_skill_0N_token`, S1's `thorn2_skill_01_range`), 波登可 S2 (`podego_skill_02_range`), 歌蕾蒂娅 S3's tornado
(`glady_skill_03_range` / `_02`), 异客 S3's storm (`pasngr_skill_03_range`, its strikes the projectile's
`_specialHitEffect` `pasngr_skill_03_hit`); 溯光星源 S2's links are `projectile_chr_halo2_s2_link` (hit
`halo2_skill_02_hit_02`), 焰影苇草 S2's carrier strikes `projectile_chr_reed2_fire` (hit `reed2_skill_02_hit`), 蕾缪安 S3's
locks the aim projectile `projectile_chr_lemuen_s3_aim` (`lemuen_skill_03_crosshair_range` / `_02`) and her bombard
`projectile_chr_lemuen_s3_attack` (reached `lemuen_skill_03_hit_01`). Other kit looks have no official counterpart at
that moment — the client shows the skill's own effects there: 莫斯提马 S2's zone (her range rows), 白面鸮 S2's heal field
(her loop only), 流明 S1's (the skill's buff on the patient, already played with the heal), 凛御银灰 S2's blast, 远牙
S2's and 溯光星源 S3's links, the volleys of 空弦 S1 / S2, 流星 S2 and 雪猎 S2, 信仰搅拌机 S3's counters (attacks of hers),
歌蕾蒂娅 S3's tornado pulls, the summon pillars of 伺夜 S1 and 迷迭香 S3. A summon's arrival is its birth effect
(`FxWiring.deploy`: its own, else the generic `common_char_birth_01`) — no pillar beside it.

Now (`render/vfx/skillfx.js`, `render/vfx/skills.js`; DESIGN §33.16 "The procedural looks the official layer stands
for"): `SkillFx.skill` returns true for a plan without cast rows when the unit's effects are installed (any prefab of
its plan, its skill's `effects`, its modes' rows, its `refs`, its `startEffect`) — no procedural burst or aura.
`FX_BINDINGS` gains `keys` (prefabs no projectile record lists as reached), `hold: 'fx'` (the fx's own `duration` /
`dur` / `hold`), `during` (only while the skill lasts) and owner `'skill'` (an fx naming no owner: the unit whose skill
of that id lasts), with the entries above; `FX_COVER` lists, by skill, the kit kinds the skill's official effects stand
for — skipped while the skill lasts (on, its activation frame, 1 s after its end, or a binding of its owner still held)
when the skill's plan has an installed prefab; a 'summon'-archetype fx (not the floating units' kinds) on a unit whose
official birth effect was drawn in that frame is skipped, and so is a token's arrival burst ('radiantSword',
'summonStun', 'summonBurst', 'paperDoll', 'obelisk') when that birth effect is its own. With 官方特效 off, or none of an
entry's prefabs installed (a lite install), every procedural look stays — the floating units' "only when the real
effects are drawable" rule (§32.14.37). The audit harness's runs on this branch (handoff `wip-r46/fix-procedural/`:
`raw/`, `pairs/` before | after): every R10 burst gone (the 11 skills' 13 casts in those runs); the procedural kit fx of the bound and covered cases
gone, the official areas, strikes, crosshairs and hits drawn in their place.

[ASSUMED]: `COVER_AFTER_S` = 1 s (蕾缪安 S3's last shells land 0.3–0.7 s after the skill's end, 溯光星源 S2's links 0.1 s);
a lock without its own hold keeps the crosshair 1.5 s; the area bindings' fallback times when the fx carries none.
Open — kept procedural because the client shows a cue that is not placed here, or the fx is not the skill's: 引星棘刺
S2's drifting unit (its zone re-sent every second as it moves), 余 S3's fire wall (built from `yu_skill3_range_01_*`
segments), 新约能天使's air strikes (`angel2_skill_N_loop_*` on projectiles the kit does not fly), 蕾缪安 S3's falling
shells, 扎罗's fear-cage beams (the client's `enemy_wlfmster_a_skill_end_buff` on the caged operators is not placed), 缇缇
S3's wake (the fx names no owner), the talents' cues (刺玫's counter, 雪猎's strike, 风丸's 替身 burst) and 泡泡 S2's counter
(audit R17); 古米 S1's heal projectile (the sim makes 古米 melee) and the element bursts of 塑心, 余 and 菲莱 are not
this item's. Tests: `test/render/vfx-procedural.test.js` (new: no burst for a plan without cast rows — with the unit's
effects installed, by its `refs`, not in a lite install or with the layer off; FX_COVER during the skill and 1 s after,
not later, not without the skill's effects; 锡人 S1's zone as the flask's reached effects at its tile for the zone's
time, not outside the skill, the procedural zone without the prefabs; 焰影苇草 S2's carrier strike by owner 'skill';
the summon pillar and the arrival bursts against the birth effect, not in a later frame, not without it). Golden:
unchanged (client only).


### 可选罗素专项 AI 与自适应演习

[ASSUMED] 默认保留原版 AI；SP_BOT_IOLETA_POLICY=experimental 或 Match 的 botIoletaPolicy 启用罗素采购与 adaptive 站位。自动选策略优先可用且未占用的罗素，排除歌利亚和杜宾，真人选择不变。装备让位于搜牌仅在实际刷新预算与资金足够时生效。示教仅覆盖普通 R1–13 已知地图，首批三个候选，完整零漏即停止扩展，否则总预算为单候选 tick 上限的 12 倍，在候选之间检查；服务端及浏览器委派使用同一限制。来源、假设、历史证据和配置见 [罗素 AI 说明](../research/ioleta-adaptive-default.md)。未修改官方机制或 golden 场景。
